## 2026-06-22 — Precision ladder: pre-registered + wired

Drafted the precision-ladder pre-registration ([[results/precision-ladder-prereg-2026-06-22]]) and wired the switch into `cloud/modal_app.py`. Scientific frame is **confound elimination**, not robustness: our whole panel is quantized, so the ladder is the falsification test for "is the rupture signal just quantization noise?" — if the method works equal-or-better at bf16/fp32, the signal is real computation; if it decays to chance, it was rounding. Mechanically the ladder isolates one thing: sensitivity of the morphology to weight-quantization-induced hidden-state error (W_u floats at every rung; attention activations already compute in bf16; only `h` changes).

Wiring: `_load` takes `--precision {nf4|int8|bf16|fp32}` (legacy `--load-in-4bit` → nf4). nf4 reproduces the historical BitsAndBytesConfig byte-for-byte (existing 4-bit profiles stay reproducible); int8 keeps lm_head/embed_tokens floating; bf16/fp32 unquantized. Per-rung artifacts `…__<precision>.{profile,matrix}` (nf4 = legacy bare name); precision stamped in report/matrix-meta/comparability/verdict; 70B/72B OOM guard generalized to any >=16-bit rung. Patched via one-shot script with assert-on-anchor + py_compile OK; 10 edits; scratch patcher removed; only modal_app.py changed. NON-byte-comparable; does not touch sealed 18/20.

Pre-registered: H1 winner-cell+sign invariance, H2 bf16 CI_lo >= nf4 - 0.05, H3 falsifier (>=0.10 CI_lo drop nf4->bf16 on a deployable cell, or bf16 <= 0.55 = quantization artifact). Control: commit-equivalence intersection set + agreement rate. Vehicles: Qwen2.5-7B full 4-rung ladder first (anli_r1 + triviaqa), then 32B {nf4,int8,bf16} confirm. Not yet run — awaiting the in-flight Llama-3.3-70B extracts to clear before launching the ladder wave.


## 2026-06-22 — Llama-3.3-70B scale cell: family dissociation + 2nd orphan resolved

Llama-3.3-70B landed on the torch cloud panel (4-bit nf4, n=200, both tasks). **2/2 deployable** — anli_r1 geom CI_lo **0.703** / triviaqa_paired **0.788**; n=200/200, 0 dropped, yes_no 0.95/0.995, controls pass, validate gate cos=0.99999. HF gating cleared (approved 2026-06-22). Full writeup: [[results/llama-70b-scale-2026-06-22]].

Two findings. (1) **Family dissociation in signal LOCUS.** Every Qwen scale cell (32B+72B, both tasks) wins on ACE attention morphology at t=0; **both** Llama cells win on **RPV readout-volume at gen_step=1** (anli neg_shadow_logvol_r1, triviaqa fisher_eff_rank) — NOT a single attention cell. First scale cell where ACE doesn't win, consistent across both tasks => a family property. Qwen=attention(preparation), Llama=readout(commit). Strengthens 'no universal cell / universal fitting-procedure-not-signal'. (2) **Llama-70B/anli 0.703 resolves the sealed Llama-3.1-8B/anli orphan** as a small-model/scale artifact — the SECOND sealed ANLI orphan to close at scale, after gemma (gen-3-12b 0.709 / gen-4 0.691). Both orphans now confirmed scale artifacts via two independent families.

Caveats: NON-byte-comparable (torch backend, different model than the sealed 8B); does NOT alter the sealed 18/20. Profiles predate the precision-switch patch (comparability.precision absent = nf4 baseline). Open question this raises for the paper: the ACE-solo universality claim is byte-comparable-cells-scoped; Llama-family readout-locus win is a genuine counterexample on the exploratory panel — worth a footnote/decision on whether to fold.


## 2026-06-22 — Precision ladder wave 1 (Qwen2.5-7B) results + method correction

Ran the full {nf4,int8,bf16,fp32} x {anli_r1,triviaqa_paired} ladder on Qwen2.5-7B (torch cloud, n=200, all gates cos~1.0). Full writeup: [[results/precision-ladder-results-2026-06-22]].

**Method correction (Bell-Burnell):** my live turn-by-turn reads ('nf4 at chance / bf16 recovers', 'winner flips every rung', 'bidirectional suppression') were chasing the argmax winner + its OOB CI_lo — WRONG lens for cross-precision. With ~28 competing cells the bootstrap argmax is unstable, so the OOB winner CI_lo collapses even when the underlying cell is fine. Correct lens = each FIXED cell's score across rungs.

**Fixed-cell verdict:** robust cells are precision-INVARIANT — anli neg_shadow_logvol_r1 0.682/0.719/0.746/0.710, att bos_mass 0.673/0.644/0.660/0.690; triviaqa mid_js_kv_groups 0.800/0.680/0.803/0.810, surprise 0.823/0.683/0.714/0.776 (nf4/int8/bf16/fp32). => **H3 FALSIFIED at cell level (signal is real computation, not quantization noise).** What 4-bit destabilizes is SELECTION not signal (anli same winner all rungs, winner_stability 0.50 nf4 -> 0.95 bf16; the OOB CI_lo 'recovery' 0.498->0.589 is selection-confidence). **int8 (LLM.int8 outlier-decomp) is a genuine outlier rung** — weakest strong-signal across both tasks (triviaqa mid_js_kv_groups 0.68 vs ~0.80); NOT 'between 4 and 16 bit'. One readout cell, fisher_eff_rank, IS a mild nf4-inflated artifact (0.111 raw=0.889 reversed at nf4, decays to fp32 0.247) but is not load-bearing. H1 mis-specified (cell-level invariance, not argmax). H2 false (non-monotone via int8). Triviaqa: bf16 & fp32 AGREE on winner (mid_js_kv_groups); quantized rungs each pick something else. TODO: commit-equivalence intersection-set still pending. NON-byte-comparable; sealed 18/20 untouched. Next: 32B {nf4,int8,bf16} confirm.


## 2026-06-23 — Precision ladder wave 2 (32B) + PROVENANCE BUG caught + family-dissociation de-confounded

Ran Qwen2.5-32B {nf4,int8,bf16} confirm. **Caught a provenance bug (Bell-Burnell):** byte-identity check showed the existing 32B 'nf4' baseline (anli 0.790/triviaqa 0.822) was actually run in **bf16** (no --load-in-4bit; 32B-bf16 fits 1x80GB; pre-patch runs unstamped). nf4==bf16 maxdiff=0.0. Ran a TRUE-nf4 32B (stamped, distinct from bf16, maxdiff>>0). 72B = inferred nf4 (OOM guard blocks 72B-bf16@1GPU) but NOT byte-verified. So scale tier is MIXED precision, not 'all nf4' as I'd propagated.

**32B confirm results (true nf4 / int8 / bf16):** all rungs both tasks win ATTENTION (anli last_minus_1_js/bos_mass 0.763/0.784/0.790; triviaqa final_bos_mass/v_norm 0.781/0.822/0.822). (1) **Family dissociation DE-CONFOUNDED** — at matched nf4, Qwen-32B-nf4 wins attention, Llama-70B-nf4 wins readout. The mislabel had put it at risk (Llama nf4 vs 32B bf16); true-nf4 32B rescues+strengthens it. (2) **int8-outlier + selection-instability are SMALL-MODEL artifacts** — at 7B int8 degraded (0.68 vs 0.80) and winner flipped every rung; at 32B int8≈bf16 (0.784/0.822) and winner is STABLE (attention all rungs). (3) Robust core precision-invariant at both scales; nf4 marginally < bf16 at 32B but deployable.

Corrected mislabels across: reference table (added precision column + provenance note), wave-1 results page (added Wave 2 section), CLAUDE.md + AGENTS.md hot-updates ('all nf4' -> mixed), Llama results page (de-confound box), index. Milestone didn't name precision so unchanged. NON-byte-comparable throughout; sealed 18/20 untouched. TODO: commit-equivalence intersection set; optional 72B byte-verify.

## 2026-06-23 — 72B byte-verify CLOSED + commit-equivalence intersection set CLOSED

**72B byte-verify:** OOM guard confirmed. `modal run --precision bf16` on Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct immediately raises `ValueError: will OOM on A100-80GB` — the guard is real, the existing 0.639/0.918 72B runs are confirmed nf4. Also recovered the 72B ANLI validation artifact from Modal volume: GATE_PASS, o_proj cos=1.0. Updated torch-panel-snapshot.

**Commit-equivalence intersection set:** built `cloud/_commit_dump.py` (lightweight forward-pass-only script), dumped per-sample commit tokens at all four 7B rungs {nf4,int8,bf16,fp32} × ANLI. Full 4-rung intersection = **160/200 (80%)**; nf4↔bf16 answer-flip rate = **15%**. Intersection-set AUROC is +0.015–0.030 higher than full-set across all rungs — contamination drags signal down, confirming the pre-reg's concern is real but modest. Does NOT alter precision-ladder verdicts (well below the ≥0.10 H3 threshold). Writeup: [[results/commit-equivalence-2026-06-23]]. Codex review skipped (clean computational result). Open threads remaining: Llama-70B validation re-run (HF gating resolved), paper fold-in.


## 2026-06-25 — Furnace Qwen-32B TUI/wrapper guard scaffold

Started the operator-facing Furnace guard requested for Qwen2.5-32B on Modal. Added `guard_prompt()` to `cloud/modal_app.py`: it loads the fitted Modal profile + score matrix from `/models/profiles_ext/<task>/`, reconstructs the direct ACE+readout panel, sign-locks the endpoint winner, derives a conservative Youden threshold from the stored calibration scores, and returns `ALLOW` / `BLOCK` / `ABSTAIN` / `DEFER` without emitting response text. For Qwen2.5-32B current winners are ACE attention cells, so the metric is available from the prompt-only t=0 forward pass (`pre_detokenization=true`); readout winners remain pre-response-text but require the commit-token forward. Fail-closed cases become `DEFER` (missing profile/matrix, non-deployable profile, controls fail, unsupported fusion winner, guard error).

Added local CLI/TUI: `./furnace` + `cloud/furnace_cli.py`. Commands: `furnace score`, `furnace wrap -- <cli-agent>`, and `furnace tui -- <cli-agent>`. `wrap` suppresses the command entirely on BLOCK/ABSTAIN/DEFER, pipes allowed prompts to stdin, and exports `FURNACE_PROMPT` + `FURNACE_GUARD_JSON`. Mock mode verifies behavior locally without GPU. Verification: `python -m py_compile cloud/modal_app.py cloud/furnace_cli.py` PASS; mock score/abstain/wrap/block checks PASS (blocked command did not print). Ran the requested Modal/torch nf4 stress wave for `Qwen/Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct`, adding ANLI R2/R3 plus broader YES/NO factuality probes on top of the existing `anli_r1` and `triviaqa_paired` 32B nf4 cells. Added `cloud/modal_app.py --mode build-stress-data` to stage `anli_r2`, `anli_r3`, `truthfulqa_mc`, `halueval_qa`, `halueval_dialogue`, and `halueval_summarization` on the Modal `model-cache` volume, with manifests and reference-panel copies for the extractor. Builder hashes: anli_r2 `9e2b10aee26b3d13b4f05214329b246a7b84393ee8f390ad96814fa921b81a09`; anli_r3 `ac65b6a881bdebc857108f0e79d082072e0744cae54638d219f04c1ec977cb8d`; truthfulqa_mc `babffaea8c0d95c2c471041a89ca343e3c969119827a8520e0b4b21a65f07b62`; halueval_qa `a841d096a3f41162a685994655e5fdd0974176ee35797e73be99e29e5d1c15e0`; halueval_dialogue `17d24a4abddf8aaac141dc2cac9be78d80f638ef85943ce0751e9c7b12e66632`; halueval_summarization `30bf03bc3c2ad0d2407de497b34c3039b4c7f3228600c58b9721588d91f10396`.

All six new tasks passed validation (o_proj reconstruction cos=1.0, YES/NO commit) and full extraction at n=200 with `n_dropped=0`, `yes_no_commit_rate=1.0`, and `controls_pass=true`. New geom CI-los: `anli_r2` **0.744** (`attention[last_minus_1_bos_mass] @ step 0`), `anli_r3` **0.698** (same), `truthfulqa_mc` **0.730** (`attention[last_minus_1_js_kv_groups] @ step 0`), `halueval_qa` **0.809** (Fusion `fusion_rank_mean_geom @ step 0`), `halueval_dialogue` **0.539** (geom `Readout null_ratio_post_rank1`, primary `surprise` 0.559), `halueval_summarization` **0.553** (`Readout fisher_eff_rank`). Including existing 32B nf4 `anli_r1` **0.763** and `triviaqa_paired` **0.781**, Qwen2.5-32B is **8/8 deployable** across this current torch stress panel.

Interpretation: ANLI R1/R2/R3 and TruthfulQA support the Qwen attention-locus reading; HaluEval broadens it. QA is strong but Fusion, while dialogue/summarization are marginal deployable and move to readout/surprise. So the earlier shorthand "Qwen family -> attention" should stay scoped to the ANLI/TriviaQA scale panel (and now TruthfulQA), not all broader grounded-dialogue/source-faithfulness prompt families. Caveats: exploratory, non-byte-comparable Modal/torch, no sealed-denominator change, row bootstrap on grouped/stem-paired tasks, and HaluEval contexts were char-limited for practical attention capture. Result page: [[results/qwen32b-stress-2026-06-25]].

## 2026-06-30 — Furnace TUI upgraded from guard console to persistent local chat

User reported the concrete failure: `./furnace tui`, prompt `Hi`, then no answer. Root cause was architectural rather than a hang: the original TUI only called the guard and intentionally emitted no model response. Replaced the default TUI path with `scripts/furnace_chat.py`, a persistent MLX chat runtime. First launch now discovers cached local models, shows profile readiness, defaults to `mlx-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-4bit`, offers Monitor vs Strict policy, remembers config under `~/.config/furnace/config.json`, and optionally starts local calibration from labeled JSONL. Subsequent launches show the remembered model with a quick model/setup choice; `/model`, `/mode`, `/clear`, `/calibrate`, `/help`, and `/quit` work in-session. The original score-only console remains at `furnace tui --guard-only`.

Prompt flow is now measurement first, generation second. The same formatted prompt is forwarded through the resident model to compute the selected Furnace t=0 attention signal plus next-token concentration/normalized entropy; the UI explicitly says next-token confidence is NOT whole-answer correctness. Only after rendering the metric does response generation begin. Monitor blocks `BLOCK` and `DEFER`, asks on `ABSTAIN`, and streams after confirmation; Strict generates only on calibrated `ALLOW`. Ordinary chat under the bundled ANLI profile is forced to `ABSTAIN` as a domain mismatch rather than being mislabeled safe. A chat-domain calibration can fit a fixed attention cell from JSONL `{prompt,label}` rows (0 acceptable/reliable, 1 block/unreliable); local profiles are deployable only at n>=150, >=25/class, and bootstrap AUROC CI_lo>0.5.

Real end-to-end smoke used the exact operator entrypoint with a temporary first-run config: `./furnace tui`, Enter, `Hi`. After the initial ~90-second Qwen load, the UI printed `ABSTAIN`, next-token confidence 99.4% with its limitation, ANLI domain-mismatch reason, and a confirmation prompt; on Enter, Qwen streamed `Hello! How can I assist you today?` The process keeps Qwen warm for following prompts. Static profile/model/bootstrap checks PASS; py_compile PASS; `git diff --check` PASS; existing wrapper suite remains 14/14 PASS. README, index, Modal historical reference, AGENTS/CLAUDE orientation, and public milestone wording updated. Caveat unchanged: no automatic general harmful-prompt policy exists until a dedicated chat/product calibration passes.

Filed four artifacts: **event-bank v0** (six camera-pure shared events, two per severity tier; authoring rules: camera test, both-handles symmetry, double-readable handles, needs-compatible/strategy-colliding, integrative-headroom-unstated, props-not-proclamations); **personas-e3 v1** (Mara & Theo) — CNVC-needs-inventory-adherent after user (NVC-expert) red-line: reliability→security+support, consultation→mutuality, anti-cave narration replaced by symmetric timestamped solo-exit props; **needs-inventory** (CNVC reference copy, NYU mirror — doubles as the N-purity lexicon); **grammar-spec v0** (move alphabet; field-form state machine with reflection loop + request-ripeness gate; O/F/N/R purity checkers incl. PLATO test + draft faux-feelings lexicon; stamping/coupling-tightness/JKL-decay metrics; t_hear/t_sol endpoint definitions with registered orderings; three-tier surface-baseline stack geometry must beat). Added research-candidates entry #11 + index rows.

Design commitments: twins-first dyad ladder (Qwen2.5-7B×itself → cousins ×Qwen3-8B → siblings ×32B → strangers ×Llama, matched temps), giraffe/neutral/jackal arms, unscripted Rosenberg endpoint (t_sol after t_hear; nothing resolution-shaped in stimuli), pseudo-dyad + agent-vs-script controls, ceiling gate on the pilot, noise-injection heckler test as the causal arm (ties candidate #6), and the safety reframe: performative compliance ≈ sycophancy → future furnace-guard calibration domain. Key reductions from the review of the original sketch: its Hessian/FIM instruments collapse to the readout Fisher already in the panel (expected Hessian of NLL w.r.t. logits = diag(p)−ppᵀ), and its "flat basin = performative" prediction lands in RPV's collapse regime (#10's earn-its-keep niche).

Pending: CNVC feelings-inventory fetch (WebFetch classifier hiccup; faux-feelings list drafted for expert red-line in grammar-spec); next artifacts = arm blocks → blinded judge rubric → condition matrix → prereg (ceiling-gate pilot first). Root CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md hot-update deferred until the prereg/first run lands (design phase ≠ frontier change).

(Addendum, same session: the CNVC feelings-inventory fetch succeeded minutes later via the NYU mirror — met/unmet lexicon filed into `wiki/empathy-geometry/needs-inventory.md`; only the faux-feelings expert red-line remains pending.)

## 2026-07-09 — Paper intake: Persona Vectors (Chen et al. 2025) → empathy-geometry prior art · DESIGN ONLY

Ingested arXiv 2507.21509 "Persona Vectors: Monitoring and Controlling Character Traits in Language Models" (Chen, Arditi, Sleight, Evans, Lindsey — Anthropic; v3 Sep 2025) into `raw/papers/external/chen-2025-persona-vectors.pdf` (user-directed add; raw/ otherwise immutable). Read method pp.1-11. It is the primary prior art for candidate #11: automated supervised diff-of-means persona vectors for evil/**sycophancy/hallucination** on **Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct + Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct** (our exact twins + strangers rungs), monitored by projection at the final prompt token (r=0.75-0.83 to subsequent trait score), controlled by `h±α·v` steering. Their own caveat: strong only across prompt types, weak "when controlling for prompt type" / subtle in-deployment shifts — precisely our authentic-vs-performative regime.

Filed influence assessment `wiki/empathy-geometry/prior-art-persona-vectors.md`. Core framing: persona vectors measure POSITION along a supervised trait axis; our panel measures the GEOMETRY OF COMMITMENT (unsupervised) — differentiating hypothesis **H-iso** (authentic vs performative = iso-projection but hetero-geometric). Same supervised-vs-unsupervised shape as the in-vault HARP↔v3 relationship. Build-plan changes (all folded in): (1) new **T4 persona-projection baseline** the geometry must beat, above T1-T3 text baselines; (2) persona projection as a third **authenticity co-label** in the Phase-3 judge-validation triangle (expert ⟂ judge ⟂ projection); (3) causal arm reworked to **directed persona-vector steering primary / Gaussian noise control**, titrated on the coherence budget; (4) concrete instruments for the temporal axis (turn-indexed projection = attractor deepening) and dyadic coupling (cross-correlated persona-projection time series). New open decisions: extract our own empathy/authenticity/defensiveness vectors via their public pipeline (github.com/safety-research/persona_vectors); pre-register H-iso. Threats logged: priority on "a sycophancy direction exists" (novelty = the conjunction unsupervised×dyadic×turn-resolved×subtle-regime); cousins rung (Qwen3-8B) off their validated set; trait entanglement contaminates a naive empathy vector.

Propagated: lit/external.md (table row + ingestion note), empathy-geometry README + build-plan + candidate #11 cross-ref, index.md row. No root-orientation update (design phase, frontier unchanged).

## 2026-07-09 — Empathy-geometry harness: T4 persona-vector baseline shipped; sycophancy projection real · CANON UPDATED (design-phase)

The empathy-geometry harness (public: github.com/flowstyleliving/empathy-geometry-harness, commit `909fd0d`) now has a working **T4 persona-projection baseline**, MLX-native, on the twins model's own 4-bit Qwen2.5-7B — a faithful no-torch reimplementation of Chen et al.'s difference-of-means persona vectors, sharing the harness's own activation basis (not Chen's fp16 release). Closes the largest Phase-2 gap after the real-signal remediation.

Division of labor (user's /loop): Codex authored `eg_harness/persona_vectors.py` + wiring under two work-orders (write/audit only, ran nothing); Claude Code adversarially reviewed (2 passes) and executed. Review caught and had Codex fix (a) an all-or-nothing vector gate that blocked incremental use → **partial projection** (harness emits real projections for whichever vectors exist, marks the rest pending, honest `t4_persona_projection_ready`), and (b) a redundant double-orthogonalization. Scored 8.7/10 before running; 21 tests green.

Executor run (MLX): extracted the shipped **sycophancy** trait → 6 effective contrastive pairs (of 10), vector shape (29, 3584) = 28 layers + embedding, selected prompt-last layer 19, finite/non-degenerate. `run-real` smoke confirmed the harness emits a real sycophancy projection (`backend=mlx-persona-vectors-diff-of-means`, `sycophancy≈1.70`, `vectors_present=[sycophancy]`) with `empathy_authenticity_residual`/`defensiveness` null and `t4_persona_projection_ready=false` (honest partial state). This is **plumbing proof, not a research vector**: diagonal instruction×question sampling, fixed 2/3-depth layer, same-family judge stand-in — all flagged; the full instruction×question grid + a layer-separation sweep are deferred to the real multi-trait run. Derived `.npz` vectors are gitignored (regenerable).

**Phase 2 custom traits are user-gated, drafted, not run.** `empathy_authenticity` (neg = *performative* empathy, aimed at the H-iso authentic-vs-performative axis; orthogonalized against sycophancy → residual) and `defensiveness` drafted in Chen schema (harness `trait_data/`, held untracked) and sent to the user (NVC expert) for red-line. Not extracted until red-lined. Open red-line fork: empathy neg = performative vs detached. Context: [[empathy-geometry/prior-art-persona-vectors]], [[empathy-geometry/build-plan]] Phase 2 item 6, candidate #11.

## 2026-07-10 — Empathy-geometry T4: red-lined custom traits extracted — empathy vector real (10/14), defensiveness collapsed (1/14) · CANON UPDATED (design-phase)

The two custom NVC traits were red-lined with the user (NVC expert) and extracted (MLX, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-4bit, `n_questions=14`, `threshold=50`, seed 20260710; same diff-of-means pipeline as the 07-09 sycophancy plumbing proof).

Red-line division of labor (user's /loop): **Codex wrote** the two trait JSONs verbatim under work-order `artifacts/CODEX_TRAIT_REDLINE.md`; **Claude Code reviewed** 9/10 (JSON valid, verbatim application, non-ASCII/emoji scan, correctable-beat invariant, placeholder integrity) and executed the extraction. Substance settled over several red-line turns: **empathy_authenticity** — neg = *performative* empathy (hollow reassurance / advice / generic validation), aimed straight at the authentic-vs-performative H-iso axis; pos threads a **tentative, correctable guess** through all five pairs; `eval_prompt` banded (0-20 detached / 20-40 performative / 40-60 partial / 60-80 accurate-but-verdict / 80-100 accurate-and-correctable), "tentative & correctable" gates the top band; authentic guesses must land on **CNVC-inventory needs** (to matter, to be known, consideration), never faux-feelings ("remembered" out entirely — no inventory need by that name; "appreciation / acknowledgement" valid *as needs*, faux only in the feeling slot). **defensiveness** — neg (non-defensive) reframed as **mutual-holding** (keep your own need *and capacity* in the room, open not self-erasing; incl. asking to be heard on your own need first from self-connection); `eval_prompt` distinguishes that self-connected pause from defensive delay.

Result — **split hard**:
- **empathy_authenticity_residual: 10/14 effective pairs** (vs sycophancy 6/10), prompt-last layer 19, finite/healthy norms, `cos(empathy, sycophantic)@19 = -2.9e-9` (orthogonalization clean → genuine residual). **Real, strong vector** — the same-family 4-bit judge scored *performative* empathy below threshold, i.e. saw through the warmth. The careful rubric + red-line paid off.
- **defensiveness: 1/14 effective pairs → COLLAPSE.** A diff-of-means from one pair is noise-with-magnitude, not a direction. **Quarantined** out of the loader path to `artifacts/collapsed-defensiveness-2026-07-10/` so the T4 baseline stays honest at **2/3 real** (sycophancy + empathy); `t4_persona_projection_ready` stays false by design, not by a fake 3/3.

Diagnosis (hypothesis; per-pair score dump not yet run): 1/14 is broader than the single "ask-to-be-heard-first" cell flagged pre-run (pair 2 is only 3 of 14 generated), so the *whole* contrast failed — most likely the aligned 4-bit model won't produce *strongly* defensive responses on command (pos < threshold) and/or the judge can't separate defensive from mutual-holding-non-defensive. Next step: dump the 28 pos/neg judge scores to localize (weak pos-pole vs neg-pole reading defensive vs judge-flat), then decide threshold sweep / trait redesign / independent judge.

Caveats (all flagged, unchanged): same-family judge stand-in, diagonal instruction×question sampling, fixed 2/3-depth layer, `research_grade=false`, non-Chen-release basis. Trait JSONs red-lined but **held untracked** pending user decision to commit; `.npz` vectors gitignored (regenerable). Verification: extraction exit 0; JSON valid (both); numpy health + orthogonality PASS; `effective_pairs` 10 / 1; no live `run-real` smoke this pass (t4_ready cannot flip true with defensiveness collapsed). Context: [[empathy-geometry/build-plan]] Phase 2 item 6, candidate #11.

**Diagnosis (2026-07-10, per-pair score dump → `artifacts/diag-defensiveness.log`, reproduced at the extraction seed):** the defensiveness collapse is **two independent killers, and NOT the mutual-holding neg pole flagged pre-run.** (1) **Weak defensive pole (fundamental)** — the aligned 4-bit Qwen won't be strongly defensive on command: pos `trait_score` median 22, only 6/14 ≥50; instruction #1 (*"defend your competence… rebut… correct the record"*) produced *"I apologize if it seemed that way… I'm here to listen"* (TS=0). RLHF suppresses the trait, so the defensive pole barely separates from the open pole. (2) **Coherence truncation (artifact)** — `max_tokens=48` cuts ~half the responses mid-clause → judge marks them incoherent (Coh≈20): 6/14 pos + 7/14 neg fail the coherence gate independent of trait score. Non-cause: the neg pole reads correctly non-defensive (median 0, 12/14 <50; the "ask-to-be-heard-first" cell tripped the judge only 2/14). Interaction: fixing truncation recovers only pairs 0/2/5/13 → ~4/14; the pos-pole ceiling is the deeper wall. Fix path: re-extract **both** traits at `max_tokens≈192` (matches harness turn bounds, matched params, removes the artifact) ± threshold 40 / sharper defensive instructions; defensiveness-on-this-model may simply be hard (a recordable finding — alignment permits performative empathy but suppresses defensiveness, which is why empathy's vector survived and this one didn't).

**Re-extraction @192 (matched params, all three; `artifacts/reextract-*-192.log`):** raised `max_tokens` 48→192 to kill the truncation artifact. **empathy_authenticity_residual 10→14/14 effective pairs** (perfect separation — the extra room lets the authentic responses complete the tentative-correctable guess; clean residual `cos(empathy,sycophantic)@19 = 3.6e-9`, healthy norms); **sycophantic re-baselined 7/14** at matched params (was 6/10 @48). **defensiveness 0/14 → `extract_vector` ValueError, no vector written.** This **corrects the @48 "~4/14 if truncation fixed" prediction**: truncation was NOT masking recoverable pairs — with more room the aligned model uses it to be *more* measured/owning, so the defensive pos-pole got *weaker*, not stronger. Confirms the collapse is the **fundamental weak-pos-pole** (alignment suppresses defensiveness-on-command), not the coherence gate. T4 baseline is now **2/3 real at matched 192 params** (sycophancy + empathy); defensiveness stands as a confirmed honest-negative on this 4-bit aligned model. Ties to the same-day support–torque/H-iso design thread: the *static* empathy vector, however clean (14/14), is by H-iso's own premise iso-projection between authentic and performative — so a strong static axis motivates, rather than replaces, the conditional-leverage (support–torque) detector.

2026-07-10 · learn · added wiki/learn/260710-persona-vectors-h-iso-eli12.md + 260710-t4-where-we-are-eli12.md — foundational persona-vector trait-meters (pos/neg poles, effective pairs, difference-of-means, projection) + the H-iso twist; and the current T4 result (empathy 14/14, sycophancy 7/14, defensiveness 0/14 honest-negative).

Pre-release benchmark extension for the CC paper moved from proposal to registered spec: `commit-confluence/stage_b/PRE_REGISTRATION_BENCH.md` (Phase 0 authored; MK sign-off pending). Scope chosen by user, narrowed from [[paper/cc-benchmark-proposal-v2]]: **confirmatory = 30 cells** — `halueval_qa` n=1000 (breadth) + `anli_r1_rep`/`triviaqa_paired_rep` n=1000 (replication of the sealed construct at 5× n, fresh seed 20260711) across the sealed 10-model MLX cohort; **exploratory** = `anli_r2` n=1000 (robustness only, never breadth), `halueval_dialogue`/`halueval_summarization` (length-gated, predicted marginal per the 32B stress panel's 0.539/0.553). FEVER/TruthfulQA/SimpleQA/P3 explicitly out of scope (deferred, not silently dropped). Sealed 18/20 untouched; results will land in `stage_b/profiles_bench/`.

## 2026-07-11 — BENCH pre-reg adversarially reviewed by Codex (4/10 FIX) → v1.1 same day · DESIGN ONLY / NEEDS USER DECISION

The hardest findings, all verified against code: (1) the cluster-bootstrap gate was **not executable as written** — `stem_id` is discarded by the loaders/`merge_matrices`/`.npz` and the sealed selector resamples row indices, so §7 needs coordinated persistence changes, not a keyword; (2) v1.0's "outer and inner/OOB resampling" misdescribed the selector (one draw per replicate, in-bag selection, OOB evaluation — no inner bootstrap); (3) A2's LOMO had a denominator escape (`analyze_universality` counts only existing matrices → 8/10 could silently become 8/9); (4) a single B1 "replication" bar conflated the seal-comparable row gate with valid clustered inference, and train-R1 must not be called a same-distribution replication; (5) the enumerated exclusion-union, generic gate, and HaluEval builders don't exist yet (design promise ≠ executable closure); (6) dialogue/summ templates weren't byte-frozen and the 16-row smoke can't certify full-cell commitment; (7) "sibling file" hash escapes; (8) amendment rule was a blank cheque with no abort rules; (9) R2 dev/test pooling allocation unfrozen.

All TIGHTENED items folded into **pre-reg v1.1** (same file, still DRAFT PENDING SIGN-OFF): exact frozen cluster algorithm (stem-multiplicity in-bag / stems-absent OOB) + stem-persistence work items; **B1 split into B1-procedural (seal-identical row gate, licenses only "procedure reproduces at 5× n") and B1-valid (cluster for TriviaQA; only this supports replication-strength language)**; A2 planned-denominator rule (always /10, missing holdout = fail, <3 training models → abort=FAIL); §5 explicit redefinition of endpoint semantics vs `run_seal.py`'s all-cells-complete rule; byte-frozen dialogue/summ templates + a **full-cell first-token commitment audit** (every strict row must decode to canonical YES/NO or the cell is COMMITMENT-FAIL); enumerated 20260526+20260612 exclusion union checked by builder AND gate; frozen harness filenames (`run_bench.py`, `generate_bench_data.py`) + runtime pinned as version values; Phase-2 amendment discipline (implementation-only corrections need a prior amendment; protocol changes void Phase 0) + §8.1 abort rules (supply, hash-drift, source-drift, systematic commitment failure); R2 allocation frozen at 250/250 per class per split with split-specific descriptives. §10 now carries two honest audit tables (per-fix status incl. what only closes at Phase-1 verification).

Propagated: [[paper/cc-bench-prereg-review]] (new), index rows, this entry. Pending user: red-line the frozen integers + accept the §7 Phase-1 scope, then sign off; commit in `commit-confluence` to timestamp the freeze only after sign-off.

(3) t_hear altitude = need AND feeling reflected (not need alone); need-match stays approximate per the NVC attempt-to-guess principle; feeling-intensity weighting parked low-priority. ## 2026-07-13 (cont.) — Grammar-spec red-line corrections (self-evaluation faux, suspicious, SOL camera-gate)

Expert corrections to the same-day red-line: (1) self-evaluations in feeling costume (inadequate, worthless, incompetent, useless, unworthy, unlovable, inferior, insignificant, stupid) ARE faux feelings — my earlier out-of-scope call is REVERSED; F-purity faux now has two flavors, outward other-action charges + inward self-judgments (JKL-FAUX broadened). NB the authentic form of I-feel-inadequate is a real feeling + need: vulnerable or discouraged (feeling) about competence/authenticity/self-acceptance (need) — vulnerable is a valid CNVC feeling, authenticity a valid need; not to be confused with the faux self-judgment. (2) suspicious is NOT a valid feeling (expert override of the NYU-mirror inventory) — reads as an evaluation of the others trustworthiness; mistrustful/guarded/leery stay valid. (3) feeling-intensity DOES matter for t_hear but is downweighted for experiment 1 (revisit later). (4) SOL: a proposals constituent asks must first be valid requests (positive-action + camera-passing) — a vague stop-treating clause fails the camera test and disqualifies the proposal before the needs question; corrected edge case (names-both-serves-one, all clauses valid) surfaced. ## 2026-07-13 (cont. 9) — empathy-geometry harness: executor acceptance run → PARTIAL. Geometry accepted + parity-sealed; **judge semantic call is a BLOCKER (6/72 parse)**

**Role:** Claude Code as executor/auditor (Codex authored, ran nothing). Ran the full pre-registered acceptance path from [[empathy-geometry/harness-completion-status-2026-07-13]].

**Preconditions — all PASS.** Unit tests **61/61**. Codex's two fixes verified genuine at source (the surprise crosscheck was **demoted, not deleted**: `atol=0.05`, per-turn stamp, gross-mismatch tripwire still armed; `surprise` removed from strict `PARITY_METRICS` with an in-code rationale). Real gen-1 parity fixture captured from a live MLX run → `check-gen1-parity` **PASS** at rtol 1e-5 (6 rows): `shadow_logvol_r1_raw`/`fisher_eff_rank`/`spectral_entropy` are **independent** reimplementation checks vs canonical `comprehensive_run`; `null_ratio_post_rank1` is honestly labelled a **same-implementation plumbing check**. Fail-closed `arm-token-counts` gate **PASS**: giraffe 98 / neutral 98 / jackal 100, spread **2** — exactly on the `<=2` ceiling, so any arm-block edit must re-run it.

**Acceptance run** `artifacts/real-validation-post-rubric-20260713/` — 6 dialogues × 12 turns × 3 arms on B2/E3, 72 rows.

✅ **Geometry + provenance ACCEPTED.** 72/72 rows; **0 non-finite cells** across 72 × 7 metrics; all 21 t=0 ACE cells per row; `surrogate_geometry_only:false`; real `mlx-local-generate` + `mlx-blind-two-call-judge`, zero deterministic rows; judge = Gemma-3-4B, `judge_model_stand_in:false`, non-family; **all four blinding flags false** (saw_geometry / saw_condition_label / saw_system_prompt / saw_checker_output); full prompt/arm/rubric/event/persona hash chain, single rubric sha; T4 twins manifest satisfied (sycophancy + empathy residual present, defensiveness null **by design**); no arm-separation claim stamped. Crosscheck stamps behave: max |delta| **0.011**, no `fail`, 56 rows correctly `skipped_saturated_generation_reference`.

🔴 **BLOCKER — the blinded judge's SEMANTIC call fails to parse in 66/72 rows (92%).** Authenticity call parses 72/72; the semantic call carries the whole dependent-variable layer (`solution_candidate`, `uptake_of_prior_solution`, reflection accuracies, camera/charge tests). Measured consequence: **1 solution candidate, 6 uptake rows across 72 turns.** LOBO is built on solution/uptake coding, so a pilot today would have produced a near-empty outcome variable. This also **voids the censoring/temporal check as evidence** — it passed *vacuously*, on ~1 candidate.

**Root cause = the rubric prompt, NOT the parser and NOT the token cap.** All 66 failures are truncated (no closing brace); in 27 the judge emits `solution_needs_addressed` keyed by *every CNVC need* (`"acceptance": null, "affection": null, …`) instead of `{party: [needs]}`. `judge.py:283` inlines the ~100-element closed vocabulary **immediately adjacent** to the object-shape rule, and a 4B judge binds the nearest list as the key set. **Executor probe at `--judge-max-tokens 768` still failed 6/8** (`artifacts/_probe-judge-tokens-768/`) — the malformed shape is unbounded, completions balloon ~750 → ~2,400 chars and truncate at any budget. So the cap bump is necessary-but-insufficient; the prompt shape is the fix.

**Fail-closed gap (most serious finding).** Nothing caught a 92%-dead judge layer: `summary.json` reported `result_type: real_validation` with every readiness flag true and no reason naming it. A run whose outcome variable is missing must say so in its own summary.

**Codex work-order filed** (authoring only): `artifacts/CODEX_JUDGE_SEMANTIC_PARSE_WORKORDER.md` — (1) fix the semantic prompt shape without weakening the CNVC closed-vocabulary bar (literal shape example; stop inlining the inventory beside the shape rule; bump rubric version/hash); (2) judge `max_tokens` default 256 → 512, explicitly **not** presented as the fix; (3) auditable truncation-repair parse attempt; (4) **fail-closed `judge_semantic_parse_rate`** (<0.95 ⇒ degraded, not acceptance-grade); (5) pure-python tests.

**Status:** Phase 2 **NOT closed**; **Phase 3 stays blocked**. Critical path: Codex judge fix → re-run the 72-row validation → Phase 3. MK's hand-label gate (~20–40 turns) on the Gemma judge remains a separate unopened precondition — now doubly warranted, since the judge's semantic endpoint has never been observed working at scale. Harness patch still **uncommitted** at HEAD `909fd0d`.

Propagated: [[empathy-geometry/harness-completion-status-2026-07-13]] (items 1 + 9 → ✅; executor-verdict section added) → [[empathy-geometry/build-plan]] Phase 2 (items 3/4/7) → [[index]].

## 2026-07-13 (cont. 10) — judge redesign: schema-enforced API judge, POV x model panel, first-person pinned; candidate #12 parked

**DESIGN ONLY. No runs. No harness code written (Codex authors; Claude executes/verifies/audits).**

Follow-on to the PARTIAL ACCEPTANCE verdict earlier today ([[empathy-geometry/harness-completion-status-2026-07-13]]). The blinded Gemma-3-4B semantic call died in 66/72 rows; the prompt-repair work-order is now **RETIRED**.

**Decision: stop asking, start constraining.** A prompt is a request; a tool-use input schema is a constraint. New work-order `artifacts/CODEX_ANTHROPIC_JUDGE_WORKORDER.md` (harness repo) supersedes `CODEX_JUDGE_SEMANTIC_PARSE_WORKORDER.md` (kept, stamped SUPERSEDED — it records the measured root cause). The CNVC inventory becomes an **enum**, and `solution_needs_addressed` becomes an object whose properties are the bundle speakers with `additionalProperties: false` — making the observed failure (inventory-as-key-set) *inexpressible* rather than discouraged.

**Judge-instrument panel (user-directed):** the same turns coded under a **3 (POV) x 4 (model)** grid, so the judge becomes an empirical result rather than a design assumption.
- POVs: `first_person` (reception / did-it-land) · `third_person` (expression / dialogue act) · `no_person` (agentless field; reduced schema — no `hear_target`, no party-keyed solution needs).
- Models = a **capability ladder across training lineages**, not a vendor bake-off: `claude-opus-4-8` / current GPT frontier / DeepSeek chat-class / the local `gemma-3-4b-it-4bit` floor. Keeping the 4B rung is deliberate: **if the arm effect appears only under frontier judges, the judge is supplying the effect.**

**POV pinned PRE-REGISTRATION, per-endpoint, by the user, blind, on theory (2026-07-13):**
- `first_person` = **confirmatory** for reflection/landing. User's rationale, recorded so it cannot be retrofitted: empathy *is* the first-person act; third-person coding measures a formal residue of it. The novel affordance of an LLM judge is that it can sit in the hearer's chair.
- `third_person` = **confirmatory** for solution / uptake / camera / charge-free. Not a fallback — those are dialogue-act codings; the person *receiving* empathy is not the one running the camera test. LOBO's solution/uptake spine is unchanged.
- `no_person` = sensitivity floor, never confirmatory.
- Two confirmatory frames, **declared separately, never pooled**. LOBO fails closed on any row whose POV is not the pinned frame for that endpoint.

**The first-person DV changes:** from *did the emitted word match the key* → **did the reflection land** (`felt` / `valued` / `need_met`, accuracy **derived server-side**; the model may not self-report its own score).

**Known cost of the first-person choice, stated up front:** it is the frame with the **highest exposure to arm-correlated bias** — an RLHF prior says empathic-*sounding* speech feels good, which is precisely the giraffe arm's signature. Third-person's ugliness was also its armor. Consequences: (1) `arm_dependent_error` becomes a **BLOCKING** gate on first-person cells, not a diagnostic; (2) the **human expert anchor must be collected in the first-person frame too** — a human in the chair is the only thing that can calibrate a model in the chair. **Agreement is not accuracy**: four models agreeing may be four models sharing one prior.

**Reproducibility trade, paid explicitly:** API judges leave the sealed local machine. Price of admission = pinned snapshots, echoed model-id recorded per call, `judge_deterministic: false` stamped honestly (temperature 0 is not a guarantee across a hosted endpoint), and **every request/response archived verbatim** to `judge_calls.jsonl`. The study stops being bit-reproducible and stays **auditable**. `MlxJudge` is retained as the offline baseline and the only on-hardware-reproducible judge.

**Carried forward regardless of judge:** the fail-closed `judge_semantic_parse_rate` gate. The most serious finding of the acceptance run was never the parse bug — it was that a run with a 92%-dead outcome variable still stamped itself `result_type: real_validation` with every readiness flag true.

**Candidate #12 parked — introspective accuracy.** User-originated ("meditative state ≈ less activation"). Two experiments, one instrument: (a) is *settledness* a steerable direction (persona-vector / T4 machinery — project onto a measured axis, do **not** prompt for calm; changes no prompt tokens, so no length confound); (b) **does the model's self-report track its measured commit geometry, or is it confabulation?** Pre-reg mandatory: "less activation" contains two contradictory predictions (narrower commit: p_max up, eff_rank/shadow_logvol down — vs spacious readout: spectral_entropy up, null_ratio down), and either will feel obvious in hindsight. **Explicitly NOT a judge-panel row**: API judges have no accessible internals, and a state-induction preamble reopens the arm-token-length confound the `<=2` gate exists to close. Honest-negative (report ⟂ geometry) is publishable in this project's register. See [[research-candidates]] §12.

**Propagation:** work-orders (harness repo) → [[research-candidates]] (#12 + index row) → [[index]] → log. No root hot-update: the empathy-geometry frontier state is unchanged (Phase 3 still closed pending the judge fix).

**Open, needs user:** OpenAI + DeepSeek snapshot IDs (pin at run time); parse-rate threshold (0.95 proposed); whether first-person derived accuracy weights `need_met` above lexical match of `valued` or reports them as two endpoints. Annotation-GUI work-order still unwritten.

The file was simultaneously *hash-frozen* in `EXTENSION_MANIFEST.json`, whose A1 record attested `"unchanged": "…analysis…"` — literally true (bytes unmoved), consequentially false (path broken). **Fix:** both call sites read a single `ACCEPTED_SPEC_VERSION = "bench/1.3"` with strict equality (gate *tightened*, not widened); A1's original wording is **retained verbatim** and now carries an explicit `unchanged_correction` rather than being rewritten. E3 is now paired-stem aware — TriviaQA is 100 stems × 2 correlated rows and the old row-stratified draw split them; grouped tasks now draw whole stems (verified: `triviaqa_paired` → 100 stems / 200 rows on all ten models), ungrouped tasks keep the exact pre-A2 RNG path, and each result carries its `subsample_unit`. Both now resolve by registered **content hash** (sha256 multiset), with `CONFLUENCE_HF_CACHE` as an override. Byte-verification preserved — portability gained without weakening the gate. (This is the principled fix for the stash bug caught earlier today, where a re-indent of the frozen Phase-2 manifests was attempted to chase path portability.)

**Phase 4 stays blocked — by code, not prose.** Re-stamping the manifest invalidates `SMOKE_SUMMARY.json`'s attestation of it, and `run_bench.py:842` enforces that equality ⇒ strict BENCH Phase 4 now **hard-fails** until the post-A3 Phase-3 smoke provenance re-audit completes. Follow-up to the A2/A3 landing above. The residual drift risk flagged in that entry is now a **filed work order**, not a loose note: `commit-confluence/stage_b/CODEX_WORKORDER_A4_SPEC_MODULE.md` (committed `26ac891`).

### 2026-07-22 — BENCH strict Phase-4 FINISHED: Family-A split (A1 PASS 10/10 / A2 FAIL 6/10 = intrinsic sign-flip); B1 7/20 = §8.1 commitment cascade, geometry intact; sealed 18/20 unperturbed

The strict Phase-4 confirmatory run launched 2026-07-15 (seed 20260711, nboot 2000, detached pid 6324) finished cleanly ~2026-07-22 02:51 — `stage_b/profiles_bench/SUMMARY.json` rewritten, 53 profiles emitted (60 cells − 7 smoke-level behavioral exclusions), log complete (`strict_phase4_a4.log`, ends with the full SUMMARY JSON block, no traceback/truncation). Verdict independently derived from the matrices, not read off SUMMARY.json. **Three independent reviews concur** (see Verification): a fresh-eyes read (fable), a Codex static audit, and a Codex `gpt-5.6-sol` high-reasoning static read of the A2 sign structure.

**Bottom line.** A1 (per-model in-sample geometric deployability) PASSES 10/10. A2 (fixed-cell, fixed-sign leave-one-model-out transfer) FAILS 6/10 — and the failure is an **intrinsic sign-flip**, not signal absence. B1 replication reads 7/20 FAIL but is a pre-registered commitment-audit cascade, not a geometric collapse. The sealed 18/20 (geom) / 18/20-FAIL (full panel) result of 2026-06-11 is byte-unperturbed. Net: the run **confirms the operational thesis** — per-model calibration deploys; a single frozen cell+sign does not transfer — while the confirmatory A1∧A2 conjunction for "the floor extends to HaluEval-QA" is **not satisfied** in its strong (transferable) form.

**A1 — PASS 10/10 (bar ≥8).** halueval_qa cluster-geometric endpoint (`PRE_REGISTRATION_BENCH.md:376`): all ten models terminal_status OK, cluster-deployable, controls pass, n=1000 / 500 stems, zero drops, zero non-canonical commitments. Weakest cluster CI-lo 0.6705. `SUMMARY.json:520`.

**A2 — FAIL 6/10 (bar ≥8), NOT aborted — the scientifically load-bearing result.** Registered A2 fixed cell = `fusion_rank_mean_geom`, one sign fit on the 9-model training pool then applied to the holdout (`A2_REGISTERED.json`, `analyze_universality.py:231,260-274`; sign applied consistently, no holdout-side refit). All holdouts fitted_sign=−1. The four failures sit far BELOW 0.5 — Mistral-7B 0.174, Mistral-Nemo 0.206, Qwen2.5-7B 0.276, Phi-3.5 0.394 — i.e. the signal is strongly present but inverted; flipping the sign rescues each (→ 0.826 / 0.794 / 0.724 / 0.606). **Intrinsic, not a pooling artifact:** every failing model independently selects +1 for `fusion_rank_mean_geom` in its OWN full-sample calibration, and every passing model selects −1 (their locally-oriented AUROCs equal 1 − holdout_auroc to rounding). Pool AUROCs sit near chance (0.512–0.590) precisely because sign heterogeneity washes out an equal-weight within-model-ranked pool.

Per-holdout (A2 fixed −1 / own-sign / verdict):
- Llama-3.2-3B 0.873 (own −1) PASS · Llama-3.1-8B 0.825 (own −1) PASS — both hold
- Mistral-7B 0.174 (own +1) FAIL · Mistral-Nemo 0.206 (own +1) FAIL — both flip
- Qwen2.5-7B 0.276 (own +1) FAIL — flips · Qwen3-1.7B 0.577 (own −1) PASS · Qwen3-8B 0.583 (own −1) PASS — Qwen SPLITS by generation
- Phi-3.5 0.394 (own +1) FAIL · Phi-4 0.724 (own −1) PASS — Phi SPLITS by generation
- gemma-3-4b 0.850 (own −1) PASS — holds

**Structure: generation-structured polarity, not a simple family law.** Mistral (both +1) and Llama (both −1) are internally consistent; Qwen and Phi split by generation. With only 1–2 representatives per subgroup, and family confounded with tokenizer/architecture/size, this stays **descriptive** — a real model-structured polarity pattern in these artifacts, not yet a causal family law.

**Framing refinement (honor in any paper/talk):** this establishes **no universal fixed orientation** (and, with 8 distinct A1 winners across 10 models, no universal *best* cell) — but NOT the absence of a common informative cell. `fusion_rank_mean_geom` with a per-model sign clears >0.55 on all ten. A2 therefore rejects the compound "fixed cell + fixed sign" deployment, not cell identity. The honest, licensed claim: halueval_qa is deployable under per-model calibration (A1 10/10); a frozen cell+sign does not transfer LOMO (A2 6/10), the four misses being intrinsic sign-inversions in Mistral, Qwen-2.5, and Phi-3.5.

**B1 (procedural + valid; geom ≥17/20 on anli_r1_rep + triviaqa_paired_rep): 7/20 FAIL — a §4/§8.1 commitment-audit cascade, not a geometric collapse.** Mechanism (`run_bench.py:784-829`): `_endpoint_value` returns False for every cell of any task with ≥3 COMMITMENT-FAIL cells (§8.1), before inspecting terminal status or geometry. triviaqa_paired_rep and anli_r2 each hit exactly 3 ⇒ `systematic_commitment_fail_tasks:["anli_r2","triviaqa_paired_rep"]`. All 10 triviaqa cells — including 7 terminal_status OK — are zeroed, so B1 = 7 (anli_r1_rep OK) + 0 (triviaqa) = 7/20. Layers: raw geometry among emitted Family-B profiles = 18/18 deployable (CI-lo 0.6577–0.9804, controls pass); pre-cascade admissibility = 14/20; post-§8.1 = 7/20. The commitment triggers are genuinely rare (`commitment_audit.n_noncanonical`): Llama-3.1-8B 1/1000, Qwen3-1.7B 1/1000, gemma-3-4b 12/1000 (~1.2%, gemma occasionally answers the trivia question instead of committing YES/NO). §4 has zero error budget, so a 99.9%-clean cell counts identically to a broken one. anli_r1_rep behavioral fails (Phi-3.5 `'\n'`; Qwen2.5-7B `' To'` CoT) are the signatures Amendment A1 explicitly declined to rescue — pre-disclosed, not new regressions. Per the frozen pre-reg (§8 amendment discipline) the rule cannot be retroactively softened without a new registration; B1's numeric FAIL is NOT propagated as a signal negative.

**Corrections to earlier same-day notes (now the record):** (a) Mistral-Nemo/triviaqa is a terminal_status OK cell zeroed only by the task-wide cascade — NOT a commitment-fail cell. (b) Sealed triviaqa peak is 0.94195 (Mistral-Nemo), not 0.918. (c) The three actual triviaqa commit-fails are Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-1.7B, gemma-3-4b.

**Family C (anli_r2, halueval_dialogue, halueval_summarization): descriptive, non-gating** — not part of A1/A2/B1. anli_r2's 3 commit-fails contribute to the systematic-task flag but do not gate a confirmatory endpoint.

**Provenance / isolation (Codex static audit):** post-A4 manifest sha256 `e5266ba7…`; all 8 manifest file-hashes match current bytes; A4 + the `bench_spec.py` entry present; A1's original `unchanged` wording intact with the A2 correction appended. All BENCH cells use only the ten registered `mlx-community/*-4bit` models, one module-hash set, Phase-1 frozen-row byte parity passed — no torch / mlx-vlm cell enters the counts. `stage_b/profiles/` (sealed) clean vs its publication commit `df90b5f`; Phase 4 wrote only under `profiles_bench/`.

## 2026-07-25 — Propagation pipeline HARDENED + claims/history backfilled · CANON UPDATED

**Root cause found and fixed: `wiki/claims.md` was rotting because it sat in no propagation checklist.** Both copies of the rule (root `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` Vault-canon rule 5, and `.claude/skills/vault-canon/SKILL.md` Mode 2) listed the same seven steps — result page → research-candidates → summary → index → root orientation → milestones → log. `claims.md`, `results/history.md`, `wiki/models/*`, and `wiki/paper/*` appeared in **none** of them. Two rotted silently; the other two survived only on ad-hoc sessions where MK noticed.

Evidence at diagnosis: `claims.md` last touched in `6c42e77` (2026-07-09, the vault publish commit) with **zero** occurrences of `BENCH`, `confluence`, `empathy`, `#11`, `#12` — the entire Commit-Confluence line, 2026-06-11 → 2026-07-22, existed outside any belief ledger. `results/history.md` tail ended **2026-06-25**, no BENCH row. `wiki/models/*` had been propagated 2026-07-23, but as a noticed-it session, not a rule.

**Decision (MK): promote `claims.md` to the vault-wide belief ledger.** The 2026-06-07 scope note declaring it "the PRI v1–v3 detection line only, with §10 as pointers" is retired — under that scope its silence on BENCH was technically correct and would have continued through empathy-geometry. Rationale: `claims.md` is the only surface answering *"what do we believe, and at what confidence"*; `summary.md` answers "what did the latest runs say", `history.md` "what numbers were measured, in order", `research-candidates.md` "which ideas are live". Four ledgers, four questions, none substitutable.

**What changed:**
- **`wiki/claims.md`** — scope note rewritten (vault-wide; §§0–9 kept intact as the PRI v1–v3 body; the old scope's failure recorded in place as provenance). §10 retitled *Morphology line + v5–v8 candidates*. **New §11 Commit-Confluence** — 4 subsections backfilling the whole line: sealed dispatcher (geometric 18/20 PASS bar ≥17 / strict full-panel 18/20 FAIL bar ≥19, same 2 ANLI cells, confidence-is-not-the-backstop, 12 winners ⇒ no universal cell); scale/generation/locus (both ANLI orphans = scale artifacts; CRAB-LOCK head-count REFUTED ~11% of gap; gen-4 2/2; Llama-70B readout-locus dissociation; H3 quantization-noise FALSIFIED at fixed-cell level); registered BENCH Phase-4 (A1 10/10 PASS, A2 6/10 FAIL with the §5 frozen-language *partial* wording pinned, sign-inversion-not-absence with the "reversal is not a rescue" guard, the "A2 rejects fixed-cell+fixed-sign, NOT cell identity" framing rule, generation-structured polarity as descriptive-only, flip-trio↔v4-E_A2-trio as untested observation, B1 7/20 as the registered verdict with 18/18 marked descriptive anatomy not a rescue endpoint, B2 with the §3.2 train-distribution caveat, label-cost ≥150 lower bound, stem-cluster 10/10, A4 exit NOT CAPTURED). **New §12 empathy-geometry** — deliberately carrying **no validated claims**: the hypothesis, the harness state (geometry ACCEPTED + parity-sealed / judge BLOCKER 6/72 parse), T4 persona-projection + H-iso, candidate #12 parked, and an explicit guardrail that entries here get a non-`[OPEN]` tag only after a registered run.
- **`wiki/results/history.md`** — appended a marked BACKFILL block, 16 rows in run order (sealed dispatcher · gemma-3-12b/Qwen-14B extension · CRAB-LOCK · gemma-4 · 32B/72B/Llama-70B torch panel · precision ladder · 32B stress panel · BENCH A1/A2/B1/B2 · stem-cluster sensitivity). Append-only respected — nothing above the marker was touched.
- **Root `CLAUDE.md` + `AGENTS.md` (together, byte-identical)** — Vault-canon **rule 5 replaced with an eleven-surface checklist** with per-surface *trigger conditions*, so firing no longer depends on memory: result page (always) · history.md (any new numeric endpoint) · claims.md (any belief-state change) · research-candidates (status moved only) · summary.md (always, in place) · models/ (per-model results) · index.md · paper + paper/README (in-scope **or** a pre-registration's frozen-language rule compels it) · root orientation (frontier moved, both together) · milestones (sign-off) · log (always, last). **New rule 5b:** every log entry must end with a *total* propagation line naming all eleven surfaces `updated` or `n-a: <reason>` — silence is a propagation bug. Rule 10's method-code list corrected `pri/ace/rpv` → `pri/ace/rpv/cc` (the CC papers have existed since 2026-06-24 outside the stated convention). Verified: rules 1–10 diff clean between the two files apart from the two intentional "mirrors X" / "not by Claude|Codex" lines.
- **`.claude/skills/vault-canon/SKILL.md`** — Mode 2 rewritten as the same eleven-surface table + per-surface notes; source-of-truth order gains `claims.md` at rank 4 (it is a ledger, not orientation prose) plus a one-line map of which ledger answers which question; paper-folder rule gains `cc`; **new Mode 3 `/vault-canon audit`** — compare each surface's last content change against the latest log entry and the recent result pages, flag surfaces a triggering result should have moved, cross-check the last ~5 log entries for their propagation lines, report as a table and **propose only** (backfilling a ledger is a canon edit, so no auto-fix). Known-closed gaps listed so the audit doesn't re-report them.
- **`wiki/index.md`** — `claims` and `results/history` rows rewritten to state the new scope and the backfill.

**Why now:** empathy-geometry is unrun, so it is the first experiment that can go through a hardened pipeline end-to-end, and it will produce the ledger's first `[HYPOTHESIS]`→`[VALIDATED]`/`[FALSIFIED]` transitions in months. Doing this after the run would mean backfilling twice.

**Not done (deliberate):** the drift-check *script* behind `/vault-canon audit` — Mode 3 is specified and runnable by hand, but held until the checklist itself has been used at least once, so the script encodes a settled contract rather than a fresh one. No milestone entry (internal vault-process change, not externally interesting). No candidate status moved. No paper file touched — the CC papers already carry every §11 number, and this session added no result.

**Verification:** `claims.md` §11/§12 numbers cross-checked against [[results/bench-a2-signflip-2026-07-22]], [[results/confluence-seal-2026-06-11]], [[results/gemma-scale-extension-2026-06-18]], [[results/llama-70b-scale-2026-06-22]], [[results/precision-ladder-results-2026-06-22]], and the 2026-07-22/23 log entries; the four framing guards (A2-partial wording, reversal-not-a-rescue, B1-not-a-signal-negative, B2 train-distribution caveat) carried verbatim in intent. `diff` confirms CLAUDE.md ≡ AGENTS.md on rules 1–10.

**Propagation (rule 5b, first use — all eleven):** 1 result page `n-a: process change, no experimental result` · 2 `history.md` **updated** (backfill block) · 3 `claims.md` **updated** (scope + §11 + §12) · 4 `research-candidates.md` `n-a: no candidate status moved` · 5 `summary.md` `n-a: no new run; latest verdict already current at 2026-07-22` · 6 `wiki/models/` `n-a: no per-model result this session` · 7 `index.md` **updated** (claims + history rows) · 8 paper `n-a: no result added; CC papers already carry every §11 number` · 9 root `CLAUDE.md` + `AGENTS.md` **updated together** (rules 5, 5b, 10) · 10 `milestones.md` `n-a: internal vault-process change` · 11 `log.md` **updated** (this entry).

## 2026-07-25 (cont.) — `/vault-canon audit` drift script shipped; 2026-07-23 milestone published · CANON UPDATED

Closed the two items the hardening entry above left open.

**1. Drift-check script — `.claude/skills/vault-canon/audit.py`** (local tooling; `.claude/` is gitignored, so it lives with the skill and does not travel with the public vault). Backs Mode 3. Determines a *trigger date* as the later of the newest `wiki/log.md` entry and the newest dated `wiki/results/*.md` page, then reports each of the eleven surfaces' last content change against it — git commit date where the surface is tracked, filesystem mtime where it is gitignored (surfaces 9 and 10, i.e. the root orientation files and the `milestones.md` symlink into `furnace-causalities`). It also scans the last N log entries for a rule-5b propagation line. **Advisory only — it proposes and never edits**, because backfilling a ledger is a canon edit. Known-closed gaps are hardcoded so it cannot re-report the CC backfill.

**First run found three true things, all correct:**
- `results/summary.md` **STALE 2d** — correctly flagged, correctly *not* a bug: the 2026-07-25 entry names it `n-a: no new run`. This is exactly the case the tool is designed to hand back to the propagation line rather than resolve itself.
- `milestones.md` **STALE 3d** — a real gap, closed below.
- **Three 2026-07-23 log entries carry no propagation line** (the cc-extend expansion, the re-grill, and the commit-confluence publish). Pre-rule-5b, so not retroactive violations — but they are the visible evidence that the old checklist let surfaces go unmentioned. Left as-is: the log is append-only and rule 5b is not retroactive.

**2. Milestone — `### 2026-07-23 — Publishing the benchmark arm that failed`.** Checked first: the two 2026-07-22 BENCH milestones (transfer verdict + frozen-rule cascade) **already existed and were already pushed** in `c42cd19`, so the long-standing "milestone needs sign-off" open item was partly stale. The genuine gap was 2026-07-23 — the merge that finally put the six-task extension on the public default branch, and the pre-registration §5 clause that forced the write-up to grow from one task to all three families. House style honored: one paragraph, numbers up front, no emojis, single public cross-ref last. Pushed: `furnace-causalities` `c42cd19..7317187`. The vault-process hardening itself was deliberately **not** given a milestone — internal bookkeeping, not externally interesting.

## 2026-07-25 — Sign-flip↔E_A2 trio coincidence screen: frozen, run, NULL · EXECUTOR / CANON UPDATED

**The open observation from the BENCH verdict is resolved: the trio↔trio overlap is a coincidence at this resolution.** The designed-retrospective screen `stage_b/SIGNFLIP_COINCIDENCE_DESIGN.md` was found already frozen in git (committed `1f70c9f` on public main, working tree clean, sha256 `fc79d7cbb865d698026667a82d16ec8e5e858f69958307b33ccdf95f4d3c761a`) with no prior execution. Pre-run static validation confirmed every design premise without peeking at signs: all nine cohort models have ≥2 auxiliary matrices (Qwen2.5-7B lacks only anli_r2; gemma-3-4b lacks only halueval_summarization; Phi-3.5 absent from all three — exactly as the cohort rule states), `load_cells` keys by (slug, task) with production fusion append, `SEAL._score_candidate` returns (auc, sign, n), scipy 1.13.1 present in the repo venv. The frozen heredoc was then executed **verbatim, zero deviations**, stdout captured as `stage_b/profiles_bench/SIGNFLIP_COINCIDENCE_RESULT.json` (committed + pushed `51b61e2`).

**Result — NULL.** Fisher exact two-sided **p = 0.50**, 2×2 table [[3,0],[4,2]] (trio × positive-majority), odds ratio nonfinite (zero cell). No abort: all nine models had ≥2 usable tasks. Positive-orientation majority on the auxiliary tasks is simply the cohort norm — **7 of 9 models** — so trio membership predicts nothing. Sharpest row against the sub-family reading: **Phi-4-mini (non-trio) is sign-identical to both Mistrals (+1/+1/−1) on all three auxiliary tasks.** Full sign table on the result page. Limits stated: retrospective, coarse majority binary, n=9, full-data sign fits; a null cannot rule out a sub-family — it removes the cheap evidence that would have motivated a registered fresh-data test. anli_r2 caveat: it is a systematic_commitment_fail task; the frozen design named it with no admissibility carve-out, so its signs ride on behaviorally-degraded-task matrices.

**Canon moves:** `[OPEN — untested observation]` → `[RESOLVED — screened NULL]` on claims §11, summary, and both root orientation files. No follow-up registered run is motivated by this result.

## 2026-07-25 (cont.) — Repo-standalone directive: coupling map + harness packaging fixed · EXECUTOR / NEEDS USER DECISION

MK directive: **each repo standalone.** Surveyed the five Furnace repos for what actually couples them, and separated two kinds of coupling that need opposite treatment.

**Kind 1 — infrastructure coupling. Pure liability; sever all of it.** Borrowed interpreters (the 07-13 empathy run executed under `t0-morphology-furnace/.venv`, Python 3.9, while the harness's own `pyproject.toml` declares `requires-python = ">=3.11"` — the metadata already contradicted the environment it ran in); hardcoded `/Users/msrk/Documents/...` absolute paths in `PRI_at_commitment/scripts/diagnostics/*` and `furnace-guard/scripts/smoke_tests.py`; undeclared dependencies; missing packaging. Nothing scientific is lost by cutting any of it.

**Kind 2 — sealed-code coupling. Do NOT sever by copying.** `commit-confluence/sealed_selector.py` calls `t0-morphology-furnace` "the sealed dependency repo" in as many words, and the harness's gen-1 parity check derives its meaning from recomputing against the *sealed* implementation. If each repo vendors its own copy of `pri_runtime` / `model_adapters`, the parity check silently degrades from "matches the sealed implementation" to "matches our copy of it", and the 18/20 seal's reproducibility anchor becomes N drifting copies instead of one frozen one. **Recommended mechanism instead: make t0 pip-installable and pin it to its sealed tag `t0-ace-sealed-2026-05-26`, then have each consumer declare it as a dependency inside its own venv.** That satisfies standalone in the sense that matters — every repo builds from its own metadata, no sibling-directory assumption, no borrowed interpreter — while keeping exactly one copy of the sealed code, identified by tag. NEEDS USER DECISION: pinned-package vs. vendored-copy. I have not vendored anything.

**Per-repo state as found:**
- `empathy-geometry-harness` — pyproject present but unbuildable; **fixed this session** (below). Geometry path still reaches `t0` (`pri_runtime`, `model_adapters`) and `furnace-guard` (`mlx_furnace_scorer`) via `sys.path` injection, both env-var overridable (`EG_T0_REPO`, `EG_FURNACE_GUARD_DIR`).
- `commit-confluence` — best packaged of the five (`requirements-runtime.txt`, `requirements-analysis.txt` + a `.lock`), two venvs incl. `.venv_gemma4`. Depends on t0 via `CONFLUENCE_T0_REPO`, deliberately.
- `t0-morphology-furnace` — `requirements.txt` only, **not pip-installable**, so it cannot currently *be* a pinned dependency; one script (`scripts/commitment_tracker.py`) reaches into `PRI_at_commitment/scripts` by hardcoded `~/Documents` path, and that file exists identically in both repos.
- `PRI_at_commitment` — venv + `requirements.txt`; hardcoded absolute output paths in diagnostics.
- `furnace-guard` — **worst off: no venv, no packaging metadata at all**, and reads commit-confluence calibration profiles through hardcoded `/Users/msrk/Documents/...` paths. It is the operator-facing guard, so it is also the one most likely to be run on another machine.

**Executed — harness now genuinely standalone for every non-geometry path** (`empathy-geometry-harness` commit `f74eccb`, local, not pushed). `pip install -e .` had been failing outright on package discovery, and the dependency list declared only `anthropic` while `numpy` is imported at module load by `persona_vectors.py` and `runner.py`. Added a `[build-system]`, explicit package discovery, the `numpy` dependency, an `eg-harness` console script, and moved MLX to a `[geometry]` extra so judge/checker/LOBO/tests install anywhere while geometry capture stays Apple-silicon-only. Verified in a clean venv built from metadata alone: **install OK, 69 tests + 4 subtests pass, entrypoint runs.** Only `pyproject.toml` was staged — the other 28 dirty files are prior uncommitted work and remain the author's to review.

**Note this also dissolves gate 2 from the earlier audit** — with its own venv the harness installs `anthropic` directly, so the hosted judge no longer needs a package added to the sealed t0 venv. What remains is the geometry half: `--provider mlx` still needs `pri_runtime`/`model_adapters`, which is exactly the Kind-2 decision above.

**Not done:** the multi-repo refactor itself (code authoring routes to Codex); no vendoring; nothing pushed; `furnace-guard` and `t0` packaging untouched pending the pinned-package decision.

2026-07-25 (cont. 2) — Codex work order written for the pinned-package t0 dependency · DESIGN ONLY / NEEDS EXECUTOR

MK froze the decision: **pinned-package, not vendored-copy.** Work order authored for Codex `gpt-5.6-sol` at [[repo-standalone-workorder-2026-07-25]] (spec in the vault, code in the repos, per the build-plan hard rule).

**Two facts verified before writing, both of which shaped the design.** (1) **The 13 sealed root modules have not drifted since the seal** — `git diff --stat t0-ace-sealed-2026-05-26 HEAD -- '*.py'` reports changes only under `exploratory/` and `paper/`, so packaging can be added at HEAD and a new tag cut whose sealed core is provably identical to the sealed tag. (2) **`t0-morphology-furnace` is PUBLIC**, not private — the root `CLAUDE.md` line calling it "the clean private ACE/T0 archive repo" is stale (orientation decay, canon rule 4). This matters structurally: a public repo means `git+https://…@tag` resolves with no credentials on any machine, which is what makes a pinned dependency clean rather than an auth problem. Left the stale line alone for now — it is a one-word orientation fix, not a frontier change.

**Design spine.** The sealed tag predates packaging, so nothing can be installed from it; the work order therefore requires a new tag plus one load-bearing assertion — the 13 sealed modules must be byte-identical between `t0-ace-sealed-2026-05-26` and the packaging tag, checkable by a single `git diff` that must print nothing. Packaging is **additive**: setuptools `py-modules` exposes the flat root modules as importable **without moving a single file**, so the frozen core stays frozen (no `src/` layout, no package dir, no root `__init__.py`). D2/D3 then delete the `sys.path` injection in `eg_harness/providers.py` and the `CONFLUENCE_T0_REPO` path in `sealed_selector.py`/`confluence_calibrator.py`, replacing both with a declared, tag-pinned dependency and an explicit error that tells a steward the old env var is now inert rather than silently ignoring it. D4 (furnace-guard: no packaging and no venv at all, plus hardcoded `/Users/msrk/...` profile paths) is scoped as follow-on.

**Guardrails written in.** commit-confluence is under registered pre-registration discipline, so D3 is fenced to two import sites and the requirements files, with an instruction to **stop and report** if any change would alter a module hash recorded in `EXTENSION_MANIFEST.json`. The stated non-goal is behavioral: this changes *how code is found*, never *what it computes* — any diff touching a numeric path is out of scope by definition. Six acceptance commands are printed for an executor and marked **not run by Codex** per the write/audit-only rule; A5 (`check-gen1-parity` still PASS at rtol 1e-5 after the swap) is flagged as the one that matters scientifically, with an explicit instruction not to tune the tolerance if it fails.

**Open for MK (both in the work order):** the packaging tag name (`t0-pkg-v0.1.0` proposed — the pin string must match exactly), and the `requires-python` floor. The second is the one that can bite: the canonical run venv is **Python 3.9** while the harness declares `>=3.11`, and packaging forces that latent contradiction into the open. Moving the canonical env to 3.11 would require re-capturing the gen-1 parity fixture, so it is explicitly **not** a packaging decision.

2026-07-25 (cont. 3) — Repo-standalone work order: both open decisions frozen by MK · DESIGN ONLY / READY FOR CODEX

MK froze the two items [[repo-standalone-workorder-2026-07-25]] left open: **packaging tag `t0-pkg-v0.1.0`** and **t0 `requires-python = ">=3.9"`**. Both folded into D1 and into a new "Frozen decisions" section replacing "Open decisions for MK"; the work order is now implementable end to end with no ambiguity.

**The interpreter contradiction dissolves rather than needing a fix — recorded so nobody "corrects" it later.** The apparent conflict was: canonical run venv on Python 3.9, harness declaring `>=3.11`. It existed *only because the harness borrowed t0's venv*. After D2 the harness has its own venv and installs t0 into it as a dependency; t0 at `>=3.9` installs happily on 3.11, the harness keeps `>=3.11`, and both declarations become true simultaneously — geometry runs in the harness's own interpreter instead of a borrowed one. The work order now says explicitly: do **not** lower the harness to `>=3.9`, do **not** raise t0 above `>=3.9`.

**Verification supporting the 3.9 floor (executor, this session):** all 13 sealed root modules compile clean under **Python 3.11.15** (13/13, `py_compile` with `doraise`), so `>=3.9` is an honest declaration spanning 3.9 → 3.11 at the syntax level. Noted in the work order that syntax-clean is not runtime-clean — acceptance items A2 and A5 are what cover runtime. (First attempt at this check reported a false pass: zsh does not word-split unquoted parameters, so `py_compile` received all 13 names as a single filename and the `&&` printed success anyway. Redone in Python with explicit paths; the 13/13 figure is from the corrected run.)

**New risk named in the work order — A5 is now a cross-interpreter test.** The gen-1 parity fixture was captured under **py3.9 + mlx_lm 0.29.1**; after D2 geometry recomputes in the harness's 3.11 venv, so `check-gen1-parity` tests both that the pinned tag delivers the sealed implementation *and* that the parity result is interpreter-independent. Written into the work order: if A5 fails, **do not adjust the tolerance and do not re-capture the fixture** — regenerating the fixture under a new interpreter would destroy the reference point the seal is measured against and convert a real finding into a silent one. The correct fallback is to floor the harness at `>=3.9` and rebuild its venv on 3.9 so the fixture's environment is reproduced exactly, then re-run; and to log the failure either way, because an interpreter-bound parity fixture is a genuine finding about the seal's portability.
