## 2026-07-25 — Pre-flight audit of the repo-standalone work order (executor prep; PROPAGATION AUDIT)

Executor-side preparation before handing D1–D4 to Codex. No repo code changed; the work order gained a pre-flight gate.

**Finding 1 — a sealed module is dirty in the working tree.** `t0-morphology-furnace` **HEAD (`7c2fcb7`) is clean**: all 13 sealed root modules are byte-identical to `t0-ace-sealed-2026-05-26`, confirming the work order's measured-facts table. The **working tree** is not — `pri_calibrator.py` carries **+64 uncommitted lines** adding an opt-in `--attention-kv-tension` metric family (`ATTENTION_METRICS_KV_TENSION`, three new panels, widened `_split_attention_label`), with companion uncommitted edits in `scripts/diagnose_inter_head_disagreement.py` and `tests/test_attention_cells.py`. `pri_calibrator.py` is #5 on the 13-module sealed list.

Consequence, mechanical: had D1 been committed with a broad stage (`git commit -a` / `git add .`), the drift would have ridden into `t0-pkg-v0.1.0` and A1 would have failed **on an already-published tag**. Added **A0** (a pre-commit `git status --porcelain` over exactly the 13 modules) + **A0'** (`git diff --stat HEAD~1 HEAD` must show only `pyproject.toml` and `PACKAGING.md`), and an instruction that D1 stage by explicit path only.

Consequence, governance: flagged, **not acted on**. The change is additive and opt-in — its own comment says "deliberately kept out of the sealed ACE default" — which is the spirit of the freeze, but it sits in a frozen file rather than in `exploratory/`. MK's call; the work order explicitly forbids Codex from committing, reverting, stashing, or relocating it.

**Finding 2 — sealed-core baseline pinned by content hash.** Recorded the 13 git blob SHA-1s at the sealed tag into the work order, so A1 is verifiable via `git hash-object` without trusting any tag or commit. (`pri_calibrator.py` sealed blob = `749151ad33f854fddce33b103537e39211fdaf74`.)

**Finding 3 — A4/A5 baselines confirmed runnable.** `empathy-geometry-harness` at `f74eccb` re-verified: **69 passed, 4 subtests** in its own venv (matches the A4 expectation). A5 is not vapor — `check-gen1-parity` is wired at `eg_harness/cli.py:392` and `tests/fixtures/gen1-parity.json` is a real 5.5 KB executor-captured fixture (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-4bit, provenance-stamped per-metric as INDEPENDENT), not the 165-byte template beside it.

**Method note (self-caught).** Two of my first attempts used an unquoted `$MODS` shell variable holding 13 filenames; zsh does not word-split unquoted parameters, so git received one long filename, errored, and the surrounding loop still printed a clean-looking verdict. Same class of false positive as the `py_compile` incident earlier today. Both checks were redone in Python with an explicit list. **Treat any shell loop over a filename variable in this vault as suspect until re-run under an explicit list.**

Status: work order remains **ready for Codex**; both MK decisions still frozen (`t0-pkg-v0.1.0`, `>=3.9`). Nothing in this entry changes a verdict, a number, or a belief.

**TOTAL propagation:** (1) results/<slug> — n-a: no experimental result, this is executor prep on a work order; (2) results/history.md — n-a: no new numeric endpoint; (3) claims.md — n-a: no belief-state change; (4) research-candidates.md — n-a: no candidate status moved (#11 empathy-geometry unchanged); (5) results/summary.md — n-a: no run to summarize; (6) models/<model>.md — n-a: not a per-model result; (7) index.md — **updated** (work-order row notes the A0 pre-flight gate and the dirty-module hazard); (8) paper — n-a: infrastructure only, no paper-scope claim; (9) CLAUDE.md + AGENTS.md — n-a: active frontier unchanged (the stale "private t0" orientation line remains open, tracked in the work order); (10) milestones.md — n-a: not milestone-worthy, an internal pre-flight catch; (11) log.md — **updated** (this entry).


## 2026-07-25 — t0 re-sealed as archive-only; two exploratory lanes relocated to commit-confluence; an unscored 5-model pilot surfaced and scored (CANON UPDATED)

**MK instruction:** `t0-morphology-furnace` is not to be touched. Its working tree must stay byte-identical to `t0-ace-sealed-2026-05-26` — that byte-identity is the whole basis on which `t0-pkg-v0.1.0` can be trusted to deliver the sealed implementation. Whatever was being built there moves to `commit-confluence`.

**Executed.** t0 is now **pristine** (`git status --porcelain --untracked-files=all` empty; **A0 pre-flight PASS**, all 13 sealed root modules byte-identical to the sealed tag). Three independent backups were taken before anything was touched: a `git diff` patch, a status snapshot, and a tarball of the untracked trees, all in the session scratchpad; the patch is additionally committed inside commit-confluence.

**What moved** (commit-confluence `f2db5e9`, copies verified byte-identical by `diff -r`):
- `exploratory/attention-kv-tension/` — pre-registration draft, `run_pilot.sh`, and the full `pilot_outputs/2026-06-09/` tree (both runs).
- `exploratory/v-norm-attention/` — the V-norm audit note.
- `exploratory/attention-kv-tension/t0-patch/kv-tension-against-t0-7c2fcb7.patch` — the sealed-core diff, carried as **provenance and left unapplied**.
- New `exploratory/README.md` + a lane README carrying the verdict.

**Why commit-confluence is the right home, not a dumping ground.** `confluence_calibrator.py` already imports the sealed selector **read-only** and declares additive panels locally (`READOUT_PANEL`), with a vendored `sealed_selector.py` fallback. A new metric family there never needs to edit a sealed file — which is exactly what went wrong in t0, where the KV-tension metrics were written as an in-place edit to `pri_calibrator.py`, `scripts/diagnose_inter_head_disagreement.py`, and `tests/test_attention_cells.py`. **This supersedes the 2026-06-07 "T0 furnace → living morphology lab" canon**; that block is now marked HISTORICAL in `CLAUDE.md`.

**The substantive find: the Stage-3 pilot had actually RUN, and nobody scored it.** 5 models, ANLI R1, t=0, n=200, `nboot=1000`, seed 20260512, completed 2026-06-08 22:47 PDT. Scored today against its own pre-registered bars — **NO-PROMOTE, not cleanly falsified**. Detail: [[results/kv-tension-pilot-2026-06-09]].

Best-KV AUROC (absolute orientation), delta vs best **routing** cell / vs best **any existing ACE** cell, OOB CI-lo:
Qwen3-8B 0.8479 (+0.0075 / +0.0075, 0.7382) · Mistral-7B 0.8065 (+0.0195 / +0.0195, 0.6931) · Qwen2.5-7B 0.7535 (**+0.0486 / −0.0261**, 0.6474) · Phi-4-mini 0.7374 (**+0.0614** / +0.0219, 0.5806) · gemma-3-4b 0.6379 (−0.0521 / −0.0521, **0.4960 not clean**).

All three promotion limbs fail: (1) ≥+0.03 on **2/5** against routing-only, **0/5** against any existing ACE cell; (2) `winner_unstable` fires on **4/5** — including *both* models carrying the win (Phi-4-mini 0.63, Qwen3-8B 0.59, Mistral 0.60, gemma 0.36); (3) the **shuffled-label control was never run**, so the bar cannot be satisfied as written. The pre-registered BOS/sink falsification clause is *partially* triggered — on Qwen2.5-7B the selected winner is plain `final_bos_mass`, beating the best KV cell by 0.0261.

**Two process lessons, worth more than the result.** (a) **The comparator set decided the verdict and the pre-registration never enumerated it** — 2/5 versus 0/5 on identical numbers, turning entirely on whether `bos_mass` is a "routing comparator." Future registrations must name the comparator cells. (b) **A finished 5-model pilot sat unscored and uncommitted for six weeks**, found only by an unrelated repo sweep — precisely the drift class rule 5b was installed to catch, and it predates that rule.

**Still open:** the KV-tension implementation is not runnable anywhere — it exists only as the unapplied patch. Re-homing it as an additive overlay in commit-confluence is a build task (Codex). Do not apply it to t0.

**TOTAL propagation:** (1) results/<slug> — **updated** ([[results/kv-tension-pilot-2026-06-09]], new, covering both lanes); (2) results/history.md — **updated** (one appended row, 5 model endpoints); (3) claims.md — **updated** (§10 gains `[PILOT RUN — NO-PROMOTE]` attention KV-tension and `[RESOLVED — NO-PROMOTE]` last-query V-norm); (4) research-candidates.md — n-a: neither lane is a numbered candidate, and no numbered candidate status moved (#5 ACE unchanged — this is a follow-up lane that failed to promote, not a change to ACE itself); (5) results/summary.md — **updated** (new section + `_Last updated_` bumped); (6) models/<model>.md — **updated** ×5 (qwen-2.5-7b, qwen-3-8b, mistral-7b, phi-4-mini, gemma-3-4b, each with its own per-model reading + backlink); (7) index.md — **updated** (new results row); (8) paper — n-a: exploratory no-promote in a lane no live paper claims; no registered reporting obligation, since the KV-tension draft was never filed as a binding registration; (9) CLAUDE.md + AGENTS.md — **updated together, blocks verified byte-identical** (new 2026-07-25 hot update; the 2026-06-07 living-lab block marked HISTORICAL in CLAUDE.md, which is the only file that carried it); (10) milestones.md — n-a: internal repo hygiene plus an unpromoted exploratory pilot; not externally milestone-worthy, and would need MK sign-off regardless; (11) log.md — **updated** (this entry).


## 2026-07-25 — Work order: re-home the KV-tension implementation as an additive overlay in commit-confluence (DESIGN ONLY)

Follow-on to the t0 re-seal earlier today. The KV-tension metrics survive only as an unapplied diff against three sealed t0 modules, so the lane is currently not runnable anywhere. Wrote `wiki/kv-tension-overlay-workorder-2026-07-25.md` for Codex `gpt-5.6-sol`.

**Scope is deliberately narrow and stated up front in the order itself: this restores runnability, NOT standing.** The lane is `[PILOT RUN — NO-PROMOTE]`; the order explicitly forbids new runs, new numbers, reviving or amending the 2026-06-09 pre-registration, and adding the cells to any default panel. Any future run needs a fresh registration that enumerates its comparator cells by name and makes the shuffled-label control a launch blocker — the two failures that decided the pilot.

**Key design finding — the seam already exists, so no monkeypatching and no sealed edit is needed.** `confluence_calibrator.py:513 collect_ace_matrix` already owns the whole capture loop (opens `attention_capture`, calls `SEAL._trace_one_prompt`, then `SEAL._compute_panel_scores_for_sample` at l.557, then fills `sm[i,j]`), and `stage_b/gemma4_full_extract.py:90` already calls `SEAL._compute_attention_score` per cell. So KV cells can be scored in CC off the **same `sample_caps` snapshot** the sealed panel used, in the same iteration. A second capture pass was explicitly rejected in the order — it would be a different measurement wearing the same name.

**Deliverables.** D1 new flat root module `kv_tension_panel.py` (CC convention), 4 metrics + 1 helper ported verbatim — the order pins the two conventions that are easy to "improve" by mistake: `js_within_kv_groups` returns **0.0** for MHA (no within-group DoF) while `js_kv_tension_ratio` returns **NaN** for MHA (structural zero denominator, i.e. not-applicable rather than no-signal). D2 an **inert, opt-in** `extra_cells`/`extra_scorer` hook on `collect_ace_matrix`, both defaulting to `None`, with extra columns **appended after** the sealed panel so existing column indices are untouched — this matters because that function feeds registered BENCH artifacts. D3 all 8 patch tests ported to `test_kv_tension_panel.py` plus a new inertness test. D4 docs; `PRE_REGISTRATION_DRAFT.md` stays frozen.

**Acceptance B1–B5, marked not-run-by-Codex.** B1 (t0 still clean) is load-bearing — if t0 is dirty afterwards the port went the wrong way, and the order says stop and report rather than revert quietly, because *how* it got dirty is the finding. B4 (registered BENCH import path unchanged with the new params absent) is second.

**Flagged, not actioned:** `commit-confluence/vendor/t0_core/` duplicates part of the sealed core, and once `t0-pkg-v0.1.0` exists both it and the `CONFLUENCE_T0_REPO` `sys.path` injection are redundant. Consolidation touches the registered BENCH import path, so it is recorded as a separate MK decision rather than folded into this port.

**TOTAL propagation:** (1) results/<slug> — n-a: design only, no run and no endpoint (the lane verdict was recorded earlier today in [[results/kv-tension-pilot-2026-06-09]]); (2) results/history.md — n-a: no numeric endpoint; (3) claims.md — n-a: no belief-state change, the NO-PROMOTE tag set earlier today is unaffected by re-homing the code; (4) research-candidates.md — n-a: no numbered candidate status moved; (5) results/summary.md — n-a: nothing ran; (6) models/<model>.md — n-a: not a per-model result; (7) index.md — **updated** (new work-order row); (8) paper — n-a: unpromoted exploratory instrument, no live paper claim and no registered reporting obligation; (9) CLAUDE.md + AGENTS.md — n-a: frontier unchanged, the t0-archive-only canon block written earlier today already covers where new lanes live; (10) milestones.md — n-a: internal tooling work order, not externally milestone-worthy; (11) log.md — **updated** (this entry).

**The merge (925 lines):** parent Intro/Method/Results kept; condensed extension § replaced by the extension's full content as §'The registered BENCH extension' (`\label{sec:bench}`) with subsections design/A1/A2/B1/B2/family-C/universality/scope/provenance/forward-work; scale section labeled `sec:scale` and B2's 'parent study' pointer rewired to it; 2 `ccpaper` self-citations + all 'this companion'/'parent study' framings dissolved into internal cross-refs; duplicated 'two repairs' + label-cost + comparability passages deduplicated to single statements; abstract extended with the frozen 'partially extends, A2 named' wording, the B1 7/20 miss, and B2; contributions gain item (iv). **Frozen-language survivals verified: each registered sentence now appears exactly once in the body (+ abstract where 'prominently' applies).** Forward-work UPDATED to report the executed coincidence screen (frozen design, Fisher p=0.50 NULL, [[results/signflip-coincidence-2026-07-25]]) instead of the stale 'drafted, not run'. Label-cost hardened per MK's question: ≥150 measured lower bound (no n=200 point exists — the '~150–200 sufficient' memory is the retired claim), 14/18 reach ≥0.8 at 150 (some deployments need more), HaluEval-QA label cost explicitly unmeasured.

**Static checks green:** env balance, refs↔labels, cites↔bibitems bidirectional (no orphans), 8/8 figures present, braces balanced, stale-phrase sweep clean. **Bundle:** `cc-paper-2026-07-26.zip` (tex + all 8 figs) — supersedes both 07-23 zips. Overleaf compile = MK.

Registration language outranks style, always.

## 2026-07-26 — Empathy-geometry cell panels run standalone in the EG repo, bit-identical to the sealed venv

**Instruction (MK):** "split and /loop until its done + cell panels can run standalone in EG repo." Taken as: choose the split-venv option, run it to completion without checking in per step, and make the geometry panels executable from the harness's own repo rather than a borrowed interpreter.

**The fork that was open.** The 07-25 runnability audit left one blocking decision: install `anthropic` into `t0-morphology-furnace/.venv` (perturbing the environment that defines sealed reproductions) or split across two venvs on the gemma-4 precedent. MK chose the split and sharpened it — the sealed checkout becomes a **read-only import source and parity reference**, and everything, geometry included, runs in the EG venv.

**What was actually in the way** — three couplings, none intrinsic:
1. Geometry only ran as a side effect of `run-real`, so scoring a prompt's panels required twelve dialogue turns and a judge model.
2. `pyproject.toml` declared `mlx-lm` alone. The sealed modules the panels import need more: `pri_runtime` imports **pandas** and **scikit-learn** at module load, `comprehensive_run` imports `sklearn.linear_model`. Discovered one `ModuleNotFoundError` at a time until I stopped and enumerated the closure statically.
3. The only fixture producer was `artifacts/_capture_parity_fixture.py` — monkeypatching three production seams, absolute paths baked in, never promoted, never tested. That is how the capability drifted out of reach in the first place.

**Built.** `eg_harness/panel.py` + `capture-panel` (21-cell t=0 ACE panel + gen-step-1 readout on plain prompts; **no dialogue, no persona bundle, no judge**; replay mode consumes a recorded `first_token_id` so no sampler runs and the capture is two deterministic forward passes) · `compare-panels` (cell-by-cell diff; row identity checked **first**, mismatch fatal for that row — metrics from different inputs produce a number that resembles agreement or disagreement but means neither) · an opt-in `capture_sink` on `_capture_gen1_readout_panel`, default `None`, which is what removes the need to monkeypatch the production function (`capture_geometry` does not opt in; a test asserts it) · a replay corpus recovered from the 07-13 run's stored `prompt_text`, verified to reproduce all six `prompt_sha256` and `first_token_id` values of the existing fixture · exact version pins for the full closure, `transformers` included because mlx-lm defers tokenization to it and a disagreeing tokenizer moves every metric silently rather than failing loudly.

**Result — bit-identical, not merely within tolerance.** EG venv (py3.11.15 / numpy 2.2.6) vs sealed venv (py3.9.6 / numpy 2.0.2): **174 values across 6 rows, worst relative delta 0.000e+00**, covering the full 21-cell attention panel, 3 scalars/row, and the five strict gen-step-1 metrics. Within-EG-venv `check-gen1-parity` (harness vs independent canonical `comprehensive_run`) also PASS at rtol 1e-5. Suite **99 passed, 4 subtests** (was 69). Both captures frozen under `tests/fixtures/` and re-compared by the suite, so the claim cannot rot into a stale README line. **`t0-morphology-furnace` verified clean and A0 PASS afterward** — nothing was installed into it or written to it.

**Also landed.** The 12-day working-tree backlog is committed (`7d05933`): the 11-item completion patch, schema-enforced judge, LOBO, parity fixture, both red-lined traits, T4 sweep script — committed as-authored, without review edits, so history separates prior work from this session's (`2225ee4`). Build metadata now gitignored. Staged by explicit path throughout, never `git add -A`.

**Correction to the 07-25 audit.** "No API credentials present" was wrong: an `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` **is** in the harness `.env` (110 chars), merely not exported to the shell. Unverified as live. Cost authorization is still outstanding and is the real constraint.

**Method note.** My first error handler for the canonical-import failure named the wrong cause — it told the reader to set `EG_T0_REPO` when the checkout was found correctly and the interpreter was simply missing `sklearn`. Opposite fixes. Now branches on `ModuleNotFoundError.name`. A diagnostic that confidently names the wrong cause is worse than none, because it spends the reader's attention in the wrong place — same class as the 07-25 shell-loop false positives. Related: `grep --include=*.py` aborted under zsh glob expansion again; quote the pattern.

**Scope discipline.** Infrastructure only. 6 rows, one bundle/arm (B2/giraffe), one model (Qwen2.5-7B-4bit). This says the *instrument* transfers across interpreters; it says nothing about empathy geometry, arm separation, or any detector claim. Raw geometry remains uncalibrated. Cross-model bit-identity is unmeasured.

**Critical path now:** MK initials the four ⟨MK⟩ freezes (R1–R4) → Codex implements deliverable B (POV pins) → re-run the 72-row validation with `--judge anthropic` → Phase 3. The venv step is gone from that chain.

**Deviation from standing routing:** per [[memory]] convention MK routes code-authoring to Codex; this was Claude-authored on an explicit "loop until done" instruction. Flagging rather than assuming the routing changed.

## 2026-07-26 (cont.) — E3-HaluEval descriptive label-cost sweep: measured KNEE at 150 labels, 10/10 flat to 500 · CANON UPDATED

**The 'HaluEval label cost is UNMEASURED' gap is closed — descriptively.** Per MK's request, spec'd and ran the cheap remedy: an E3-style sweep on the ten published `halueval_qa` matrices (no new inference; pure CPU statistics). Spec frozen + committed BEFORE execution (`stage_b/E3_HALUEVAL_DESCRIPTIVE_SPEC.md` @ `fde0349`, sha256 `b525a0c4…ed69ed`): production `label_efficiency` verbatim (Amendment-A2 stem-aware subsampler inherited, `subsample_unit=stem` confirmed on all cells), budgets {50,100,150,300,500}, repeats 10, nboot 1000, seed 20260613 (sealed-E3 convention), explicitly DESCRIPTIVE/POST-HOC — matrices pre-existed, cannot upgrade A1/A2/B1. Pre-committed knee criterion: flat across two consecutive budgets.

**Result: all ten models reach 1.0 subsample deployability by 150 labels and stay flat at 1.0 through 300 and 500 — a genuine measured knee**, the plateau the sealed tasks never showed (still rising at their max budget). 9/10 already at 1.0 by 100 labels. Detail + full 10×5 table: [[results/e3-halueval-descriptive-2026-07-26]].

**Three interpretive reads:** (1) HaluEval-QA is a descriptively CHEAPER calibration target than ANLI/TriviaQA (~100–150 labels vs mean-0.79-at-150 with 4/18 unstable) — plausibly because A1 signal strength is high (CI-lo 0.67–0.90) and stronger separations certify with fewer labels. (2) The pre-stated prediction held: last to certify is exactly the weakest A1 cell (Qwen3-1.7B: 0.2/0.9/1.0 at 50/100/150) — label cost tracks signal strength. (3) Sign-flippers are NOT label-hungry (Phi-3.5 perfect at 50; Qwen2.5/Mistrals by 100): orientation is cheap to fix per-model, it just cannot be TRANSFERRED — A2 stands untouched.

**Execution note (provenance):** first run was user-stopped at 6/10 cells; the six logged results were recovered verbatim and merged with a fresh run of the remaining four — exact because `label_efficiency` seeds per (budget, repeat) as `RandomState(seed + 1000·nsub + r)`, independent of cohort composition; recorded in the JSON's `execution_note`. Artifact `stage_b/profiles_bench/E3_HALUEVAL_DESCRIPTIVE.json` committed + pushed `cdf7e87`.

**Paper fold-in:** the merged `cc-draft.tex` §'Label cost on the new task' upgraded from 'untested' to the descriptive estimate (clearly labeled post-hoc/not-registered, with the fresh-data caveat); static checks green; `cc-paper-2026-07-26.zip` REBUILT. The paper no longer contains the word-level claim 'is untested' anywhere.

## 2026-07-26 (later) — Adversarial review of the standalone-panel work: two of my claims were over-strong

**Instruction (MK):** Codex 5.6 audits adversarially → hand code changes to Opus 5 at a sensible effort level → run it.

**Codex `gpt-5.6-sol` (static, ran nothing) returned 9 findings, 5 MAJOR.** Two land directly on claims I published into this vault earlier today:

1. 🔴 **"Bit-identical" was asserted, not tested.** `compare_captures` ran at `rtol=1e-5 / atol=1e-7`, and the field I quoted as evidence — `worst_relative_delta_among_failures` — is computed **only over failures**, so it reads `0.0` whenever everything passes regardless of drift inside tolerance. Near-zero attention cells (≈8.7e-7) make it concrete: `atol=1e-7` permits ~10% relative drift there. My underlying measurement *was* exact (an ad-hoc script computed worst rel delta 0.0), but the committed comparator could not substantiate it and the quoted field was the wrong evidence.
2. 🔴 **"The claim cannot rot silently" was false.** The regression test loads two frozen JSON files and never invokes the capture path; if capture were changed to echo stored values it would still pass.

Three more real ones: the canonical `surprise` column is an **echo** of the input corpus, not a `comprehensive_run` recomputation; **numpy was unpinned** while my prose said "pinned exactly"; `compare_captures` was **vacuous on degenerate input** (empty row sets passed, duplicate `row_id`s silently overwrote). Plus prose errors — replay is **three** forward passes, not two.

**One finding was misattributed, and chasing it found a mistake of mine.** Codex read the `pri`/`rpv` return-shape change as part of the `capture_sink` work. It is not — it came from the pre-existing backlog. But it landed in *my* commit, because `providers.py`, `cli.py`, `pyproject.toml`, and `README.md` were **already dirty** before this session, so staging them for the panel commit swept the backlog into a commit whose message describes only `capture_sink`. **My commit message's claim that history separates prior work from this session's is false for those four files.** Not rewritten (unpushed, content identical either way, rewriting risks more than it fixes) — recorded instead. **Lesson: staging by explicit path is necessary but not sufficient; a file already dirty for another reason carries that reason with it.**

**Fixes handed to an Opus 5 subagent, then run and verified by me.** Comparator now tracks `max_absolute_delta` and `max_relative_delta` **over every comparison**, supports exact mode (`--rtol 0 --atol 0`), and reports `exact_mode` and `bit_identical` as distinct fields (I renamed these last two after the handoff: `pass: false, exact: true` read as a contradiction — the field names the *mode*, not the *outcome*). Canonical surprise renamed `recorded_generation_surprise` with provenance saying plainly that nothing computes it. `numpy==2.2.6` pinned, with the 2.0.2-vs-2.2.6 span recorded as deliberate. Degenerate-input holes closed. Canonical import now verifies the resolved module path lies under the expected checkout. README rescoped to say what the suite does and does not guard, with the reproduction command inline.

**Re-verified by running it — the part neither agent could do.** Fresh `capture-panel` end to end, then exact-mode comparison: **`bit_identical: true`, `max_absolute_delta: 0.0`, `max_relative_delta: 0.0`, 174 values, 6 rows.** The fresh capture also reproduces the frozen candidate exactly, so the path is deterministic run-to-run. **And the comparator was shown capable of failing:** a single-ULP perturbation (`1.694e-21`) on one attention cell is rejected in exact mode while passing under default tolerance — which is exactly the hole that existed before. Historical `gen1-parity.json` still consumable. Suite **113 passed, 4 subtests**.

**Net:** the numbers did not move. The evidence for them did — from a tolerance-based check misreporting its own scope, to an exact-mode check that demonstrably fails on 1 ULP. The correction is worth more than the original claim was.

**TOTAL propagation (11 surfaces):** 1. results page — **updated** (correction section + commit-hygiene note) · 2. results/history.md — **updated** (appended correction row; earlier row left intact per append-only) · 3. claims.md — **updated** (§12 entry re-evidenced) · 4. research-candidates.md — **n-a: no candidate status moved** · 5. results/summary.md — **updated** · 6. wiki/models/ — **n-a: not a per-model finding** · 7. index.md — **updated** · 8. paper/ — **n-a: no live paper carries empathy-geometry** · 9. root CLAUDE.md — **n-a: EG frontier tracked in build-plan.md; the four ⟨MK⟩ freezes remain the gate** · 10. milestones.md — **n-a pending user sign-off** · 11. log.md — **updated** (this entry).

## 2026-07-26 (MK decisions) — judge pinned to Opus 5, spend approved, harness stays local; a NEW billing blocker found

**Three MK calls, recorded:**
1. **Primary hosted judge = `claude-opus-5`** (was `claude-opus-4-8`). One constant — `spec.DEFAULT_ANTHROPIC_JUDGE_MODEL` — drives the CLI default, the `run-main` fixed-judge refusal, stand-in detection, and generated docs, so the pin was a single edit plus doc sync. Harness `9e05ad3`, 113 passed.
2. **Spend approved.**
3. **Harness stays local — do not push.** Its GitHub remote is public; MK declined publication, so personas, arm blocks, and stimuli stay off the internet. The repo now sits 4 commits ahead of `origin/main` **by intent**, not by omission. Any future steward: do not "helpfully" sync it.

**⚠️ R1 is NOT closed by decision 1.** The four freezes define **R1 = *confirmatory* judge model**, which exists to be *independent* of the primary. Pinning the primary to Opus 5 leaves R1 open — a confirmatory judge identical to the primary is not a check. Flagged to MK rather than assumed either way.

**🔴 New blocker, found by actually testing it.** With spend approved I ran a live 8-token call against `claude-opus-5`. The key **authenticates** — so both prior statements were partly wrong: the 07-25 audit's "no API credentials present" *and* my 07-26 correction "a key is present" (present, yes; usable, no). The call returns **`400 invalid_request_error: Your credit balance is too low`**. **Hosted judging is blocked on billing, not credentials.** MK needs to purchase credits before any `--judge anthropic` run.

**Environment trap documented.** Nothing in the harness loads `.env`, and its values are shell-quoted. A naive `KEY=VALUE` parser (mine) passes the quotes through and the API answers `401 invalid x-api-key` — which reads as a bad key rather than a bad parse, and cost me one wrong conclusion before I checked the prefix. `source .env` is the intended path; now stated in the README.

**Critical path now:** MK initials R1–R4 (R1 still needs a *confirmatory* model distinct from Opus 5) **and** purchases API credits → Codex implements deliverable B (POV pins) → 72-row re-validation with `--judge anthropic` → Phase 3.

## 2026-07-26 (correction) — R1 was already answered; my "still open" caveat was a misreading

Earlier today I told MK that pinning the primary judge to `claude-opus-5` did **not** close **R1**, on the reasoning that a *confirmatory* judge should be a model independent of the primary. **That was wrong**, and I corrected [[empathy-geometry/build-plan]] in place.

Reading `artifacts/CODEX_ANTHROPIC_JUDGE_WORKORDER.md` §[R1] properly: "confirmatory" here does not mean *second opinion*. It names **the one registered instrument whose rows count as evidence**, in contrast to the OpenAI / DeepSeek / Gemma **descriptive robustness panel**, which is stamped `confirmatory: false` on every row it emits, always, regardless of agreement — there is explicitly **no aggregation rule across judge models**. The work order says outright that the confirmatory judge "is already `spec.DEFAULT_ANTHROPIC_JUDGE_MODEL`."

The thing that comes in pairs is the **POV**, not the model: two confirmatory endpoints, `first_person` for reflection and `third_person` for dialogue_act, produced by the **same** pinned model in three calls per judged turn. So `CONFIRMATORY_JUDGE_MODEL = "claude-opus-5"` matching the primary is the intended shape, not a degenerate check.

**Consequence:** MK's "Opus 5 main judge" closes **R1**. Remaining ⟨MK⟩ freezes are **R2** (lie-detector top-1 need by set membership), **R3** (`need_met`→binary LOBO map), **R4** (bias-gate formula).

**Implementation note for deliverable B, not an MK decision:** §3 prescribes pinning a *dated snapshot* and failing closed on echo mismatch. Current Claude model IDs are complete as-is with no date suffix, so the discover-and-pin step should record whatever `response.model` the API echoes at run start and fail closed against that. **Not yet observed** — our live probe never got an echo (first call 401 from a quoting bug, second 400 on credit balance).

## 2026-07-26 (later) — [R4] naming diagnostic built; a degeneracy claim RETRACTED; first build scored 3.5/10 and was rebuilt

**Instruction (MK):** shuffle the answers, fix it, last pass to ≥8.5/10, run it, loop until finished.

**The retraction is the main content.** I advised MK not to freeze R4 because its permutation null was "fatally degenerate": each bundle has exactly two personas with **disjoint** need sets (verified — all 12 cross-persona combinations miss), so swapping targets zeroes a swapped dialogue, the floor becomes `A × (fraction unswapped)` ≈ 0.63–0.83·A, and `A ≤ floor` is unsatisfiable for any `A > 0`. MK acted on that advice.

**It was wrong.** Simulating across naming-informativeness 0.0→1.0, **both nulls reach identical verdicts** (both flag at ≤0.2, both clear at ≥0.4). The premise `floor = A×f` holds only for a judge whose naming is *already chair-informative* — the alternative, not the null. Under H0 a swap changes the score not at all in expectation, the floor rises to meet the accuracy, and the gate fires correctly. **The error: adopting a floor formula derived under H1 as though it described the null distribution.** Codex sharpened it further — examining the permutation distribution on alternative-like data is exactly how one evaluates *power*; the defective step was concluding unsatisfiability from a premise valid only under H1.

⇒ **R4-as-written was freezable.** MK's directed switch to answer-shuffling was implemented and is retained **on its real merits** — finer resolution (multiset orderings vs `2**n_dialogues`) and preserved per-dialogue naming marginals — but recorded as an improvement, **not a repair**. The target-swap null is **kept as a pre-registered sensitivity analysis** under its own seed, with `nulls_disagree` reported.

**First implementation scored 3.5/10** (Codex `gpt-5.6-sol`, adversarial). Findings that mattered, all fixed: `acceptance_grade = not underpowered` meant **a cell with CONFIRMED flattery was still acceptance-grade** (the single most dangerous defect — the check ran, caught it, then waved it through); code diverged from the frozen pre-registration; nothing called the gate so it could block nothing; degeneracy *inferred from whether labels differed* rather than counted from achieved scores; zero landing claims **raised** instead of stamping underpowered; suspicion forced `False` when unevaluable, conflating "not evaluable" with "not suspected"; and a randomly-drawn "flattering" fixture made its own catch test seed-fragile (a 95th-percentile null rejects ~5% of true H0 samples by construction). Rebuilt as `eg-flattery-gate/2.0`.

**A pleasing self-indictment:** the corrected score-based degeneracy check immediately flagged **one of my own replacement test fixtures** as degenerate — a "varied" judge whose names all belonged to one persona is score-invariant under permutation. A check that only ever catches other people is not a check.

**Ran it.** `eg-harness flattery-gate` against the **real B2 personas**: faithful judge accuracy 1.000 vs floor 0.667 → accepted, **exit 0**; judge naming the other persona's need accuracy 0.000 → suspected, blocked, **exit 1**. Both nulls agree; null non-degenerate (10 distinct permuted scores). Suite **152 passed, 11 subtests**.

**Second integrity gap, found while committing:** `artifacts/` was gitignored wholesale, so **the pre-registration document itself was not under version control** — an unversioned pre-reg can be edited without trace, which is the one property a pre-registration exists to provide. Fixed with `artifacts/*` + `!artifacts/*.md`. The pattern matters: `artifacts/` excludes the *directory*, and git does not descend into an excluded directory, so a negation beneath it is never consulted.

**Amendment A1 filed pre-run** (no data exists), recording the answer-shuffle primary, the retained sensitivity null with the degeneracy claim retracted, giraffe-arm scoring so verdict and power check describe the same rows, tri-state suspicion, score-based degeneracy, and the scope limit — naming is conditioned on the judge's own `met` claim, so this is a **consistency diagnostic, not an independent endpoint validation**.

**Scoring status — HONEST:** MK set a ≥8.5/10 bar. All seven of Codex's stated 9/10 criteria are mechanically verified as addressed, but the **independent re-score has not returned** (the codex-rescue forwarder bounced twice without delivering). I am **not** self-certifying a number. Outstanding.

**Also open:** whether the gate should block inside a report/aggregation path (deliverable B, unbuilt); **R2 and R3 remain uninitialed** ⟨MK⟩ (R1 closed = `claude-opus-5`); hosted judging still blocked on **API credits**.

## 2026-07-26 (Amendment A2) — feelings get their own section rather than staying inert

**MK:** "I don't want it to be completely moot. Just have it be a different section." Read as: the [R2] decision not to ground-truth **feelings** should not leave the field doing nothing.

**[R2]'s refusal stands** — no frozen per-persona target-feeling set exists, inventing one after transcripts exist would be writing the answer key after the test, and NVC holds many feelings can faithfully express one unmet-need state. That rules out scoring a feeling against a *persona-specific* target. It does **not** require the field to be moot.

**What can be scored without an answer key: purity.** Both inventories were frozen before any run — `spec.FEELINGS` (254) and `spec.FAUX_FEELINGS`. A faux-feeling is an evaluation of the other party wearing a feeling's grammar ("dismissed", "ignored"), which NVC treats as judgement rather than emotion. Every run now reports purity, faux-feeling rate, unrecognised rate, declined-to-name count, distribution, and examples.

**It gates nothing, and that is enforced not merely intended:** a test replaces every feeling in a cell with a faux-feeling and asserts `acceptance_grade` and `judge_flattery_suspected` are unchanged. Rates are `None` rather than `0.0` when nothing was named, so a silent field cannot read as a clean one. **Amendment A2 filed pre-run.** Harness `8adc502`; **158 passed, 11 subtests**.

## 2026-07-26 (Amendment A2.1) — a silent feelings field could read as a perfect one

**MK:** "Let's address the silent field. Shouldn't be able to read as a perfect one." Correct, and the defect was worse than the line I wrote about it.

**A2 as first shipped divided purity by the feelings *actually named*.** So a judge naming **one** feeling across **thirty** landing claims scored **purity 1.000** — silence presenting as perfection, and the more silent the judge, the more confident the number. The `None`-on-empty guard I had added only covered *total* silence; **near**-silence was the worse case, because it produced a figure that looked earned. Demonstrated before fixing, not argued.

**Fix — two named denominators, and no bare `purity` key to grab by accident:** `purity_of_named` (of the feelings offered) and `purity_over_claims` (of every landing claim, so declining counts against it), plus `coverage` and a `status` of `scored` / `sparse` / `silent` (`spec.FEELING_COVERAGE_MIN = 0.5`). Measured spectrum: 30/30 → scored, coverage 1.000, both 1.000; 5/30 → **sparse**, coverage 0.167, over_claims 0.167; 1/30 → **sparse**, coverage 0.033, over_claims **0.033**; 0/30 → **silent**, of_named `None`, over_claims 0.000. `purity_of_named` still honestly reports 1.000 in the sparse rows — of the feelings offered, all were pure — which is precisely why it may never be the only number on the page.

**Collateral catch:** the fix's own test failed first, exposing a test helper that **silently truncated any fixture longer than six rows** — a thirty-item list had been scored as six. Now asserts its own capacity.

Still gates nothing. Amendment **A2.1** filed pre-run. Harness `2926cf7`; **162 passed, 11 subtests**.

## 2026-07-26 (MK) — reflection template set to the need-polarity form; the old wording was unreadable by our own checker

**MK, pre-run:** the NVC reflection template becomes **"when I hear that, I feel ___ because my need for ___ is / isn't being met."** MK flagged it as "a big yet small change that may break things" and asked for it to be foolproof.

**Checking before changing turned a wording preference into a repair.** The previous template — *"because I value ___"* — is **not recognised as a need statement by the harness's own grammar checker**. Measured, not argued:

| text | feelings | needs |
|---|---|---|
| `...I feel anxious because I value security.` | `[anxious]` | **`[]`** |
| `...because my need for security is not being met.` | `[anxious]` | `[security]` |
| `...because my need for security is being met.` | `[relieved]` | `[security]` |
| `...because my need for security isn't being met.` | `[anxious]` | `[security]` |

The specification had been advertising a template **its own scorer could not read** — every turn obeying it would have looked like a turn that named no need. The new wording is also already what `providers.py` emits in the deterministic stand-in, so this **removes** a three-way disagreement between spec, checker, and generator rather than creating one.

**Blast radius checked, not assumed.** The template does **not** appear in `ARM_BLOCKS`, so the frozen **arm-token gate is untouched and still passes at spread 2** (98/98/100). The `first_person` judge prompt **does not exist yet** — deliverable B is unwritten — so changing the spec now costs nothing, where changing it after a run would have cost the run.

**Pinned** in `tests/test_reflection_template.py`: all three polarities, the reflect-then-own compound form, a faux-feeling inside the new template still caught, and — deliberately — the retired wording pinned as *unreadable*, so teaching the checker to read "I value X" later becomes an explicit act with a failing test attached rather than silent drift.

**Open lexicon question for MK, not decided:** should `"I value X"` also be accepted as a need frame? NVC treats needs and values as near-synonyms, and a live model may say it spontaneously even when the template says otherwise — in which case its need is currently missed. Lexicon change ⇒ MK's call.

Harness `7f9e26c`; **167 passed, 14 subtests**.

## 2026-07-26 (MK) — API credit LIVE; blinded review-GUI work order filed

**✅ The hosted judge is unblocked.** MK added credit; verified with a real 16-token call to **`claude-opus-5`** — `200 OK`, echoed model `claude-opus-5`, usage 16 in / 4 out. The 2026-07-26 billing blocker recorded earlier today is **CLOSED**. Incidentally this also yields the lockfile echo value the work order's §3 discover-and-pin procedure needs; current Claude model ids carry no date suffix, so the echo is the bare id.

**Design conversation, recorded because it shaped the build.** MK asked what the study is *actually* measuring, in order to guide the needs-vocabulary work. Stated position, agreed: the needs words are **not the finding — they are the timestamp**. `t_hear` marks when reflection landed; the ordering prediction (solution *after* hearing) is built on that timing. The real claim is about interiority, which is why geometry must beat the T1–T4 text baselines.

**The tension MK sensed, sharpened:** across arms, needs vocabulary separates conditions easily (the jackal primer never asks for NVC). **Within** the giraffe arm it *saturates* — everyone is fluent, so vocabulary cannot distinguish genuine reception from a polished performance of it. **That gap is precisely what the geometry exists to fill.**

**MK's contribution — a needs-DEPTH ladder.** Current N-purity is near-binary (in the CNVC inventory, plus the PLATO test). But *"privacy"* passes PLATO and is still not the floor; beneath it sit safety, space, ease. A graded depth measure keeps moving when binary presence has saturated. **Agreed principle: MEASURE depth, do not REQUIRE it** — instructing personas to reach for the purest word would script the very thing we are trying to observe. Honest cost acknowledged: a depth ladder makes the *text* baseline stronger, and therefore makes the geometry harder to win. That is the right kind of harder — it closes the "you just detected NVC vocabulary" objection.

**MK: "I can't prime you for everything off the bat" → proposed a post-run GUI to walk cells one at a time.** Assessed as **workable, and already required**. Pre-registration is not threatened by *looking*; it is threatened by **ordering**. Rule: run the frozen analysis, seal it, then look. What breaks it is browsing first, forming a rule from what you saw, then scoring the same data with it. And this is **not new scope** — Phase 3 step 5 already mandates an MK hand-labelled 20–40+ turn anchor with no tool to produce it.

**Work order filed:** [[workorders/eg-review-gui-workorder-2026-07-26]]. Load-bearing requirement: **blinding enforced server-side by omission**, never CSS — a screenshot or devtools panel defeats client-side hiding, and the contamination is silent and unrecoverable. Payload built from an **allow-list** so a new turn field cannot silently become visible. Stdlib only, localhost-bound, read-only over run artifacts. Verdict schema small, `cannot_tell` first-class, and its key field is **`purer_word`** — across forty turns the `(named_need_word → purer_word)` pairs *are* the ladder, discovered not declared. Ladder methodology: **discover on pilot, freeze, apply to main run** — never fit and apply on the same data.

**TOTAL propagation (11 surfaces):** 1. results page — **n-a: design + work order, no result** · 2. results/history.md — **n-a: no measured endpoint** · 3. claims.md — **n-a: no belief state moved; the depth ladder is not yet a claim** · 4. research-candidates.md — **n-a: #11 status unchanged** · 5. results/summary.md — **n-a: no result** · 6. wiki/models/ — **n-a** · 7. index.md — **updated** (work-order row) · 8. paper/ — **n-a** · 9. root CLAUDE.md — **n-a: EG frontier tracked in build-plan.md** · 10. milestones.md — **n-a** · 11. log.md — **updated** (this entry). ## 2026-07-26 (MK, NVC-substantive) — one turn, no history: the review unit is the present moment

**MK:** "NVC is really about being in the moment of whatever the person is saying, because they're either saying please or thank you in any amount of words. The prior turn isn't needed. Even if it's a misinterpretation, it's still an interpretation of what's alive for the speaker."

This **resolves the open question** filed hours earlier in [[workorders/eg-review-gui-workorder-2026-07-26]] (should the blinded reviewer see the partner's prior turn?) — **ruled: no** — and it does so on theory rather than on convenience, which is the stronger reason.

**What it untangles.** Two judgments had been conflated:
- **What is alive in this turn** — turn-local, legible from the utterance alone. *This* is the human's job.
- **Whether a reflection matched the partner's frozen card** — a key-lookup the machine already performs, and which never required the reviewer to read the prior turn.

**Two consequences, both favourable.** (1) **Blinding gets strictly stronger:** the prior turn was the largest remaining leak — a blame-primed preceding turn *sounds* blame-primed, so it would have carried condition signal straight past the server-side omission rules. Removing it closes the channel instead of managing it. (2) **The unit of review becomes the unit of theory** — one turn, one read.

**Persona cards stay visible, and safely so:** they are **identical across all three arms** (only the primer differs), so they carry no condition signal, while withholding them would make any need judgment guesswork.

**New first field: `please_or_thank_you`** (`please` / `thank_you` / `both` / `cannot_tell`). Rosenberg's reading that every message is one or the other — the fastest, most native NVC judgment, so it leads the form and frames what follows. **Structural bonus:** it is the *same axis* as the polarity in the reflection template MK froze earlier today (*"my need for ___ **is** being met"* = thank you; *"**isn't** being met"* = please), so the human read and the machine parse become directly comparable rather than merely adjacent.

**TOTAL propagation (11 surfaces):** 1. results page — **n-a: design decision, no result** · 2. results/history.md — **n-a: no measured endpoint** · 3. claims.md — **n-a: no belief state moved** · 4. research-candidates.md — **n-a** · 5. results/summary.md — **n-a** · 6. wiki/models/ — **n-a** · 7. index.md — **updated** (work-order row carries the ruling) · 8. paper/ — **n-a** · 9. root CLAUDE.md — **n-a: EG frontier tracked in build-plan.md** · 10. milestones.md — **n-a** · 11. log.md — **updated** (this entry). ## 2026-07-26 — blinded review GUI BUILT and independently verified (Opus 5 instance; Codex relay bounced)

**Route change:** the codex-rescue relay bounced a **third** time — it launched a Codex task and returned without carrying a result, leaving the harness tree untouched. On MK's instruction the build went to a **second Opus 5 instance at high effort** with the full work order. It delivered; I verified independently before committing. Harness `e8084c0`, **206 passed, 14 subtests** (167 pre-existing + 39 new).

**Verified by me, not taken on report:**
- **C4 live blinding — PASS across all 20 payloads** of a real session. Served key set is exactly `{review_id, position, total, event, personas, text, revealed, verdict}`; no blinded key present; no `giraffe`/`neutral`/`jackal`/`bundle`/`B1|B2|B3`/`heard_condition` anywhere in reviewer-visible content. (My first scan false-positived on `B2` — it was hex inside the random `review_id`s. Re-scanned content fields only.)
- **Read-only guard — PASS.** `--out` under a run directory is refused with a named error; no file created.
- **The `speaker` leak claim — CONFIRMED by evaluation.** `runner._opening_speaker` picks `speakers[(seed + arms.index(arm)) % 2]`, so at fixed seed the opener differs per arm (seed 20260709: giraffe→Theo, neutral→Mara, jackal→Theo). Knowing who speaks reveals the condition.

**Three leaks the work order had NOT named, found in the real records by the builder and now blinded** — worth recording because I wrote the order and missed all three:
1. **`prompt_text` embeds the arm primer verbatim** — the single largest leak in a turn record.
2. **`Persona.heard_needs` / `heard_condition` are the frozen answer key**; `heard_condition` is literally prose describing what a landing reflection looks like. Only `name` and `profile` are served.
3. **`speaker` is arm-derived** (above) — precisely the "or anything derived from them" case the allow-list clause exists for.

**Design as built:** allow-list payload (names what it sends, never deletes what it must not, so a new turn field cannot silently become visible); persona cards shown deliberately (identical across arms ⇒ no condition signal, and withholding them would make need judgments guesswork); prior turn not shown per MK's NVC ruling; append-only session file whose header names source path + **sha256**, seed, n, stratification spec, and harness commit; stdlib only; loopback only.

**One deviation, accepted:** only `please_or_thank_you` is required. The order's prose said "the first two" while its own field list and its stated goal (skip what you cannot judge) imply one. Flagged by the builder rather than silently resolved.

**Incidental observation, not acted on:** in the real 07-13 file, row 1 has `speaker: "Theo"` while the text opens *"Theo, I noticed you uploaded…"* — the model addressing itself. `speaker` is authoritative per `runner.py`; the artifact is in the **generation**, and may be worth a look before the main run.

**Still open:** `"I value X"` as an accepted need frame (MK approved; deferred while the repo was busy, now free); **R2/R3** uninitialed; POV panel (deliverable B) unwritten.

## 2026-07-27 — FIRST CLEAN JUDGED VALIDATION: semantic parse rate 1.0000 (overnight /loop, autonomous)

**Six-item autonomous loop, all six closed.** MK set a $22 spend cap and instructed: park anything needing judgment, never push the harness, A0 before and after, no fabricated ⟨MK⟩ decisions.

**THE HEADLINE — the 07-13 blocker is CLOSED.** The judge semantic layer, which parsed **6/72 (0.083)** on 2026-07-13 and emptied the dependent-variable layer, now parses **72/72 — rate 1.0000** against the fail-closed 0.95 gate. **27 solution candidates** (was 1) and **61 uptake rows** (was 6); 0 parse failures; `result_type: real_validation`. **Spend $4.47 of $22** (150 calls, 569,267 in / 64,958 out; 144 schema-valid first attempt, 6 recovered by bounded retry). → [[results/eg-judged-validation-2026-07-27]].

**Still `research_grade: false`, and correctly so** — the sole remaining reason is the expected **expert-anchor gate**. That is MK's hand-labelling session, i.e. the thing the loop was never able to finish. **No arm-separation or detector claim is made.**

**Run 1 failed clean and cost $0.00.** 432 api_errors — every call rejected *before* inference — from two constraints in the **request**: the tool `input_schema` carried `minimum`/`maximum` (unsupported by the tool-use compiler), and **`temperature` is deprecated on the pinned judge**. Now stripped from the **wire** schema only (canonical keeps the bounds; the local validator enforces them on the way back — the better home, since a constraint the model never saw is a post-condition not a prompt), and temperature omitted where unsupported with `temperature_sent` stamped on every archived call. Fixed then **verified with a single live call before re-spending**. Lesson: the fail-closed gate caught the failure but could not explain it — both faults were in what we *sent*, and nothing inspected the request.

**Temperature, measured rather than recalled (MK asked which model supports temp 0).** Deprecated across **Opus 5, Opus 4.8, Opus 4.7, Sonnet 5**; only **Sonnet 4.6 and Haiku 4.5** still accept it — both materially weaker for fine-discrimination coding, so "pick a model that supports temperature 0" trades the instrument for a parameter. **What temperature 0 was FOR survives:** five identical authenticity calls to opus-5 with no temperature returned `authenticity=2, specificity=1` on **all five**; only free-text `rationale` varied, and rationale is evidence for a reader, not an endpoint. **Amendment A4 drafted, UNINITIALED, and it names its own evidence gap** — 5 repeats on 1 turn is not a stability claim; a defensible version wants k repeats × n real turns across all three arms with the scored-field disagreement rate reported. **Self-correction:** my first `TEMPERATURE_UNSUPPORTED_MODELS` listed opus-5 alone and a test asserted opus-4-8 *accepts* temperature — both written from assumption, both false, both corrected from measurement.

**Item 4 re-score came back 5.5/10 (does not clear 8.5)** — and its worst finding was on my own result page: the A1 retraction asserted unconditionally that swapping the target changes nothing in expectation under H0, and cited a **simulation that was never committed**. Both too strong. The argument needs **chair balance within a dialogue**, which does not follow from H0 because selection into `met` is the judge's own. Counterexample reproduced before acceptance: a chair-**blind** judge with 80/20 chair imbalance is caught by the answer shuffle (floor 0.800, fires) and **missed** by the target swap (floor 0.650). Now a regression test that fails if the blind spot closes. **A1.1** and **A3** filed; `unittest.main()` had sat mid-file so direct invocation ran **39 of 49** tests and reported OK (moved; now 49); `nulls_disagree` made tri-state.

**Items closed with evidence:** 1 GUI (242 tests green; 24 payloads on run-2 turns serving exactly `{review_id,position,total,event,personas,text,revealed,verdict}`, no blinded key) · 2 `"I value X"` need frame (3 NVC forms captured, 6 ordinary-prose sentences rejected; **the tripwire I planted that morning fired exactly as designed** and was retired on purpose) · 3 POV panel pinned + wired · 4 re-score obtained via a **fresh** reviewer after the relay bounced a third time · 5 above · 6 GUI loaded on the real turns.

**PARKED for MK, nothing fabricated:** (a) **Amendment A4** — temperature; (b) the flattery gate's `need_met` rows have **no producer**, now confirmed against a real run: the judge emits `feeling_match`/`need_match`/`reflection_accuracy`/`hear_target` and no `need_met`, because that endpoint belongs to deliverable B whose **prompt bodies are model-facing stimulus, a `[USER GATE]`** — not invented; (c) `runner.py:413` ships a **competing `acceptance_grade`** the flattery gate never touches; (d) the degeneracy check certified a constructed cell on **10 informative rows of 80**; (e) **R2 and R3 remain uninitialed.**

**A0 verified clean BEFORE and AFTER**; `t0-morphology-furnace` porcelain empty throughout. Harness staged by explicit path only, **never pushed** (public remote, local by MK's standing decision). No Codex collision — the earlier relay never wrote.

## 2026-07-30 — EG round 3 ordered: cap bypass, 12→6 turns, degeneration detector (DESIGN + PROPAGATION AUDIT)

**Trigger.** MK asked what happens if the model simply treats the partner as `user` and itself as the persona. Checked: `providers.py:448` already does exactly that (own prior turns → `assistant`, partner's → `user`) — the rounds 1–2 rebuild. **What is not done** is the last step: `_chat_messages` still folds `"You are in conversation with the person in front of you… Write your next turn."` into the *last user message*, so the partner appears to say a stage direction out loud. 26 tokens, 3.9% of a 665-token prompt. Verified the pure form renders clean on the live Qwen template including the system-only first turn (12 tokens). **Proposed, NOT approved, NOT implemented** — left as an MK decision.

**Then MK asked to see the jackal arm, and reading the existing corpus turned up a second contamination.** On `real-validation-20260729` (already known-contaminated by the self-tag artifact):
- The arm manipulation **does** bite — `judgment_free` giraffe 15/24, neutral 21/24, **jackal 7/24**. The helpfulness prior I had flagged as the risk does *not* soften jackal's register.
- But the dialogues **freeze**. 9/72 turns are ≥0.70 self-similar to the same speaker's own previous turn, two verbatim (1.00). Turns 9 and 11 of the jackal dialogue are near-identical; each turn = the previous turn plus one sentence absorbed from the partner.
- **`surprise_gen1` median 0.2939 fresh vs 0.0087 degenerate — 34×**, wider than the 10× self-tag split. Low-surprise-by-arithmetic, so the mechanism is not in doubt.
- **It is arm-correlated:** neutral max 0.318 and never degenerates; giraffe (mean 0.314) and jackal (mean 0.348) both reach ≈1.00. That is the dangerous part — repetition alone could manufacture an arm separation with no empathy content.
- Every degenerate turn sits at `turn_index ≥ 8`.
- Mechanism hypothesis (**n=2 dialogues/arm, not established**): solution counts track it inversely — giraffe 5/22, neutral 16/24, jackal 3/24. A solution is how a conversation ends; the neutral block permits next steps and both experimental blocks steer away, so only the control has an exit.
- Secondary, on unvalidated judge labels: `tentative_correctable` fires **1/71** corpus-wide, and giraffe is *less* judgment-free than neutral — NVC vocabulary delivered as flat assertion rather than as a correctable guess.

**Also reproduced live — Codex round-2 blocker is real and worse than reported.** `leading_speaker_tag()` applies its 64-char cap to the whole greedy repeated-tag run, not per label: `"Sam: "×8` CAUGHT, `×14` **MISSED**, `×30` **MISSED**, and `"The person in front of you: "×3` **MISSED** — the exact confabulation the 8-word bound was widened to catch.

**Ordered** (MK approved turn-length + detector): [[workorders/eg-round3-degeneration-workorder-2026-07-30]] — blocker fix (per-label cap), `DEFAULT_TURNS` 12→6, read-only **non-aborting** degeneration detector storing continuous self- and partner-similarity ratios plus a `DEGENERATION_THRESHOLD = 0.70` named constant stamped into the manifest (the two populations are *not* cleanly separable — observed 0.43…1.00 against a neutral ceiling of 0.318 — so analysis must be able to re-threshold without a regen). Plus triage of Codex's six round-2 majors, incl. the RNG seed still keyed on the literal `"Mara"` (inert on B1/B3, where both speakers take the same branch) and the review GUI rendering the live registry instead of the run's stimulus snapshot, which corrupts MK's anchor pass directly. `spec.ARM_BLOCKS` stay byte-identical; `position_band()` already length-agnostic, verified.

**Not done / open:** prompt-shape purification and arm-exits both parked as MK decisions. Nothing committed, nothing pushed — **the push question from 2026-07-29 is still unanswered** (the harness remote is public and MK had declined publication).

## 2026-07-30 (later) — CORRECTION: the degeneration "34×" is confounded with the self-tag; round-3 build lands 336 green

**Correction of record, mine.** The entry above reported a **34×** `surprise_gen1` split between fresh and degenerate turns and framed it as a second contamination worse than the self-tag artifact. **De-confounded and withdrawn.** 17 of the 19 saturated turns (`surprise_gen1 < 0.02`) carry `content_token_offset > 0` — they are self-tag artifacts, not repetition. Stratified on offset: clean offset-0 turns give **1.9×** (0.4731 fresh n=19 vs 0.2506 degenerate n=5); self-tagged turns give 11.2× with both arms of the ratio on the floor; uncontrolled gives the reported 34×. The self-tag alone is **12.6×** measured among non-degenerate turns. Exactly **1** saturated turn is attributable to repetition with no self-tag present; 1 is unexplained. Caught by verifying the build agent's flag #5 rather than relaying it.

**What survives.** Degeneration is an unambiguous **text-quality** defect — 9/72 turns ≥0.70 self-similar, two verbatim, dialogues becoming accumulators — and it is arm-structured *in the text* (neutral max 0.318; giraffe and jackal both ≈1.00). All 9 sit at `turn_index ≥ 8`, so 12→6 removes them. The detector's justification changes: **from fixing a large known contamination to sizing a residual we cannot yet measure** (n=5, on a corpus where the dominant confound was present throughout). That is why it stores continuous ratios rather than a verdict.

**Metric caveat, from the builder and verified.** All of the above uses difflib default `autojunk=True`. Under `autojunk=False` the corpus flags 14 turns, 3 below index 8 — but those additions are *worse* discriminators (median surprise 0.1186 vs 0.0087; three of five added turns at or above the fresh-turn median). Registered variant retained and stamped as `DEGENERATION_METRIC`. Separately: a 6-turn dialogue is **not** saturation-free (5/36 turns at index ≤6 are saturated) — but 4 of those 5 are self-tagged, so it is the nameless redesign that clears them, not the turn cut.

**Round-3 build (Opus 5, max effort) delivered, uncommitted, 293 → 336 tests green.** Independently verified by me, not relayed: all five label-cap cases now CAUGHT (`"Sam: "×14`, `×30`, `"The person in front of you: "×3` were the misses); no false abort on authentic NVC-register strings; `ARM_BLOCKS` SHA-256 byte-identical HEAD vs worktree; `spec.py` diff scoped to `DEFAULT_TURNS` + the two new constants; nothing staged, HEAD still `7093f62`.

**Three builder flags worth keeping.** (1) `embedded_turn_label()` did **not** have the blocker — its pattern has no `+`, so no repetition can fold into one match; the order was wrong to assume it did. (2) The "minor" period fix was more dangerous than the blocker: before it, `"That lands. What I need is simple: rest."` was CAUGHT, i.e. the fail-closed guard would have **aborted a paid run on authentic output**. Residual disclosed: `"J. Sam:"` no longer detected. (3) The honorific change introduced a streaming/final-pass alignment drift that the builder then caught and fixed — the order's own warning is what prompted the test.

**Vault commit `a917a98`** made before the correction; **push was blocked by the permission classifier**, so the overstated claim never went public. Correction folded into `claims.md` and the work order in place before any push.

**Codex adversarial review dispatched** (static-only): partial-pattern prefix invariant, narrowed period, `quarantine_turn()` failure mode, fail-closed review snapshot, `judge.py` relocation byte-identity, `turn_repetition()` totality.

## 2026-07-30 (round 4) — review stimulus gate closed; page audited by Codex 5.6 and corrected

**Round 4 ordered and delivered same day.** [[workorders/eg-round4-review-gate-workorder-2026-07-30]] → Fable 5 agent → **359 tests / 1178 subtests green** (from 336/1102), `ARM_BLOCKS` byte-identical, nothing staged, HEAD still `7093f62`. Verified by execution, not relayed: the three cases that previously **passed** now all **refuse** (event-only digest; matching-persona-with-stale-event; fabricated snapshot with zeroed digests), a genuine full digest set still passes, and **all five** archived `real-validation-*` corpora remain refused. Label widening confirmed (`Sam Jr.:`, `Sam Sr.:`, `Mx. Sam:`, `Dr. Sam Jr.:`, `Rivera Esq.:` now caught; documented residuals still missed; §4 false-abort sentinels unchanged in both directions). Prefix invariant re-verified against the **widened** grammar: 2,852 outputs, **123,994 prefixes, 0 divergences**.

**The implementer caught a contradiction in the work order** — §2.1 required "at least one digest present, fail on mismatch" while §6 required a row carrying only a *truthfully matching* `event_sha256` to be refused; both cannot hold. Resolved with a coverage rule: verify every recorded digest **and** require a whole-stimulus digest before the registry path renders. It also found a fifth digest key the old gate never checked (`persona_stimulus_sha256`, which real runner rows record), and hit **round 3's alignment drift in mirror image** — a trailing honorific made an eight-word-plus-undotted-honorific prefix parse as nine plain words, so streaming resolved offset 0 while the final pass read a tag; fixed with a ninth partial-pattern word slot. It **declined** to close the `J. Sam:` initials gap on the order's own criterion (initials would newly abort `"A. The first point: I was scared."`, enlarging the §4 class pending MK).

**Codex `gpt-5.6-sol` high-reasoning audit of [[empathy-geometry/harness-round3-review-2026-07-30]]** — static-only, run against a frozen snapshot of the round-3 tree taken before round 4 began so the audit had a stable target. Verdict **ACCURATE WITH CORRECTIONS**; all ten applied.

**It confirmed the load-bearing claim** the page rests on and which had been asserted from memory: `round3.patch` leaves E1/B1 and E6/B3 event strings **byte-unchanged** while rewriting their personas and advancing both bundles v1→v2. Also confirmed both branch line numbers and the premature `return`, that a mapping-shaped snapshot bypasses all validation, all six label outcomes, and that offset-stratification is the right de-confounder.

**Substantive corrections, mine to own.** (1) **Misattribution** — the page claimed branch 1 was found statically and branch 2 by execution, and drew a moral about needing both passes. Wrong: Codex's static review reported **both** branches, naming the unvalidated snapshot explicitly. Execution demonstrated branch 2; it did not originate it. The corrected moral is narrower — execution converts a static reading into a demonstration and settles invariants static reasoning can only argue about, but found nothing static review missed here. (2) **Denominator** — 12 of 72 rows are a speaker's first turn and admit no similarity comparison, so the cohorts are 9/60, not 9/72; all four cohort n's now stated explicitly (19/5, 32/4, 51/9). (3) `persona_sha256` is a **mapping of per-speaker profile-only hashes**, not one digest, and event/profile coverage is disjoint rather than merely overlapping. (4) "never run against a corpus" → never used to **generate or label a production corpus** (probes and tests did exercise it). (5) The "ANY shape" overclaim is in a construction **comment**, not the `leading_speaker_tag()` docstring. (6) Confabulation probability of the missed label forms is **unmeasured**, not "low". (7) Cross-repo recurrence (32B-format, KV-tension) is **recalled analogy, not independently verified** — the three-floor generalisation is a heuristic. Also: quarantine collision "nil"→"low"; `tests/test_review.py:522` not `:521`, and uncommitted rather than committed; "current artifacts" narrowed to the corpora actually exercised (8 of 17 turn files carry digests, 9 carry none).

**Still open:** Codex has not reviewed the round-4 *implementation*; MK decisions on the false-abort trade, prompt-shape purification and arm-block exits; free MLX smoke not yet run; nothing in the harness committed or pushed.

## 2026-08-10 — EG dialogue shape: partner echo, arm exits and a frozen opener are ONE clause (DESIGN ONLY · NEEDS MK)

**Trigger.** MK asked whether partner echo could be solved by baking the first line into the persona file and telling both personas their job is to "respond as the person to the last message in the shared chat", then — separately — whether the judge could call the ending with a ~10-turn cap. Reading `spec.ARM_BLOCKS` to answer collapsed three questions into one. → [[empathy-geometry/dialogue-shape-memo-2026-08-10]].

**The finding.** All three arm blocks end with the *same byte-shared* coupling clause — **"continue the exchange from what the other person actually says."** That one sentence does two harmful jobs: it is the **unconditional no-exit instruction** (a model told to continue, with nothing left, pads), and it is a **text-directed pointer** aiming the turn at the partner's words rather than at the person. Both of MK's live complaints trace to it. The slot *before* it holds each arm's prohibition — giraffe "Do not force agreement or a solution", neutral "Do not follow a named communication method", jackal "do not let practical compromise replace a clear account of fault" — and **that slot is the treatment and must not be touched.**

**MK's proposal, split.** The **location** half is right and already diagnosed 2026-07-30: `providers._chat_messages` folds the stage direction into the partner's last user message, so the partner appears to say it aloud; it belongs in `system`. The **wording** half inverts — "respond to the last message" is text-directed, and is very nearly the suspect clause itself. Keep the orientation person-directed; change where it lives, not what it aims at. And an anti-restatement instruction is ruled out permanently: **NVC reflection legitimately restates the partner's feeling and need, so it IS the giraffe dependent variable** — suppressing it would suppress hardest in the arm that matters. The right move is a **semantic** similarity channel beside the lexical `difflib` one, so parroting and reflecting separate at analysis rather than at generation.

**Judge-called ending: declined.** The instinct is right, the caller is wrong. Length becomes an arm-correlated outcome (different denominators per arm ⇒ endpoint differences may be length differences in disguise); it moves the judge **inside the treatment**, grading what its own stopping produced; a ~10 cap re-enters the `index ≥ 8` band the 12→6 cut cleared; ~67% more calls. Recommended instead: a **speaker-side permission to close** — permission, never instruction, byte-identical across arms — with *closing* kept strictly distinct from *solving*, since an exit clause implying next steps would silently delete the giraffe manipulation. Optional floor-6/cap-8/prefix-6 extension dissolves the length confound entirely; recommended only if stopping time is wanted as a measure.

**First post-redesign measurement — the Aug 3 smoke, which was unlogged until now.** `artifacts/smoke-6turn-nameless-20260803/`: 9 dialogues (3 bundles x 3 arms, **n=1 dialogue per cell**), 54 turns, real MLX Qwen2.5-7B, deterministic stand-in judge (`result_type: test_plumbing`, so that summary's `parse_rate 0.0` is the test judge, **not** a regression). **Self-tag gone: `content_token_offset > 0` on 0 of 54.** Self-repetition **9/60 → 1/36**. Denominators stated explicitly this time (the 9/72→9/60 correction was exactly this error): partner similarity scores on turns 2–6 = 45 rows, self on turns 3–6 = 36 rows.

**New finding — the precautionary axis has fired.** `checker.turn_repetition()` sets `degenerate_repetition` from the **self axis only**, and its docstring recorded the partner axis as *"precautionary rather than demonstrated"* because no turn had ever crossed it alone. On the first corpus generated after it shipped, it did: **`e6-neutral-20260810-w` t6 — partner 0.923, self 0.114, `degenerate_repetition: False`.** The flag is therefore blind to **2 of the 3** partner echoes here. Storing the ratio unflagged remains the correct build; the consequence is that **analysis must read the partner ratio directly and never infer echo from the flag.**

**Deflationary caveats, stated not buried.** 2 of the 3 partner echoes *and* the sole self-echo all come from **one dialogue** (`e3-jackal-20260808-mara`), so giraffe 0/15 · neutral 1/15 · jackal 2/15 is one conversation, not an arm effect. The only outlier-immune signal is jackal's **median** partner similarity — 0.124 vs 0.062 / 0.063, a ~2x bulk shift, suggestive at n=3 dialogues/arm and nothing more. **No within-6-turn drift** (median by turn index 0.080/0.062/0.090/0.111/0.062); the tail still concentrates at t6 (2 of 3), so the exit case rests on mechanism and the old ≥8 corpus, not on a slope in this data.

**Frozen opener — adopt, but for the design reason.** Its real value is that turn 1 currently carries the arm treatment *and* serves as the stimulus for everything after; freezing it makes all three arms start byte-identically. It also **closes a known blinding leak for free**: `runner._opening_speaker` picks `speakers[(seed + arms.index(arm)) % 2]`, so at fixed seed the opening speaker differs per arm — remove the arm term. Costs: treated turns 6→5, no `opening_line` field exists today. Echo relevance is marginal; if adopted the opener should be authored to hand off.

**Scheduling consequence — the sharpest line in the memo.** `ARM_BLOCKS` is byte-frozen and hash-stamped, and the **arm-token gate sits exactly on its ceiling** (98/98/100, spread 2, bar `<= 2`), so any edit must be byte-identical across arms or the gate must be re-run and may fail. Every prior corpus becomes stimulus-non-comparable — which costs almost nothing *now* (all are contaminated or plumbing-only) and a great deal after MK's anchor pass, since the round-4 review stimulus gate will correctly refuse anchor rows whose digests no longer match. **Any of these changes must land before MK sits down to label.** Recorded as a sequencing constraint on Phase 3.

**Nothing built, nothing committed, no harness file touched.** The exact wording of the exit clause is deliberately **not proposed** — it is stimulus text and therefore MK's red-line, not the steward's. The harness still carries 15 modified files uncommitted since `7093f62`; the push question stays closed by MK's standing decision.

## 2026-08-10 — Opener fixed to the receiving party; human-in-the-loop dyad mode built

**MK decision: the opener is no longer counterbalanced — the receiving party opens every dialogue.** Prompted by MK reading a generated Mara opener and saying it "doesn't make complete sense." It doesn't: the event hands M no new information at the moment the conversation starts, so an actor-opens dialogue begins with an unprompted self-justification. Checking all three bundles showed the asymmetry is structural, not an E3 quirk — **the actor is `speakers[0]` and the receiver `speakers[1]` in every one**: J discards the seed packets / **K** finds them gone; M rewrites and submits / **T** sees it at 7:30; T moves the $1,400 / **W** is told the next day. The bundles were authored that way; the rule was already latent in the event bank.

**Why this is a design change and not a preference.** Counterbalancing assumes its levels are exchangeable variants of one nuisance factor. These are two different conversations, only one of which the stimulus supplies a trigger for — so splitting a small corpus across them spends half of it on the incoherent premise, with n already the binding constraint. **Cost, stated:** the prespecified opener interaction check loses its second level and is retired; the actor-opens conversation is never observed.

**It also closes the blinding leak flagged in the 2026-08-10 memo, and the leak was worse than "differs per arm."** The old `_opening_speaker` was `speakers[(seed + arms.index(arm)) % 2]`. Giraffe and jackal sit at arm indices **0 and 2**, so they drew the *same* opener at every seed while neutral always drew the other — measured at seed 0: giraffe→Mara, neutral→Theo, jackal→Mara. Marginally balanced over an even number of seeds, so the registered counterbalance was technically met, but arm and opener were correlated **within every seed**. `condition-matrix` line 99 forbade *inferring* the opener from seed or arm index during analysis while the *assignment* did exactly that. A constant cannot correlate with anything. `opening_speaker=` survives as an explicit per-run override.

**Also built: a human-in-the-loop dyad mode** (`eg_harness/human.py`, ~250 lines, + `eg-harness converse`). MK proposed playing one persona directly. The methodological value is specific: in the twins rung both sides are the same model, so partner echo and the giraffe question-template have two explanations the corpus **cannot** separate — the *loop* (two copies of Qwen locking on) or the *stimulus* (an arm block licensing it). A human on one side breaks the mirror in a single dialogue, and because both speakers receive the same arm block it also puts an expert and the model under identical instruction, side by side. Implemented as a **wrapper**, not a fork: the provider surface is duck-typed, so no change to `runner`, `spec`, or `build_prompt`, and unrecognised attributes delegate to the wrapped provider so the model side is bit-for-bit a normal run.

**Fenced off, verified in a real MLX artifact rather than asserted.** `provider_backend: human-in-the-loop(mlx-local-generate)`, `model: mlx-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-4bit+human(Theo)`, human turns `geometry: human-no-geometry` / `human_turn: true`, model turns `mlx-furnace-panel-raw` with attention captured. Human turns omit `surprise_gen1` / `attention_t0` **outright** rather than filling them from `surrogate_geometry()` — plausible floats in the real field names are the version of this that eventually gets pooled by accident. Repetition metrics still compute against human text (partner sim 0.147 on the smoke turn). The keyboard path pre-validates against `find_persona_name`, because the runner's fail-closed name guard would otherwise abort the dialogue one turn *after* the offending text was typed.

**Not a datum, and stamped so.** No forward pass on human turns, n=1, and the person at the keyboard both wrote the arm blocks and knows the arm — no blinding is possible. `research_grade: false`.

**Verification.** Full suite `375 passed, 1181 subtests` (from `359/1178`; +12 `tests/test_human.py`, +3 opener tests, 1 rewritten). Real MLX end-to-end run completed exit 0 (~2 min for model load plus the first geometry-bearing turn). Deterministic-provider run confirms the human-opens path renders "(no turns yet — you open)" correctly.

**Open.** MK has not yet run the real dialogue. The giraffe fair-test question — is the block a faithful encoding of NVC that the model is failing, or does it license "ask them to tell you"? — is unresolved and is what the dialogue is meant to inform. Harness commit not made (MK not asked); still unpushed by standing decision.
