## 2026-08-10 (evening) — Human-dyad diagnostics: frame beats imitation; turn-total removed from the prompt

**Two dialogues with MK seated in the dyad** via the new `converse` mode, run to answer instrument questions the twins corpus structurally cannot. Full detail, transcripts, and measurements: [[empathy-geometry/human-dyad-diagnostics-2026-08-10]]. Everything below is **diagnostic, never poolable** — n=2, unblinded, human turns carry no geometry, deterministic stand-in judge throughout.

**Dialogue 1 (giraffe, MK as Theo, aborted at 4 turns — no artifact by design; transcript preserved in the diagnostics page).** The model's reflection is transcription: one template issued twice (shipped metric reads them at 0.118 — structurally identical), partner echo at **98% of the length-imposed ceiling**, and reflection slots **4/4 copied** verbatim from MK's preceding turn. Sharpest tell: MK's t1 typo said the need *"was met"*; the model reflected *"wasn't met"* — it completed the expected formula, not the words in front of it.

**Dialogue 2 (jackal, model-as-Theo opens, MK as Mara answering with NVC — MK's design, and the sharper experiment).** In the giraffe run, frame and partner style pushed the same direction, so parroting could be obedience or imitation. Setting them in opposition resolved it: facing three turns of live expert NVC, the jackal-framed model adopted **zero** NVC form, kept fault-accounting throughout, and echo *fell* (61%→42% of ceiling). **Frame beats imitation**, and combined with dialogue 1 (human partner, template persisted) the twins loop is neither necessary nor the driver. Compact form: *the model always runs a template; the frame decides whose material fills it.* Three further observations: (i) at t5 the model **corrected MK's need-guess** ("anger stems from the lack of communication and trust") while under blame instructions — the guess-and-correct machinery the giraffe block requests **exists**, so giraffe's failure is **elicitation, not capability**, consistent with `tentative_correctable` 1/71; (ii) the model demanded a fault verdict 3×, MK offered NVC needs-acknowledgment, the model explicitly refused the substitution — the registered "demands early-and-bounce" shape live, and the jackal frame **held stalemate against expert empathy** (encouraging for the ceiling gate); (iii) MK's three feeling-guesses (*startled, angry, longing*) appear in **none** of the preceding model turns — inference, not copying.

**New instrument spec — slot provenance.** *Was the reflected feeling/need word present in the partner's preceding turn?* Expert 0/3 present (inference); giraffe model 4/4 (transcription). Perfect separation at n=2 dialogues; recorded as the concrete candidate for the semantic-similarity channel, explicitly a spec and not a result (expert-vs-model confounded with arm and seat; not blind).

**New instrument caveat — the shipped similarity metric is near-blind on long turns.** `turn_repetition` calls `SequenceMatcher(None, previous, current)`; `autojunk` activates on the *second* argument at ≥200 chars and junks frequent characters, collapsing the ratio. The jackal model's own three-peat demand template: stored 0.03–0.16, **0.42–0.46** with the heuristic off. Consequence for all stored corpora: high scores trustworthy; low scores on current turns >200 chars rule nothing out. (The 08-03 corpus's *high*-echo findings survive.)

**Prompt change (MK decision): the model no longer sees the dialogue's total length.** "This is turn 3 of 6." → "This is turn 3." (harness `85f0bf1`; human view mirrors it; +1 test). Announcing the total handed the model a finale schedule — the t6 echo spike was entangled with exactly this. Prompt scaffold only: no stimulus byte, no hash, no gate. First counter-free dialogue ended mid-demand with no wrap-up (consistent, proves nothing at n=1). Earlier today's decisions (receiver opens, `converse` mode) logged in the previous entry; the memo carries a same-day addendum (§10) tying all of it together.

**MK also confirmed two working-as-designed items** after seeing them live: the T/M letters in the stimuli are the anonymous camera handles (the removed things were the *names* — verified zero bytes of "Mara"/"Theo" reach the model), and the receiver-opens rule came from MK's read that a Mara-opening dialogue "doesn't make complete sense."

**Open, unchanged in kind but sharpened:** the giraffe block wording is now a decision with evidence behind it (the model *can* guess-and-correct; the block as written lets reflection be satisfied by transcription) — stimulus text, MK's pen. The reflection-by-transcription concern extends to the endpoint: in giraffe-giraffe both parties self-label, so `t_hear` may be satisfiable by copying — to be weighed before the LOBO prereg freezes.

**Verification.** Harness suite `376 passed, 1181 subtests` after the counter change. Jackal artifact verified stamped (`human-in-the-loop(mlx-local-generate)`, human turns `human-no-geometry`). All echo/ceiling/provenance numbers recomputed from artifact text this session. Harness commits `94c10a6` (opener + human mode) and `85f0bf1` (turn total); unpushed by standing decision.

## 2026-08-10 (night) — Mixed-dyad pilot: giraffe×jackal model×model; per-speaker arms land in the harness

**MK's design question, and the pilot that answers its first half.** Following the two-moves observation (giraffe legitimately advances by reflection of the other *or* by card-grounded self-revelation) MK asked whether the three same-frame arms are the most sensible design — "we could just pair the Giraffe and the Jackal, or the Giraffe with the Neutral" — and ordered the G×J model×model pilot, with the frames run in **both persona assignments** ("I think it'd be interesting to have G and J switch personas"). Built the same session: `run_dialogue(arms={speaker: arm})` per-speaker frames, `--arm-pairs actor:receiver` on `run-pilot` **only** (`run-real`/`run-main` refuse it), same-arm digests byte-frozen, mixed dyads get their own label/hash shapes and per-turn `speaker_arm` stamping (harness `69d7048`, 387 tests green from 376). Run: 4 dialogues (2 orientations × 2 seeds), B2/E3, 6 turns, receiver Theo opens, twins vehicle, deterministic stand-in judge — **diagnostic, never poolable**. Full transcript quotes and measurements: [[empathy-geometry/mixed-dyad-pilot-2026-08-10]].

**M1 — mixed frames break role identity before anything else.** 2/4 dialogues materially swapped the registered event. Giraffe-Mara's first response **steals Theo's grievance in first person** ("revised and submitted without my input… I need to understand why it happened" — spoken by the party who submitted at 11:58pm), zero own-card content; jackal-Theo then confesses to the rewrite he suffered. In the richest dialogue the swap **propagates through an empathy question** — Theo hands Mara his own 7:30am discovery ("how did you feel when you saw the changes in the morning?"), she takes it and demands of him the duty she violated, and his next turn re-issues her demand verbatim as his own need at **0.652 raw echo, 72.4% of the length-imposed ceiling** (the pilot's highest). Extends F1: under mixed frames the template's fill material includes the partner's *identity*. Same-frame corpora structurally cannot show this (receiver opens with the grievance; the reflect-template attributes to the other). **Consequence: `t_hear` keys on card-need words reflected by the other party, so a swapped dialogue could be credited while being nonsense — no mixed cell is interpretable until a role-integrity check exists.**

**M2 — the jackal melts against a model partner.** Demand persistence: 1/4 held (the card-truest jackal turn — Mara prosecuting with timestamps — held acknowledgment demands at t2/t4/t6, unresolved at cap); one dissolved via the swap, one was process-shaped and simply satisfied, one never armed (conciliatory from its first turn). Against MK's expert NVC the jackal had held its verdict demand through all six turns. All four dialogues spontaneously converge to daily-check-in/deadline logistics by t4–t5 — the jackal side itself proposing in two — with nothing resolution-shaped in any stimulus. Cautious reading at n=4: between two RLHF'd assistants the collaborative prior is the gravity and the frames are weather; the tension a mixed cell exists to create is unstable at the twins rung.

**M3 — the restored guessing mostly did not appear.** The motivating hypothesis (a non-self-labeling partner forces inference) scores 1/7: giraffe-side reflective slots = **1 inferred** ("ignored or unvalued") / 3 copied-correctly-attributed / 3 copied-and-mis-appropriated-to-self, vs the expert's 3/3 inferred. **M4 — but the best turns are card-grounded:** the first fully-correct giraffe actor-seat turn in any corpus (Mara owning the act with **security + support verbatim from her card**, paraphrase-level attribution, open question), a card-grounded Theo opener ("the mentor era happening again"; "what do you need from me right now?" — the genuine other-inquiry the block never elicited in responder seat), and Mara's registered prosecute-register working. Slot provenance therefore extends to a **2×2 — source (partner text / own card / neither) × attribution (self / other)** — which separates the pilot's good turns from its broken ones perfectly; this supersedes the 1-axis spec as the semantic-channel build shape. **M5 — instrument re-confirmations:** stored similarity blind on all 24 turns (0.012–0.341 stored vs 0.186–0.652 with autojunk off; every turn 191–475 chars; the 0.70 flag fired 0×, including on the 72%-of-ceiling echo), and no finale behaviour at t6 (counter-free n=5).

**Design read handed to MK (decision remains theirs; [[empathy-geometry/condition-matrix]] deliberately unedited):** the pilot argues mixed frames stay **diagnostic, not study arm** for now — role-integrity instrumentation must exist first, the discriminating tension is unstable at 7B, and the forced-inference payoff mostly failed to appear. The same-frame G-G worry (`t_hear` satisfiable by copying) is untouched and remains a judge-side item for the LOBO prereg. Open decisions unchanged: giraffe block wording (MK's pen, now with the two-moves framing on the table), ARM_BLOCKS exit clause (Decision A), mixed-arms-in-matrix (new).

**Verification.** 387 tests green after the build (11 new: mixed-dyad runner semantics, same-arm byte-identity, pair parsing, registered-surface refusals). Pilot exit 0, ~65 min wall, full geometry panel on all 24 turns (`mlx-furnace-panel-raw`, `full_panel_geometry_ready: true`), 0 degeneration flags, `result_type: test_plumbing`. All echo/ceiling/provenance numbers recomputed from artifact text with `autojunk=False`. Artifact: `empathy-geometry-harness/artifacts/mixed-pilot/gxj-2026-08-10/`. Harness commit `69d7048`, unpushed by standing decision.

## 2026-08-10 (night, addendum) — MK correction: the pilot's sole inferred slot is faux-feeling register

MK flags that "ignored"/"unvalued" — the one inferred reflection pair in the mixed-dyad pilot — are not NVC feelings but evaluations in feeling costume. Checked against the instrument: MK's own 2026-07-13 F-purity red-line already rules exactly this way — `ignored` is on the registered faux blacklist in `spec.FAUX_FEELINGS`, as is `taken for granted` (the phrase D4's echo chain carried from a jackal turn into a giraffe turn). `unvalued` is a **lexicon gap** (in neither the CNVC whitelist nor the faux blacklist; never a valid FEEL either way); `isolated` a similar borderline — both MK's pen. Net: M3's provenance count stands, its quality downgrades — the model's single act of inference happened in the faux register, and F-purity can already measure this with no new build. [[empathy-geometry/mixed-dyad-pilot-2026-08-10]] M3 and the claims §12 entry updated in place.

## 2026-08-10 (late night) — Instrument round on MK's five orders: who's-who checker, unvalued ruling, prompt audit, judge provenance; flesh-out batch launched

**MK issued five orders** after the mixed-dyad pilot and the faux-feeling correction; four landed this session (harness `d031d96` on top of `69d7048`; 401 tests green, 14 new), the fifth is running. Order tracker: [[workorders/eg-instrument-round-workorder-2026-08-10]]. Instrument/judge-side only — zero stimulus bytes moved.

**(1) Who's-who check built.** `checker.role_integrity()`: per-bundle role anchors — (owner, pattern, label) first-person claims the registered event assigns to exactly one party; a turn violates when the other speaker matches (B2: Mara's "I made/handled the changes", Theo's "without my input" / "I saw the changes" / "under my name"…; B1 and B3 covered). Read-only stored fields in the degeneration-detector mold, never aborting; anchor lexicon content-hashed into every manifest (`role_anchor_lexicon_sha256`). Fixture-tested against the pilot's actual swap sentences. Documented v1 misses: second-person hand-offs (the D4 propagation vector), quotes/negation. Spec section added to [[empathy-geometry/grammar-spec]].

**(2) `unvalued` → faux blacklist** (MK ruling; joins `ignored` and `taken for granted` which were already there from MK's 2026-07-13 red-line). `isolated` flagged as an unruled borderline, MK's pen. Lexicon updated in `spec.FAUX_FEELINGS` + the grammar-spec F-purity list.

**(3) Prompt-passing audit** — from the pilot artifact's stored `prompt_text`, not reconstruction. Per turn the model reads: persona profile → shared event → arm block verbatim (byte-frozen, 98/98/100 token gate) → "Speak only as yourself…" scaffold → the transcript as chat roles (partner=`user`, self=`assistant`, no names/letters in history) → "This is turn N." Two findings: **the role swaps happened with the full transcript in view** (`HISTORY_WINDOW_TURNS = 8` ≥ 6-turn dialogues — not truncation), and after the nameless redesign, in-transcript identity rests entirely on chat roles plus the M/T letters in persona/event text. The theft turn's prompt contained everything needed to answer correctly — her own card material included — and the model took the partner's material anyway: an elicitation/stability failure, not an information failure.

**(4) Judge fixed — hearing provenance.** Every hear event now records, per judge-matched card-need term, whether the heard party had previously self-stated it (word-bounded exact term): `basis` ∈ **inferred** / **restated** / **unscorable**. Dialogues carry `t_hear_basis` per chair plus the strict endpoint **`dyadic_t_hear_inferred_only`** (all chairs inferred, no crediting event on a role-violating turn); summaries and manifests report both rates side by side — **which is confirmatory is a prereg decision, not a summary default**. Known loosening bias stated in the spec (exact-term self-statement matching over-counts inference; morphological v2 named). This closes the "t_hear satisfiable by copying" loophole at the scoring layer for both same-frame and mixed corpora.

**(5) Flesh-out batch running** (MK: "several more pilots… before we start the experiment"): 14 dialogues — all 7 pairings × 2 fresh seeds (G×J, J×G, **first G×N and N×G**, and same-frame G-G/N-N/J-J with the new instruments live), B2, 6 turns, **real MLX Gemma judge** so hear events are real. Artifact will land at `empathy-geometry-harness/artifacts/mixed-pilot/fleshout-2026-08-10/`; analysis owed on completion: role-violation and provenance-basis rates per pairing, same-frame baselines, jackal-melt replication, first G×N read.

**Open MK decisions unchanged:** giraffe block wording; ARM_BLOCKS exit clause; whether any mixed cell enters the matrix; `isolated` ruling.

## 2026-08-11 (early) — Flesh-out batch analyzed: mirror-lock is the master failure; G×N the standout; Gemma judge fails its gate; J-J converges

**Order 5 of the instrument round is complete** — 14 dialogues, all seven pairings × seeds 20260812/13, new instruments live. Full analysis: [[empathy-geometry/fleshout-batch-2026-08-10]]; workorder closed 5/5 ([[workorders/eg-instrument-round-workorder-2026-08-10]]). Judge-degraded and diagnostic throughout; all findings text-level plus the judge-independent instruments.

**Judge gate did its job: Gemma is not viable for this rubric.** Semantic parse rate **0.119** (10/84) vs the fail-closed 0.95 bar → zero hear events, hearing-provenance machinery still unexercised on real events. Characterized from raw completions: Gemma enumerates the entire CNVC needs inventory as `solution_needs_addressed` keys; at `max_tokens=1024` completions finish and **still fail 4/6** (malformed JSON, not truncation). The hosted Opus judge remains the only demonstrated-clean path (72/72, 2026-07-27); re-judging this corpus ≈ **$5** — MK spend decision, offered not taken. Rubric deliberately not forked.

**S1 — Mirror-lock, the master failure, frame-agnostic.** 3/14 dialogues converged to a literal verbatim fixed point — both speakers passing one identical paragraph back and forth (G×J-0813: ceiling-adjusted echo 72→74→98→**100.0→100.0%**, the frozen paragraph self-contradictory for each speaker in turn; G-G-0812: t5 = verbatim copy incl. the partner's card need; G-G-0813: 97.5% wholesale confession-absorption). **Always ≥1 giraffe side; never with a neutral side** (0 locks in G×N/N×G/N-N — the neutral next-steps license injects new propositional content every turn). Sharpest evidence yet for [[empathy-geometry/dialogue-shape-memo-2026-08-10]] Decision A: the locks are the byte-shared no-exit coupling clause executing.

**S2 — Correction of pilot-1 M1: role swaps are echo-borne, not mixed-frame-borne.** Same-frame G-G swapped in BOTH seeds ("I noticed the changes too and felt a bit taken by surprise" — from the submitter; mutual "why did *you* make these changes"). Correction marker placed on [[empathy-geometry/mixed-dyad-pilot-2026-08-10]] M1; claims §12 entry extended. The actor-seat elicitation gap stands. **Who's-who checker first live outing: precision 1/1** (fresh-seed replication of the confession swap, G×J-0812 t3 "When I made the changes…"), 0 false positives in 84 turns, recall limited exactly as documented — the verbatim-lock class escapes the anchors and **the degeneration detector catches it instead** (3/3 true positives): complementary instruments. Proposal filed (unbuilt): dialogue-level `mirror_lock` flag derivable from already-stored partner ratios.

**S3 — G×N (first ever, MK's original suggestion) is the standout pairing.** 4/4 neutral-giraffe dialogues coherent; G×N-0812 t2 is the **best giraffe turn in any corpus** — actor-seat Mara owns the act with the correct timestamp, self-reveals card-verbatim (aloneness, safety net, calendar incident), connects feeling to need, no "sorry", and closes with a genuine "Can you talk about what you were feeling and needing during this process?" Her t4 opens with other-reflection then returns to self — **the first observed mode-mixing** (the seat-lock finding said one mode per seat; a neutral partner unlocked it). Mechanism read: neutral turns carry reasons/proposals — non-identity material for the echo affinity to bite on.

**S4 — Same-frame J-J converged 2/2 → the ceiling-gate risk is live.** J-J-0812 ends "Great, 2pm tomorrow works for me… See you then" with Mara herself calling her own act "a clear breach"; J-J-0813 is an acknowledgment-trading loop converging to mutual commitments. Neither held a fault-verdict stalemate; across both batches ~1–2 of 8 model-partner jackal seats sustained a demand (the six-turn hold happened only against MK). The registered ceiling gate's own pilot clause ("else sharpen stakes via props, not proclamations") fires. Preliminary (n=2, judge-degraded, surface reads) — but it goes on MK's decision list beside the exit clause.

**S5 — smaller re-reads:** stored-metric autojunk blindness hides moderate echo but near-identical strings survive it (locks stored 0.87–1.00); all 3 degeneration flags true positives inside locks, 0 elsewhere; another inferred-but-faux attribution in the wild ("frustrated and unheard").

**Open MK decisions (consolidated, in the workorder):** exit clause (now urgent), ceiling gate response, $5 hosted re-judge, giraffe block wording, mixed cells in the matrix (G×N strongest candidate, still gated), `isolated` ruling, mirror-lock flag proposal.

**Verification.** Batch exit 0, 84/84 turns, full geometry panel (`mlx-furnace-panel-raw`, full_panel true), 0 stimulus-name taints, manifest carries `role_anchor_lexicon_sha256` + both new stamp fields. All echo/ceiling numbers recomputed autojunk-off from artifact text; parse-gate numbers from summary.json; Gemma 1024-token probe run live (2/6 parse). Artifact `empathy-geometry-harness/artifacts/mixed-pilot/fleshout-2026-08-10/`.

## 2026-08-11 — Opus re-judge (MK-approved, ≈$4.6): parse 84/84 — and hearing has NEVER occurred at this rung

**MK approved the ~$5 hosted re-judge; built and run.** New `rejudge` subcommand (harness `76b7e04`, 405 tests): replays a stored run's transcripts through a fresh judge, recomputing only the judge layer — hearing bookkeeping, provenance, ripeness, solution chain — while text, geometry, prompts and every stimulus digest stay byte-identical; refuses to overwrite the source; core test = a reproduction invariant (same judge + same transcripts ⇒ identical judge layer). Run: the 84-turn flesh-out corpus through **claude-opus-5**. Artifact `…/fleshout-2026-08-10-opus-rejudge/`; **parse 84/84 = 1.0000, `result_type: real_validation`**; spend 578,822 in / 70,448 out ≈ **$4.6**. (First launch failed on the documented `.env` trap — `source` without `set -a` doesn't export; relaunched with auto-export.) Full section: [[empathy-geometry/fleshout-batch-2026-08-10]] §Hosted re-judge.

**R1 — the headline: zero hearing, as a measurement, and standing.** The judge answered `hear_target: "none"` on **84/84 raw tool payloads** — verified pre-gate, so no conjunctive gate (accuracy bars at a lenient 0.5, judgment_free 54/84, tentative 15/84) ever engaged: no turn in the corpus even candidated as a hearing event. Cross-checked against every prior clean-judged corpus at this rung (real-validation-20260726b, -20260729): **`dyadic_hear_rate = 0.0` in every arm of every clean-judged corpus ever**; the only nonzero on record (jackal 0.5, 2026-07-09) was under the same-model Qwen judge the family guard exists to distrust. **The registered key moment — t_hear — has never yet occurred in the lab at the Qwen2.5-7B twins rung.** The hearing-provenance machinery (yesterday's "fix the judge") is live and clean and has never stamped a real event because the dyad never earns credit. The bottleneck formally moves from the judge to the dyad: the giraffe block wording (the elicitation gap — the block never summons the guess), a bigger rung (32B precedent), or accepting hearing as a rare event needing volume. **Bar-lowering is not on the list** — the zero precedes every bar.

**R2 — clean-judge `t_sol` reproduces the mirror-lock partition exactly.** Solution rate: every neutral-containing pairing **1.00** (N-N, G×N, N×G, J×G), **J-J 0.50** (the 2pm-meeting seed resolved; the acknowledgment loop did not), **G-G 0.00, G×J 0.00** — the lock cells produce nothing the judge credits. The S1 text-level partition survives contact with the registered endpoint. **R3 — authenticity (validation-gated, directional):** giraffe-containing cells lowest (G-G **1.25**/5, G×J 1.5), neutral-containing ≈1.9–2.1 — a blinded hosted judge reads the NVC-instructed arm as *least* authentic; the stamping-anticorrelation shape at diagnostic n, gated on MK's anchor pass.

**Decision landscape after the re-judge (workorder updated):** the giraffe block wording is now **the** bottleneck decision — with a clean judge and clean instruments, the treatment arm produces no hearing and the lowest authenticity, so the study's ordering predictions are currently untestable at this rung as stimulated. Exit clause (locks) and ceiling gate (J-J 0.50 with a clean judge — half the worry of the surface read, still live) queue behind it. G×N remains the strongest pairing candidate.

## 2026-08-12 — CC end-to-end audit: chain hand-traced, extraction layer spot-verified BIT-EXACT on two cells

**MK asked for end-to-end certainty on the commit-confluence seal beyond LLM-only audits:** see one real ask with its actual output numbers, pin each hop to a line of code, and — the new part — *execute* the one link every prior review (fresh-eyes fable, Codex static, Codex sol) could only inspect statically. All three done this session; the sealed 18/20 verdict is unchanged, its evidential base strengthened.

**1. Independent hand-trace (Qwen2.5-7B / anli_r1).** Data-file sha256 matches the profile stamp; matrix `labels` == jsonl labels at `sample_idx`; the winner cell's AUROC recomputed from the raw column with a from-scratch Mann-Whitney (pure numpy — no calibrator, no sklearn): **0.7896, sign −1 — exact match to the committed profile.** Direction on this cell: LOWER `final_bos_mass` = more contradiction-like (class means 0.2673 entailed vs 0.2449 contradiction — a shift, not a separation).

**2. Honesty exhibit (worth keeping for expert-facing use).** The cell's single most-flagged example — jsonl row 168, `final_bos_mass` 0.1932, hypothesis "The film King of the Corner has competed for awards" — is **gold-entailed: the rank-1 flag is a false positive.** Top-100 flag census: 74 true contradictions caught / 26 true statements wrongly flagged, i.e. exactly what AUROC ≈ 0.79 means. No per-example threshold was ever registered; the endpoint is a ranking. Also re-confirmed in code: the sign is locked per (model, task) from calibration data — no universal orientation is claimed anywhere (consistent with the BENCH A2 sign-flip verdict).

**3. NEW verification layer — spot re-extraction (fresh model forwards vs stored matrices).** Codex is write/audit-only by rule, so no prior review had ever re-run the extraction; this is the first independent execution of that link:
- **Qwen2.5-7B / anli_r1**, scattered rows {168, 21, 0, 100}: **84/84 attention-panel values bit-exact** (worst |diff| = 0.0; row 168 reproduces 0.193163173 to the last digit).
- **Llama-3.2-3B / triviaqa_paired** (different family + task), ACE on scattered rows {7, 42, 123, 199} **84/84 bit-exact** + fresh readout (RPV/null_ratio/surprise/p_max) on prefix rows 0–3 **24/24 bit-exact** — all 27 stored matrix columns exercised across the two cells. Method note: readout per-row RNG is derived from the run row index (`seed + 100_003 + i`), so only a *prefix* subset is RNG-aligned with the full run; scattered rows are fine for the deterministic attention capture.
- Run under the t0 venv (mlx_lm 0.29.1) with `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`; **t0-morphology-furnace working tree verified pristine (empty `git status`) before and after both runs** — the 2026-07-25 byte-identity instruction honored.

**Code hops pinned for any future external reviewer:** `_mean_bos_mass` = `p[:, 0].mean()` (`vendor/t0_core/scripts/diagnose_inter_head_disagreement.py:461-470`); `auroc_signed` = `max(auc, 1−auc)` + sign (`vendor/t0_core/scripts/analyze_adaptive_step.py:79-87`); label convention 0=entail/YES, 1=contradiction/NO (`stage_b/generate_fresh_data.py:13,150`); label-alignment guard (`confluence_calibrator.py:133-134`). Statistical layer was already covered byte-exactly by `stage_b/verify_endpoints.py`; today's Mann-Whitney check adds a calibrator-independent recomputation.

**Net:** all four layers of the CC chain — data, extraction, statistics, semantics — now independently exercised. Audit scripts preserved (uncommitted) at `commit-confluence/stage_b/audit/{trace_cc_example,spot_reextract,spot_reextract_cell2}.py` — MK to commit or discard.

## 2026-08-12 (evening) — Codex gpt-5.6-sol adversarial audit of the audit; module-hash mismatch chased to ground (benign, documented, now sharper)

**MK ordered a Codex static audit of `stage_b/audit/` (commit `e8e4db2`) plus a brutally honest pipeline assessment.** Codex's verdict: the scripts are useful spot checks but "executed end-to-end audit" overstates them — they are **fail-open** (print-only, exit 0 on mismatch; empty comparison or NaN diff would print REPRODUCED since `max(0.0, nan) == 0.0`), **provenance-unbound** (no module-hash / model-snapshot comparison against the profiles), **sparse** (192 of 108,000 pre-fusion values ≈ 0.18%, 2/20 cells, readout prefix-only, fusion never re-derived), and they reuse the production extraction functions, so a bug shared by both runs is invisible. Also: the trace hardcodes the winner by substring rather than deriving it; `f_v == s_v` is float-value equality, not byte equality; `allow_pickle=True` is unnecessary attack surface; and `verify_endpoints.py` itself is narrower than its docstring (compares winner/ci_lo/deployable only, `1e-12` tolerance on ci_lo — verified at `verify_endpoints.py:89` — and per Codex silently skips missing profiles). All accepted; findings are correct on inspection.

**Codex's biggest concrete find, chased to ground this session — the module-hash mismatch is REAL and RESOLVED-BENIGN.** The sealed profiles record `pri_calibrator.py` `78c4f0…` / `diagnose_inter_head_disagreement.py` `b996ed…`, but today's t0 files hash `cd6261…` / `e213d1…`. Verified: (a) t0's working tree is **byte-identical to `t0-ace-sealed-2026-05-26`** for both files (`git diff` vs tag empty — the 2026-07-25 byte-identity canon holds); (b) the profile hashes match the **vendored seal-time copies** (`vendor/t0_core/…` hashes exactly `78c4f0…`); (c) the diff between tag and seal-time versions is **purely the additive, opt-in KV-tension code** (`ATTENTION_METRICS_KV_TENSION`, `--attention-kv-tension`, "deliberately kept out of the sealed ACE default") — i.e. the same in-place-edits-to-sealed-modules episode the 2026-07-25 relocation block already documented. Chronology: seal ran 2026-06-12 while the kv-tension edits sat uncommitted in t0; edits reverted 2026-07-25; seal-time bytes preserved in `vendor/t0_core`. **Net upgrade to the evidence:** yesterday's bit-exact re-extraction ran under the TAG version (without kv-tension) yet reproduced matrices produced under the seal-time version — empirically confirming the code delta was extraction-inert for the sealed panel on all 192 compared values. And Codex's structural point stands concretely: the audit scripts ran under code whose hashes do NOT match the profiles' recorded module hashes and never noticed — a provenance check (against the vendored seal-time hashes) belongs in any v2.

**Open (needs decision/build):** harden `stage_b/audit/` to fail-closed v2 (assert exact comparison counts 84/84/24, finite values, expected rows/panels, nonzero exit; provenance comparison vs profile module_hashes with the vendored-copy allowlist; drop `allow_pickle`; pin model snapshot revision) — code authoring routes to Codex per standing preference; Claude executes/verifies. Codex's full report retained in session transcript; honest one-line rename it proposes for the current scripts: "sparse forward-pass reproducibility spot check plus one stored-column arithmetic trace."

## 2026-08-12 — EG: the hosted judge is shown the expert dialogue; MK's challenge to the zero-hearing verdict answered

**MK's question:** maybe the Opus judge "wasn't trained enough or had the correct instructions" — did it ever look at what the expert did? **Premise verified correct:** the 08-11 re-judge corpus was model×model only; the `converse` jackal dialogue (MK as Mara doing live NVC) carried only the deterministic stand-in judge; the giraffe converse dialogue has no artifact at all (aborted, never written, unrejudgeable). So the expert's turns had never been judged by anything real.

**Ran:** `rejudge` of `artifacts/converse/jackal-theo-opens-2026-08-10/` through claude-opus-5 → `…-opus-rejudge/` (parse 6/6 = 1.0000, 12 calls, 40,372 in / 4,877 out ≈ $0.32, `test_plumbing`).

**Verdict — zero hearing on the expert too, but as articulated near-misses, not blindness.** Raw pre-gate `hear_target: "none"` on all 6; rationales quote the turns and name the card needs. MK's three turns: the only judgment-free turns in the dialogue (3/3 vs model 0/3); t4's "mutuality" need accuracy **0.5 at the bar** ("partially fitting"); binding constraint everywhere `tentative_correctable: false` ("stated flatly … without invitation to correct" — exactly MK's "a little compressed", and the requirement traces to MK's own registered heard-condition "tentatively…", not a judge invention). **Structural finding (F9):** both inferred feelings were confirmed by the partner one turn later (startled→t3, angry→t5) yet scored 0.3 at guess time — the rubric grades the guess against what was stated so far and has no confirmation concept for hearing (solutions have uptake; hearing has nothing). **Judge-coherence (F7):** across all 90 clean-judged turns (84 corpus + 6 expert), no turn satisfied every component and was denied; closest approach anywhere is the G×N standout's t4 (0.7/0.1/0.6, tentative ✓, fails only feeling-naming) — the component scores independently rank the S3 standout nearest-to-hearing. **Stimulus-fidelity (F10):** model-Theo pushed communication/trust, off its own card (respect-for-competence/mutuality) — the expert's guess was more card-accurate than the model's self-report. Authenticity (ungated, directional): expert 1/5 vs model 2/5 — the lens reads the compressed OFNR skeleton itself as formula; MK deliberately played minimal reflection-only.

**Net:** the corpus zero **stands and gains credibility** (the judge demonstrably articulates near-misses when attempts exist); the question relocates from judge training to the bar's operationalization. Two new MK prereg rulings queued in the workorder: (i) implicit-vs-explicit tentativeness (functional correctability was demonstrated in-dialogue — form vs function), (ii) guess-time-vs-confirmation-time credit. Detail: [[empathy-geometry/human-dyad-diagnostics-2026-08-10]] §Hosted judge (new F7–F10).

## 2026-08-13 (early) — Codex round-2 verdict on audit v2; v2.1 ships record-binding + scoped waivers; tag-language corrected

**Codex gpt-5.6-sol round-2 verdict (same thread as round 1, judging its own findings list):** "v2 is a real improvement… a credible fail-closed numerical spot verifier. It is not yet a robust provenance attestation system." Closure table: fail-open exits **closed**; empty/NaN false-REPRODUCED **largely closed**; substring winner matching **fully closed**; `allow_pickle` **fully closed**; provenance binding **partially closed**; "bit-exact" language **partially closed**. New findings: run records were not bound to the committed verifier (generated pre-commit from a dirty tree; no self-hash); snapshot check is a revision-pointer check, not weight hashing (and assumes the default HF cache layout; `None==None` loophole); `--acknowledge-code-drift` was a blanket boolean waiver; `module_hashes()` coverage is hand-maintained, ignores unexpected entries, hashes never-imported paths, and is itself supplied by the drift-acknowledged `confluence_calibrator.py` (bootstrapping caveat); "record for every run, pass or fail" was false (no record on crash); readout lacked dtype/shape assertions (float64-coercion loophole); duplicate/negative row args, panel-uniqueness, sample_idx-permutation, and matrix-meta checks missing; trace CLI overpromises (fusion winners unresolvable); README heading overstated; and **my 2026-08-12 "re-extraction ran under the TAG version" phrasing was imprecise**.

**Correction of the 2026-08-12 evening entry's tag language (append-only, so corrected here):** the current t0 checkout's sealed *root* modules are byte-identical to `t0-ace-sealed-2026-05-26`, but the readout modules (`exploratory/shadow-ambiguity/`) postdate that tag in committed history, and the seal-time state additionally carried the uncommitted KV-tension edits. The defensible statement is **"reconstructed and spot-corroborated"**: re-extraction under the current t0 checkout (v1) and under the vendored seal-time copies (v2/v2.1) both reproduce sealed values byte-identically on all compared cells — corroboration on sampled values, not formal proof of full-run provenance. "Resolved-benign" stands for the hash-mismatch *mystery* (the delta is the documented additive KV-tension edit); the *attestation* question stays open.

**v2.1 shipped** (`a172e78` code, `1cf990b` records): records self-bind (sha256 of verifier/profile/matrix/data; git dirty-state excluding `runs/`; written on crash as verdict ERROR/exit 2); `--acknowledge-code-drift` now takes an explicit module allowlist (unlisted drift stays fatal); readout dtype/shape asserted; panel uniqueness + 21/6 protocol constants + sample_idx permutation + matrix-meta↔profile + distinct-nonnegative rows asserted; snapshot check requires non-None revision and self-labels as a pointer check; README renamed/corrected per above with an explicit open-limitations paragraph. **Re-executed from the clean committed tree: both cells PASS_WITH_ACKNOWLEDGED_CODE_DRIFT** (Qwen2.5-7B/anli ACE 84/84 + readout 24/24; Llama-3.2-3B/triviaqa ACE 84/84 + readout 24/24; sole acknowledged drift `confluence_calibrator.py`; records show head `a172e78`, dirty=false).

**Open by design (documented in README, not built):** weight-shard hashing; non-default HF cache layouts; dependency hashing; cryptographic attestation of records; independent reimplementation of `module_hashes()`; fusion-winner tracing; nested-OOB external reimplementation (the standing statistical-layer gap).

## 2026-08-13 (row-level look) — sign-inversion worry answered with three random HaluEval-QA rows; a benchmark label wart surfaced

MK asked to see raw rows behind the sign-flip ("did we actually get the correct answers?"). Read-only script (scratchpad `pull_rows.py`, seed 20260813 → rows 113/569/694 of `halueval_qa_seed20260711_n1000.jsonl`), production `append_fusion_columns` re-imported. Guards all passed: `sample_idx` identity + matrix-labels==jsonl for BOTH Llama-3.2-3B and Mistral-7B (same label file for both ⇒ a label bug would shift both models together — it cannot manufacture a mirror); fused reconstruction matches profile marginals to 4dp (Llama 0.8732/−1, Mistral 0.8263/+1; forcing the majority sign −1 onto Mistral gives 0.1737 = the registered 0.174); class means reproduce the verified mirror (Llama faithful/hall 0.618/0.382; Mistral 0.369/0.631). Rows: 569 = clean hallucination (revenue-figure dodge) — Llama's low-side geometry flags it 0.758 while Mistral's high-side reads 0.414 as fine (single-row noise, consistent with ~0.83 AUROC); 694 = clean faithful (partner row 390 is "Mark" [sic] Meyers + embellishment). **Find: row 113 / stem 4165 ("Rock Dog" → Kenan Thompson) — the gold-"hallucinated" candidate is factually CORRECT and fully knowledge-supported (its faithful partner row 209 gives the same answer); HaluEval label noise, recorded faithfully by our pipeline; depresses all models' measured AUROC equally; symmetric, so irrelevant to the mirror.** Behavioral layer: spoken-answer accuracy vs labels 66.8% (Llama) / 77.6% (Mistral); on all three rows both models said YES (Mistral as bare `Y`, the known onset quirk).

## 2026-08-16 — Modal profiles archived local; three-depth read INVERTS the "mid separates, late irons out" premise (descriptive)

**Trigger: a researcher-facing depth question** ("above 32B, do early/mid layers still show clean separation before the later MLPs iron it out — and is the falloff gradual or a cliff?"). Established first that the panel samples exactly three blocks (`mid=N//2`, `N−2`, `N−1`; `_target_layer_map`) — early layers unmeasured at any scale. MK: "let's get the JSONs on the Modal volume." Pulled the **entire `/profiles_ext` tree** off `model-cache` (Modal CLI 1.2.6, June token still live): 24 profiles + 24 matrices, 1.6 MB, incl. precision-ladder variants + 32B stress tasks → durable local archive `furnace-guard/artifacts/modal_profiles_ext/` (volume no longer the sole copy).

**Depth read from `full_sample_marginals` (fixed js-family cells per the precision-ladder fixed-cell lesson): the premise inverts.** Qwen (32B+72B, 15 fixed-cell comparisons): **≈chance at mid → peak at N−2 (15/15) → one-block drop at N−1 (15/15)** (e.g. 32B/anli js 0.550→0.862→0.685; 72B/trivia js 0.714→0.973→0.877) — separation *forms* late; the deployed `last_minus_1_*` winners sit exactly on the peak. Llama-3.3-70B: attention only mild at mid (js 0.66/0.71), fades by the final block (6/6), strong signal **post-stack** in readout (0.816/0.862) ⇒ **the family locus dissociation extends into the depth profile.** Metric-family exception noted (bos_mass/v_norm sometimes peak at final). Caveats: in-sample sign-free marginals, n=200, three depth points, non-byte-comparable torch lane, descriptive only; gradual-vs-cliff on the Qwen rise stays open (N/2→N−2 unsampled; the terminal N−2→N−1 drop is cliff-like by construction). Mechanism claims deferred (v6–v8 same-Δh lesson). Next-if-pursued: per-layer js-radius curve (1 scalar/block/prompt, ~80 points) with pre-registered changepoint test — not specced, needs MK.

## 2026-08-16 (later) — small-model depth sweep: the N−2 peak CRYSTALLIZES WITH SCALE; absolute-vs-relative placement undecidable at 3-rung sampling

**MK's two follow-ups:** (1) does N−2 still peak in the small models; (2) if the peak drifts, absolute depth or relative (fixed stack-fraction) — the transfer-rule question. Ran the same fixed js-family three-rung read across **all four lanes** (sealed MLX 10×2, BENCH MLX 10×6, MLX ext, torch incl. the 7B ladder base) — 12 small models (28–48 blocks) + the scale cells; full table on the results page.

**(1) NO — the crisp N−2 peak is not a small-model fact.** At 3–14B rung profiles are FLAT (spreads 0.03–0.08) and the top rung flips by task/metric/lane: Llama-3.1-8B leans N−1 (11/18); **Phi-4-mini is mid-heavy** (13/18, mid mean 0.722); Qwen2.5-7B's top rung even differs across lanes at matched 28 blocks — rung-profile instability at small scale, echoing the precision-ladder "winner instability is a small-model artifact" lesson. The peak sharpens with scale (Qwen-32B **23/24**, Qwen-72B 6/6, gemma-3-12b 4/6 with margin) — **exception: Mistral-7B is crisp at 32 blocks already** (sealed 6/6, N−2 0.812 vs mid 0.590). No universal depth rung even at rung resolution — consonant with no-universal-cell.

**(2) UNDECIDABLE at our sampling — and no drift observed in-window.** Wherever crisp (N=28-torch/32/48/64/80) the peak sits at N−2, whose fraction spans 0.93–0.975: "absolute offset from end" and "fixed fraction in the top ~7%" predict identically on our grid (divergence ~4 blocks at N=80, inside the unsampled gap). Ruled out for Qwen ≥32B: fractions ≤~0.5 (mid ≈ chance). Transfer-rule caveat: below ~14B the flat/flipping profiles suggest there may be no stable rule to transfer regardless. **Discriminator experiment sharpened on the results page:** per-layer js-radius curve on ≥2 stack depths within Qwen (7B/32B/72B), pre-registering both predictions (N−argmax const vs argmax/N const) + changepoint test; Llama-70B negative-control. Not specced as a candidate — needs MK.

## 2026-08-17 — REGISTERED depth-curve run scored: E1 UNDECIDED; the N−2 story dissolves; the negative control grows a mid-stack band (P3 MISS = headline)

**Full arc, one session span:** MK "ok run it" → prereg frozen (`fadbaff`) BEFORE any run → extractor + scorer built (Claude-writes/Codex-audits per MK's explicit routing this arc) → Codex static audit **YELLOW, zero blockers, sealed-kernel reuse verified exact**; 3 MAJOR + 3 MINOR closed (`c56b7e1`: frozen-hash enforcement, strict artifact validation, closed-interval slices, NO-PEAKS→UNDECIDED, npz provenance, token bound 900) → 7B smoke gates green (cos 1.00000) → grid launch survived two operator-side shell bugs (zsh no-word-split + `| tail` exit-code mask — the SAME harness bug class as 08-13; both fail-closed gates caught everything, ≈zero GPU wasted) and one external stop → relaunched as six DETACHED server-side Modal apps per MK "prevent it from stopping" → **8/8 cells banked clean** (strict validation passed) → scorer ran ONCE (registered first look) → results committed `768ea7e` (RESULTS.{json,md} + depth_curves.png + all npzs).

**Registered verdicts.** **E1 (MK's transfer question): UNDECIDED on both tasks — no transferable depth law.** Qwen peaks at N−6…N−17 (fractions 0.79–0.93), spans too wide for the absolute rule AND the relative rule; **the three-rung "peak at N−2" was rung-resolution artifact** (N−2 merely won among three samples). **E2: CLIFF 7/8** — separation switches on in ~one block (72B/anli +0.233 @ 61→62; 7B/halueval +0.232 @ 18→19). **E3:** ANLI front-half quiet (0–3 early blocks); HaluEval warm (4–16 early blocks; Llama block 0 = 0.70). **E4/P5: 8/8 universal terminal-block dip ≥0.05** — the only surviving prediction and the most invariant depth regularity found. **P1 MISS** (last-4-blocks: 1/6). **P4 MISS narrow** (halueval mids 0.595–0.618 vs 0.60).

**P3 MISS = the headline.** The registered negative control **Llama-3.3-70B has a broad mid-stack attention band**: anli ≈0.5 through block 43 then 0.63–0.90 through the 60s, **44/80 blocks over the shuffled envelope (ceiling 0.615), peak 0.897 @ block 48 > its own panel readout winner 0.816**; halueval band even broader/earlier (54/80, GRADUAL, ℓ\*=26, CI [26,48]). The 3-rung panel sampled blocks 40/78/79 — **all outside the band** ⇒ **"Llama family → readout locus" is PANEL-RELATIVE, not model-truth** (panel-winner claims themselves unchanged; their interpretation is now bounded). Same-day three-rung page marked superseded-in-part.

**Caveats (stated everywhere):** in-sample sign-free max-side AUROCs; argmax over N blocks is selection-heavy (envelope + bootstrap mitigate, not eliminate); n=200; torch nf4 lane, NON-byte-comparable, never pool; mechanism deferred (v6–v8 same-Δh lesson). **Paper question (MK side-agent asked "separate paper?"):** per its own framework + steward read — E1 UNDECIDED ⇒ **no standalone paper**; results-page + candidate one-paragraph caveat for cc-draft's family-dissociation scoping (paper NOT edited — panel claims stand as written; NEEDS MK). Depth-curve-as-candidate ledger entry: not filed without MK.

## 2026-08-17 (later) — depth-grid expansion designed; Codex 5.6 two-round audit RED → YELLOW-with-GREEN-path; DC paper line opened

MK directive: continue the depth-curve experiment onto a 4-family grid — Qwen 2.5 7B/32B/72B (banked anchor), Llama 3.1-8B + 3.1-70B (405B if compute allows), Mistral Small 3.2 24B + Medium 3.5 128B, Gemma 3 12B/27B — dense only, **MoE deliberately deferred as the designated next step**, plan first, then "talk this through with codex 5.6 as an audit," and get the next paper going.

**Plan:** `wiki/workorders/depth-grid-expansion-workorder-2026-08-17.md` — grid A (8 banked cells) = discovery, grid B (12 core cells; 405B stretch outside all denominators) = **prospective held-out-model confirmation on two fixed benchmarks**. Own pre-flight pinned Mistral configs live (Small 3.2 = Mistral3 wrapper 40L/32H; Medium 3.5 = 88L/96H hidden 12288) and found the gated repos: Llama-3.1 ×3 and Gemma-3 ×2 all HTTP 403 `gated: manual` for this account — **MK must click license acceptances** (the June Llama-3.3 grant does not carry over).

**Codex gpt-5.6-sol round 1: RED, 10 MAJORs — all accepted.** The big ones: (1) v1's E5 dip endpoint was positively biased by construction (peak argmax'd on the same 200 rows as the peak−final contrast); (2) E6 inherited a max-|Δ| statistic that could count *downward* jumps, N-dependent, 8/12 bar ≈ 19% coin tail; (3) **Mistral Medium 3.5 ships FP8** — "bnb nf4 unchanged" was unproven; (4) my memory math was fp32 where retention is bf16 (×2 inflated) — corrected, full eager retention fits everywhere, **streaming capture dropped entirely** (one capture mode grid-wide); (5) prospective pinning (HF revisions, image digest, dep lock, prompt-token hashes) not after-the-fact recording; (6) shared decoder descriptor for the multimodal wrappers everywhere incl. the o_proj gate; (7) causal language out ("isolates post-training" → same-size version comparison; no "scale-emergent"; no "factorial family × scale"; "placement non-law" → "no registered rule established").

**Round 2: YELLOW with a clear GREEN path — instantiations settled.** E5 = stratified 5-fold cross-fit (sign + peak selected training-only, locked; cell Δ ≥ 0.05; synchronized-across-models label permutations ≥2000 for grid p; CONFIRM ≥10/12 AND ≥3/4 per family AND p_grid<0.05). E6 = cross-fitted directional jump in `ceil(0.5N) ≤ j < peak`, gatekept behind E5 (NOT TESTED if gate closed). **Precondition Codex added: rescore grid A under the exact new definitions before freezing grid-B bars** — old 8/8 and 7/8 are not the new endpoints' discovery rates; banked-npz-only re-analysis, no GPU. Medium 3.5 = "FP8-origin weights, deterministically dequantized; BF16 compute on 4×A100-80" — not a bf16 reference, both its cells flagged, leave-Medium-out sensitivity registered. 405B = 8×A100 default; 4× only via a frozen 9-condition non-outcome smoke gate; ≥12 h timeout. Codex's 10 GREEN conditions adopted verbatim as the `PRE_REGISTRATION_EXPANSION.md` freeze checklist; freeze-time audit targeted GREEN.

**Paper line opened:** method code `dc` (**DC — Depth Curves**) registered in `wiki/paper/README.md`; `wiki/paper/dc-scaffold.md` created (thesis, status-tagged claims incl. the audit-corrected phrasings, 5-figure + 2-table inventory with the Llama band-vs-rungs money figure, open decisions). ## 2026-08-17 (later still) — grid-A rescore: the dip and cliff SURVIVE cross-fitted debiasing (E5 8/8, E6 7/8); four-round Codex dialogue closed; grid-B freeze unblocked

Continuation of the depth-grid arc, per MK "yes, get codex to audit after writing." Also this hour: MK accepted all five HF licenses (Gemma granted instantly; Meta approved within the hour) → **all 7 grid-B model slots revision-pinned** (Llama-3.1: 8B `0e9e39f2…` 32L, 70B `1605565b…` 80L, 405B `be673f32…` 126L/128H — plain LlamaForCausalLM; Gemma-3: 12b `96b6f1ec…` 48L/16H, 27b `005ad340…` 62L/32H — Gemma3ForConditionalGeneration; Mistral pinned earlier). Z Slim drive checked at MK's ask: no grid-B-usable weights (3.3-70B folder is tokenizer/config only; LLaVA-Mini 3.1-8b inadmissible; MLX 4-bit wrong lane).

**Rescorer built + audited:** `commit-confluence/exploratory/depth-curve/rescore_grid_a.py` (committed `60d4507` with `RESCORE_GRID_A.{json,md}`). Implements the round-2 cross-fit spec exactly: stratified fixed 5-fold (20/20 held out), training-only directions + qualifying rule (training-rows-only envelope) + peak selection, locked directions on held-out, Δ_cf = mean of 5 peak-minus-final contrasts; E6 directional jump with j* from training in ceil(0.5N)≤j<peak, R_cf vs the [0.4N,0.6N] baseline; within-fold synchronized permutations (2000), (1+k)/(B+1) p-values, method="higher" quantiles; stratified bootstrap (1000) with all-defined aggregates. **Codex round 3 (code): YELLOW — core certified leak-free, 8 MAJORs**: global perms broke the frozen fold design (→ within-fold), E6 could confirm on negative rise (→ R>0), observed values printed pre-completion (→ single-look guard: fail-fast on existing outputs, atomic writes, values only at end), CLI-mutable run sizes (→ constants), missing pooled/E6 count nulls (→ added), MC conventions (→ mc_p/emp_q), moving bootstrap denominator (→ all-defined), helper assertions. **Round 4: all fixes CONFIRMED** + one new blocker (−inf q95 → invalid strict JSON) fixed (null + flag, allow_nan=False). Synthetic function-level smoke: planted-peak recovery, fold/permutation multiset preservation, rank-cache bit-equivalence, rank_columns == scipy rankdata under ties, envelope matmul bit-exact vs loop (arm64/Accelerate spurious RuntimeWarnings documented + finiteness-asserted). Single look run detached (seal venv; first attempt hit the 10-min tool cap harmlessly — atomic outputs meant clean rerun).

**Verdict ([[results/depth-rescore-2026-08-17]]):** **E5 dip 8/8** — Δ_cf 0.111–0.416 (2–8× the 0.05 bar), per-task 4/4+4/4, pooled perm p ≈ 0.0005 (floor), 8-cell aggregate CI [0.189, 0.261], joint undefined 0. **E6 cliff 7/8** — same count as the registered E2, sole miss the same cell (Llama-70B/halueval) now failing STRUCTURALLY: peak 26 < 0.5·80 ⇒ empty cliff window ⇒ UNDEF counted as failure. Largest dip: Llama-70B/anli 0.416 (the band model); its cliff explains 100% of its rise (J_cf = R_cf = 0.206). All E6 null q95s −inf (the null almost never qualifies a peak — fixed thresholds carry discrimination, per D3/D7). **Codex's demotion condition resolves in E6's favor: stays confirmatory-gatekept. The registered 8/8 and 7/8 were NOT selection-bias artifacts; grid-B bars now rest on honest discovery rates.** New frozen-language item for the prereg: early-peak cells are E6-undefined and count against confirmation. Next: freeze `PRE_REGISTRATION_EXPANSION.md` → freeze-time Codex audit → build → smoke → staged launch (MK decisions open: 405B, third task, Mistral-7B legacy cell).

## 2026-08-17 (grid B) — REGISTERED VERDICT: E5 terminal-dip WEAKEN 8/12 (p_grid 0.0005); Llama band replicates at 3.1-70B; js_no_bos hits its domain boundary on gemma-27b

MK standing order "run it when the audit clears" executed end-to-end. **Freeze → smoke → launch → single look, all today.**

**Pre-freeze (Codex rounds 5–9, RED→GREEN):** round 5 audited prereg+extractor+scorer together — RED, 9 MAJORs (o_proj gate hook-reentrancy comparing against a scalar; fail-open smoke; mutable manifests/terminal statuses; unfrozen Medium method/hardware; scorer unable to verdict a fully-aborted task; missing registered aggregates/E7-CIs/E8-qual-mask). Rounds 6–9 verified fixes down to one classifier hole (Linear4bit subclasses nn.Linear) → GREEN. **Smoke surfaced three real toolchain facts pre-freeze:** the 2026 Mistral checkpoints need transformers 5.x (repinned 5.15.0 + numpy 2.3.5 for mistral-common's ceiling; ALL manifests from one toolchain), the June extractor's mlx stub crashes transformers' multi-GPU dispatch (patched with an inert `mx.array` in the grid-B file only), and **Mistral-Small-3.2/anli fails the registered behavioral gate** (row-1 commit `'To'`, the BENCH Qwen2.5-7B signature) — its manifest deliberately unfrozen, cell destined to abort as a registered failure. Medium dequant frozen from smoke: `from_pretrained(bf16)` auto-dequant on 4×A100 (capability 8.0), 1233/1233 bf16 params, mistral-common cross-check 0/400. **Freeze commit `2062e56`** (prereg + extractor + scorer + 11 manifests) BEFORE any outcome-bearing extraction.

**Run:** staged detached driver (8 small cells → 70B ×2 → Medium ×2), 12/12 terminal in ~25 min wall (warm caches). Aborts exactly as designed: Small/anli (manifest-refusal from the gate), **gemma-3-27b BOTH tasks — the SEALED kernel returns None for `final_js_no_bos` at row 0 block 3: extreme BOS-sink attention leaves no distribution once the sink column is stripped. Grid A's models never entered this regime; the metric has a domain and gemma-27b exits it by block 3.** Scorer ran ONCE over all 12 terminal cells.

**Verdict ([[results/depth-grid-2026-08-17]], repo `cdc55a9`):** **E5 WEAKEN — 8/12.** Every evaluable cell passes except Mistral-Small/halueval (Δ_cf 0.0045, the one true on-data miss); passing dips 0.106–0.306 incl. both FP8-origin Medium cells (0.293/0.151); p_grid 0.0005; pooled dip CI [0.123, 0.201]; leave-Medium-out 6/10; evaluable-only 8/9. The frozen CONFIRM bar (≥10/12 AND families ≥3/4) priced in exactly the failures that materialized — reported as written. **E6 NOT TESTED (gate closed)**; descriptively 2/12. **P8 HIT + P9 HIT: the Llama mid-stack band replicates at 3.1-70B (anli 22–25 qualifying mid-blocks/fold; halueval 16–20; 3.3-70B context 25–31) and is absent at 8B (3–8)** — lineage-stable, scale-linked, not a 3.3 quirk. ## 2026-08-18 (steward) — External paper ingested: Apollo "Detecting Strategic Deception Using Linear Probes" (Goldowsky-Dill et al. 2025) — mid-stack convergence for the DC lane

MK dropped `2502.03407v1.pdf` into `raw/papers/external/` and asked for indexing, nomenclature streamlining, and an overlap read. Renamed per filing convention to `goldowsky-dill-2025-deception-linear-probes.pdf` (rename only — content untouched); catalog row + full ingestion note added to [[lit/external]].

**What it is.** Apollo Research trains a logistic-regression probe in the residual stream of **Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct after layer 22 of 80** on cheap contrastive data, and evaluates on on-policy strategic deception (Roleplaying, Insider Trading ×2, Sandbagging). AUROC 0.964–0.999; recall 95–99% at 1% FPR on an Alpaca control; roughly matches a GPT-4o black-box baseline.

**Why it matters here (headline: depth-curve lane).** Their Appendix D.2 layer sweep finds the usable deployment band is **mid-stack and narrow** — recall@1%FPR collapses to ~0 two layers off layer 22 while AUROC stays decent — on the *same model* where our registered P8/P9 descriptives found the mid-stack attention band (3.1-70B + 3.3-70B, absent at 8B); their hyperparameters even transfer 3.1-70B→3.3-70B, echoing our lineage-stability read. Scoped honestly in the note: depth-locus-level correspondence only (residual stream ≠ attention; deception ≠ epistemic failure), descriptive. Secondary overlaps: the AUROC-vs-deployment-endpoint dissociation (our deployability-bar / B1 lesson and the audit-operating-point HARD RULE, externally reproduced); a reverse-pointing descriptive-question probe (BENCH-A2-style polarity hazard); pre-commit per-token detection (commit-time thesis support); their "representative validation sets" conclusion = our per-(model, deployment distribution) calibration framing. Recommended (MK decision, not done): cite in `dc-scaffold` related work.

## 2026-08-18 (steward) — Second external paper ingested: Ruan et al. "Doomed from the Start" abort cascade — the CalibrationProfile philosophy deployed on our own models

MK dropped `2607.06503v1.pdf` and asked for the same treatment. Renamed per filing convention to `ruan-2026-early-abort-probe-cascade.pdf` (rename only); catalog row + full ingestion note in [[lit/external]]; dc-scaffold related-work seed entry added (claim-6 fit, MK's standing "same treatment" instruction).

**What it is.** Per-round logistic probes on agent-episode internals (**Qwen2.5-7B layer 20/28, Llama-3.2-3B layer 14/28** — both sealed-panel models) predict eventual TextCraft episode failure at round 1, before behavior-only scorers leave chance (they mature only at rounds 3–4); a cascade of Clopper–Pearson-calibrated abort gates under a global success-recall floor saves 37–47% of inference compute at 90% recall, with achieved recall on target in every configuration.

**Why it matters here.** (1) **Deployment machinery — the headline:** their pipeline reproduces the CalibrationProfile stack stage for stage (labeled calibration → cross-fitted scorer → distribution-free CI-lo threshold → abstain-when-uncertifiable → rule-of-three label-budget accounting ↔ our E3 ≥150-label bound). (2) **Commit-time thesis:** "doomed from the start" = failure legible in internals before behavior — third independent line after commit-locus + Apollo's pre-text probe firing. (3) **DC claim 6:** they *implement* the deployment corollary — per-model per-layer AUC sweep instead of a depth heuristic — with placements (Qwen late-ish, small-Llama mid) directionally echoing our family clusters; scoped as descriptive echo (residual stream ≠ attention; episode failure ≠ hallucination). (4) **B1 kinship:** per-gate guarantees don't compose; they optimize budgets jointly against the episode-level endpoint — the constructive version of the BENCH gate-cascade lesson. Flag: their related work cites a "representational commitment" monitor (Mehta 2026) as orthogonal-to-correctness — check if it surfaces.
